  FILE: ai_writer.py

    1  """
    2  ai_writer.py – Claude-powered tweet generation.
    3  
    4  Provides three public functions:
    5    generate_news_tweet(story)         – factual news tweet for a single story
    6    generate_morning_recap(headlines)  – daily 08:00 UK market-briefing tweet
    7    generate_quote_tweet(original)     – analyst-voice quote-tweet with context
    8  
    9  Voice: professional crypto news analyst (Bloomberg terminal, not Discord alpha).
   10  Report facts and data. No buy/sell calls. No dismissive language. Present both
   11  sides when relevant. Highlight what's notable without making directional calls.
   12  
   13  Engagement tweets and contrarian takes are separate scheduled categories and
   14  intentionally use a different voice — they are NOT generated here.
   15  
   16  Requires ANTHROPIC_API_KEY in .env.
   17  Falls back to a plain-text summary if the API call fails.
   18  """
   19  from __future__ import annotations
   20  
   21  import json
   22  import logging
   23  import os
   24  import random
   25  import re
   26  import time
   27  import urllib.error
   28  import urllib.request
   29  import anthropic

   31  import config
   32  
   33  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

   35  _client: anthropic.Anthropic | None = None
   36  
   37  # Map CryptoPanic currency codes → canonical hashtags
   38  _HASHTAG_MAP = {
   39      "BTC": "#Bitcoin",
   40      "ETH": "#Ethereum",
   41  }
   42  _FALLBACK_HASHTAG = "#Crypto"
   43  
   44  MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001"

   51  # ── Recent tweet history (persisted across restarts) ─────────────────────────
   52  _RECENT_TWEETS_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".recent_tweets.json")
   53  _recent_tweets: list[str] = []
   54  
   55  
   56  def _load_recent_tweets() -> None:
   57      global _recent_tweets
   58      try:
   59          with open(_RECENT_TWEETS_FILE) as f:
   60              data = json.load(f)
   61          _recent_tweets = data if isinstance(data, list) else []
   62      except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   63          _recent_tweets = []
   64  
   65  
   66  _load_recent_tweets()

   68  
   69  def record_recent_tweet(text: str) -> None:
   70      """Add tweet to recent history and persist to disk (max 10 entries)."""
   71      global _recent_tweets
   72      _recent_tweets.append(text)
   73      if len(_recent_tweets) > 10:
   74          _recent_tweets = _recent_tweets[-10:]
   75      try:
   76          with open(_RECENT_TWEETS_FILE, "w") as f:
   77              json.dump(_recent_tweets, f)
   78      except OSError as exc:
   79          logger.warning("Could not save recent tweets: %s", exc)

   82  # ── BTC data cache (120-second TTL to avoid CoinGecko rate limits) ────────────
   83  _btc_cache: dict = {"data": None, "ts": 0.0}
   84  
   85  
   86  def _fetch_btc_data() -> dict:
   87      """
   88      Fetch BTC price/market data from CoinGecko.
   89      Returns cached data if less than 120 seconds old to avoid rate-limit 429s.
   90      Returns an empty dict on failure (callers must handle missing keys).
   91      """
   92      global _btc_cache
   93      now = time.time()
   94      if _btc_cache["data"] is not None and (now - _btc_cache["ts"]) < 120:
   95          return _btc_cache["data"]
   96  
   97      url = (
   98          "https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/simple/price"
   99          "?ids=bitcoin&vs_currencies=usd"
  100          "&include_market_cap=true&include_24hr_vol=true&include_24hr_change=true"
  101      )
  102      try:
  103          with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=10) as resp:
  104              raw = json.loads(resp.read())
  105          data = raw.get("bitcoin", {})
  106          _btc_cache["data"] = data
  107          _btc_cache["ts"] = now
  108          return data
  109      except Exception as exc:
  110          logger.warning("CoinGecko BTC fetch failed: %s", exc)
  111          if _btc_cache["data"]:
  112              age_mins = (now - _btc_cache["ts"]) / 60
  113              logger.warning("Using stale BTC cache (%.0f min old)", age_mins)
  114              return _btc_cache["data"]
  115          return {}
  116  
  117  # ── Tweet length guard ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  118  _TWEET_LIMIT = 275
  119  MAX_TWEET_LENGTH = 240  # stricter limit for price, news, narrative tweets
  120  
  121  
  122  def _truncate_tweet(text: str, limit: int = _TWEET_LIMIT) -> str:
  123      """Hard-truncate to `limit` chars, preferring sentence then word boundaries."""
  124      if len(text) <= limit:
  125          return text
  126      snippet = text[:limit]
  127      # Try last sentence boundary at least halfway through
  128      for sep in (". ", "! ", "? ", ".\n", "!\n", "?\n"):
  129          pos = snippet.rfind(sep)
  130          if pos > limit // 2:
  131              return snippet[: pos + 1].rstrip()
  132      # Fall back to word boundary
  133      pos = snippet.rfind(" ")
  134      if pos > 0:
  135          return snippet[:pos] + "…"
  136      return snippet[: limit - 1] + "…"
  137  
  138  
  139  # ── AI refusal leak filter ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  140  _AI_REFUSAL_PHRASES = [
  141      "i need to",
  142      "i appreciate",
  143      "i cannot",
  144      "i can't",
  145      "i'm unable",
  146      "i am unable",
  147      "i can't help",
  148      "i can't assist",
  149      "as an ai",
  150      "my core directive",
  151      "conflicts with",
  152      "i must decline",
  153      "i can't generate",
  154      "i can't create",
  155      "i'm not able",
  156      "against my guidelines",
  157  ]
  158  
  159  # Phrases that are especially dangerous at the start of a tweet
  160  _AI_REFUSAL_START = [
  161      "i can't", "i cannot", "i'm unable", "i am unable",
  162      "i can't help", "i can't assist",
  163  ]
  164  
  165  
  166  def _contains_ai_refusal(text: str) -> bool:
  167      """Return True if text contains leaked AI refusal language."""
  168      lower = text.lower()
  169      # Check start-of-string first (catches "I can't do that" openers)
  170      for phrase in _AI_REFUSAL_START:
  171          if lower.startswith(phrase):
  172              return True
  173      return any(phrase in lower for phrase in _AI_REFUSAL_PHRASES)
  174  
  175  
  176  def _call_claude_safe(system: str, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = 120) -> str | None:
  177      """Call Claude and discard AI refusal responses. Retries once."""
  178      result = _call_claude(system, prompt, max_tokens)
  179      if result and _contains_ai_refusal(result):
  180          logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal detected — discarding and retrying: %.80s", result)
  181          result = _call_claude(system, prompt, max_tokens)
  182          if result and _contains_ai_refusal(result):
  183              logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal on retry — discarding entirely: %.80s", result)
  184              return None
  185      return result

  188  # Shared system prompt for all news/briefing/quote-tweet generation.
  189  _ANALYST_SYSTEM = (
  190      "You are CoinWatchAlert, an elite crypto market intelligence system. "
  191      "Your job is NOT to report news. Your job is to interpret market behaviour "
  192      "like a professional trader. Every tweet must feel like it gives the reader an edge.\n\n"
  193  
  194      "TWEET STRUCTURE (MANDATORY — exactly 3 lines, blank line between each):\n"
  195      "Line 1 — HOOK: Sharp, controversial, tension-based. Max 10-12 words. "
  196      "NEVER start with a coin name (ETH/BTC/SOL) or price. "
  197      "NEVER use 'X is Y' structure. Start with action, implication, or tension.\n"
  198      "Line 2 — WHAT'S HAPPENING: One factual sentence. What is actually going on beneath the surface.\n"
  199      "Line 3 — WHAT IT MEANS / WHAT HAPPENS NEXT: One opinionated sentence. Take a stance. "
  200      "Include a scenario (if X → then Y) or what smart money does here.\n"
  201      "EVERY TWEET MUST INCLUDE: what is happening, what it means, what likely happens next.\n"
  202      "OPTIONAL: Line 3 can end with a short punchy question if it adds tension.\n\n"
  203  
  204      "STYLE:\n"
  205      "- Tone: calm, sharp, confident, experienced trader\n"
  206      "- Short sentences. Max 15 words per sentence.\n"
  207      "- Slightly contrarian when appropriate\n"
  208      "- Each line is ONE sentence — never split across two lines\n\n"
  209  
  210      "FORBIDDEN:\n"
  211      "- Starting with coin names: 'ETH...', 'BTC...', 'SOL...'\n"
  212      "- Neutral reporting: 'is happening', 'is increasing', 'shows growth'\n"
  213      "- Hype words: 'bullish', 'bearish', 'looking strong', 'gaining momentum'\n"
  214      "- Weak phrasing: 'this signals', 'this suggests', 'worth watching', "
  215      "'remains to be seen', 'interesting to see'\n"
  216      "- Hedging: 'could', 'might', 'may', 'potentially', 'possibly', 'likely'\n"
  217      "- Raw price-only statements: never post 'BTC $66K (-2.3%)' without explaining what it means\n"
  218      "- News-style openings: '[COIN] BREAKS...', '[NAME] SAYS...'\n"
  219      "- Headline repetition. Generic observations. Fluff.\n"
  220      "- No hashtags. No URLs. No NFA. No 'via'.\n"
  221      "- Max 1 emoji, at the very start only. Allowed: ⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀\n\n"
  222  
  223      "STRONG HOOKS (examples):\n"
  224      "- 'This level decides what happens next'\n"
  225      "- 'The reaction here matters more than the move'\n"
  226      "- 'Smart money is already positioned for this'\n"
  227      "- 'Something is building here and most traders don't see it'\n"
  228      "- 'This doesn't happen randomly'\n\n"
  229  
  230      "QUALITY CHECK:\n"
  231      "1. If line 1 starts with a coin name (ETH/BTC/SOL/ADA/etc) — REWRITE IT.\n"
  232      "2. If line 1 uses a weak 'X is Y' structure over 8 words — REWRITE IT.\n"
  233      "3. If the post lacks a clear takeaway — REWRITE IT.\n"
  234      "4. If it reads like a news headline — REFRAME as interpretation.\n"
  235      "5. Every sentence must answer: WHY does this matter? or WHAT happens next?\n\n"
  236  
  237      "ENGAGEMENT LAYER: When instructed, use one of these (never more than one):\n"
  238      "- TENSION: 'This doesn't look right' / 'Something is building here'\n"
  239      "- QUESTION: 'Does this hold or break?' / 'Are we early or late?'\n"
  240      "- CONTRARIAN: 'Most traders are positioned wrong here'\n\n"
  241  
  242      "DATA INTERPRETATION (MANDATORY):\n"
  243      "Data without interpretation is noise. Every number must answer: "
  244      "what does this mean right now? Never post raw price data without interpretation. "
  245      "Replace data-only lines like 'BTC $66K (-2.3%)' with insight-led lines like "
  246      "'Price is holding. Participation isn't. That divergence matters.' "
  247      "Structure: observation → what it means → implication. Never just report.\n\n"
  248  
  249      "GOAL: Make the reader feel 'I understand what's happening better than everyone else now.'"
  250  )
  251  
  252  
  253  _ENGAGEMENT_TACTICS = [
  254      "\nENGAGEMENT MODE: Use TENSION — introduce uncertainty or unease. "
  255      "Something feels off, something is building, this move is being misread.",
  256      "\nENGAGEMENT MODE: Use QUESTION — end line 3 with a short punchy question. "
  257      "'Does this hold or break?' / 'Are we early or late?'",
  258      "\nENGAGEMENT MODE: Use CONTRARIAN — challenge the crowd. "
  259      "'Most traders are positioned wrong here' / 'This is where people get trapped'",
  260  ]
  261  
  262  
  263  _COIN_NAME_RE = re.compile(
  264      r'^[⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀\s]*(BTC|ETH|SOL|BNB|XRP|ADA|DOGE|AVAX|DOT|LINK|'
  265      r'MATIC|UNI|ATOM|LTC|ALGO|NEAR|FTM|APT|ARB|SUI|INJ|TIA|SEI|TAO|'
  266      r'BITCOIN|ETHEREUM|SOLANA|CARDANO)\b',
  267      re.IGNORECASE,
  268  )
  269  _WEAK_OPENER_RE = re.compile(
  270      r'^[⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀\s]*\w+\s+(is|are|has|have|was|were|shows?|remains?)\s',
  271      re.IGNORECASE,
  272  )
  273  
  274  
  275  _FORBIDDEN_PHRASES_RE = re.compile(
  276      r'bullish|bearish|looking strong|gaining momentum|'
  277      r'is happening|is increasing|shows growth|going crazy|'
  278      r'watch what happens|interesting to see|market reacting|'
  279      r'could go either way|wait and see',
  280      re.IGNORECASE,
  281  )
  282  
  283  # Patterns indicating a tweet has forward implication (cause→effect)
  284  _FORWARD_IMPLICATION_RE = re.compile(
  285      r'if .+ then|if .+ →|breaks? .+ heads?|holds? .+ expect|'
  286      r'loses? .+ drops?|reclaims? .+ targets?|'
  287      r'trap|liquidat|squeeze|flush|next|'
  288      r'sets up|leads to|opens the door|positions? for|'
  289      r'this means|what happens|expansion|compression',
  290      re.IGNORECASE,
  291  )
  292  
  293  # Vague consequence patterns — non-committal hedging disguised as outcome
  294  _VAGUE_CONSEQUENCE_RE = re.compile(
  295      r'could move|could lead|may result|might see|'
  296      r'watch closely|keep an eye|stay tuned|remains unclear',
  297      re.IGNORECASE,
  298  )
  299  
  300  
  301  def _needs_regen(text: str) -> bool:
  302      """Return True if the tweet has weak opener, forbidden phrases, or lacks implication."""
  303      first_line = text.split("\n")[0].strip()
  304  
  305      # Check 1: coin name opener
  306      if _COIN_NAME_RE.match(first_line):
  307          logger.debug("Regen trigger: first line starts with coin name: %.60s", first_line)
  308          return True
  309  
  310      # Check 2: weak "X is Y" opener
  311      if _WEAK_OPENER_RE.match(first_line) and len(first_line.split()) > 8:
  312          logger.debug("Regen trigger: weak 'X is Y' opener: %.60s", first_line)
  313          return True
  314  
  315      # Check 3: forbidden phrases
  316      if _FORBIDDEN_PHRASES_RE.search(text):
  317          logger.debug("Regen trigger: forbidden phrase detected in: %.60s", text)
  318          return True
  319  
  320      # Check 4: vague consequences
  321      if _VAGUE_CONSEQUENCE_RE.search(text):
  322          logger.debug("Regen trigger: vague consequence detected: %.60s", text)
  323          return True
  324  
  325      # Check 5: forward implication — tweet must answer "what happens next?"
  326      # Only check multi-line tweets (single-line context sentences are exempt)
  327      if "\n" in text and not _FORWARD_IMPLICATION_RE.search(text):
  328          logger.debug("Regen trigger: no forward implication found: %.60s", text)
  329          return True
  330  
  331      return False
  332  
  333  
  334  def _engagement_directive() -> str:
  335      """Return an engagement instruction ~30% of the time, empty string otherwise."""
  336      if random.random() < 0.30:
  337          return random.choice(_ENGAGEMENT_TACTICS)
  338      return ""

  341  def _get_client() -> anthropic.Anthropic:
  342      global _client
  343      if _client is None:
  344          _client = anthropic.Anthropic(api_key=config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)
  345      return _client
  346  
  347  
  348  def _pick_hashtags(story: dict) -> str:
  349      """Return 1-2 hashtags appropriate for the story's currencies."""
  350      currencies = story.get("currencies") or []
  351      codes = [c["code"] for c in currencies if c.get("code") and c["code"] != "?"]
  352      tags = [_HASHTAG_MAP[code] for code in codes if code in _HASHTAG_MAP]
  353      if not tags:
  354          tags = [_FALLBACK_HASHTAG]
  355      return " ".join(tags[:2])
  356  
  357  
  358  def _compute_price_context(coin_id: str, pct_change: float) -> dict[str, str]:
  359      """Derive trend/volume/structure context from recent price action.
  360  
  361      Returns dict with keys: trend, volume, structure.
  362      Uses heuristics based on the move magnitude — does NOT call external APIs
  363      to keep latency low. These provide structured hints to the prompt.
  364      """
  365      abs_pct = abs(pct_change)
  366  
  367      # Trend context based on move direction and magnitude
  368      if pct_change > 8:
  369          trend = "Strong breakout move — momentum accelerating"
  370      elif pct_change > 4:
  371          trend = "Pushing higher with follow-through"
  372      elif pct_change > 0:
  373          trend = "Grinding up — slow but persistent"
  374      elif pct_change > -4:
  375          trend = "Drifting lower — sellers in control but no panic"
  376      elif pct_change > -8:
  377          trend = "Sharp move down — testing support"
  378      else:
  379          trend = "Flush move — likely liquidation cascade"
  380  
  381      # Volume context (inferred from move magnitude — large moves = high volume)
  382      if abs_pct > 6:
  383          volume = "Volume expanding with the move"
  384      elif abs_pct > 3:
  385          volume = "Moderate volume — conviction building"
  386      else:
  387          volume = "Low volume — move lacks conviction"
  388  
  389      # Structure context
  390      if pct_change > 6:
  391          structure = "Breaking out of recent range"
  392      elif pct_change > 2:
  393          structure = "Testing upper range boundary"
  394      elif pct_change > -2:
  395          structure = "Range-bound — compression before expansion"
  396      elif pct_change > -6:
  397          structure = "Testing lower range support"
  398      else:
  399          structure = "Breaking below support — failed structure"
  400  
  401      return {"trend": trend, "volume": volume, "structure": structure}
  402  
  403  
  404  def generate_price_alert_tweet(alert: dict) -> str | None:
  405      """
  406      Ask Claude to write a single declarative price-alert tweet.
  407  
  408      Rules: no questions, no first person, no hashtags, no line breaks,
  409      max 220 chars, emojis only 🚀📉⚡👀.
  410      Returns None on API failure.
  411      """
  412      symbol = alert["symbol"]
  413      pct    = alert["pct_change"]
  414      price  = alert["price_usd"]
  415      window = alert["window"]
  416      sign   = "+" if pct > 0 else ""
  417  
  418      from price_monitor import _format_price
  419      price_str = _format_price(price)
  420  
  421      # Compute structured context for richer prompts
  422      ctx = _compute_price_context(alert.get("coin_id", "bitcoin"), pct)
  423  
  424      prompt = (
  425          f"{symbol} moved {sign}{pct:.1f}% in {window}. Price: {price_str}.\n"
  426          f"Market context: {ctx['trend']}. {ctx['volume']}. {ctx['structure']}.\n\n"
  427          f"Write a 3-line price alert. Blank line between each.\n\n"
  428          f"Line 1: HOOK — tension or implication, not just 'COIN MOVES X%'. Max 1 emoji at start.\n"
  429          f"Line 2: What's happening — use the market context to explain the structure.\n"
  430          f"Line 3: What it means — ONE sentence, directional stance with a conditional (if X → then Y).\n\n"
  431          f"Rules:\n"
  432          f"- Data without interpretation is noise. Don't just report the move — explain what it means NOW.\n"
  433          f"- Observation → what it means → implication. Never just report.\n"
  434          f"- Trader voice. Short sentences. Never start with coin name.\n"
  435          f"- No questions. No hashtags. No URLs. No hedging.\n"
  436          f"- Max {MAX_TWEET_LENGTH} chars.\n\n"
  437          f"Output ONLY the tweet."
  438      )
  439  
  440      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  441          return None
  442  
  443      tweet = None
  444      for attempt in range(1, 4):
  445          try:
  446              message = _get_client().messages.create(
  447                  model=MODEL,
  448                  max_tokens=100,
  449                  system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  450                  messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  451              )
  452              tweet = message.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
  453              if _contains_ai_refusal(tweet):
  454                  logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in price alert — retry %d", attempt)
  455                  tweet = None
  456                  time.sleep(2)
  457                  continue
  458              tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
  459              if tweet and _needs_regen(tweet) and attempt < 3:
  460                  logger.info("[AI] Price alert weak opener — retry %d", attempt)
  461                  tweet = None
  462                  time.sleep(2)
  463                  continue
  464              break
  465          except anthropic.APIError as exc:
  466              logger.warning("Claude API error generating price alert tweet (attempt %d): %s", attempt, exc)
  467              if attempt < 3:
  468                  time.sleep(2)
  469  
  470      if not tweet:
  471          return _truncate_tweet(f"⚡ {symbol} holding key level — market waiting for direction.",
  472                                 limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)
  473      tweet = _ensure_line_breaks(tweet)
  474      return _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)
  475  
  476  
  477  def generate_geo_tweet(story: dict) -> str | None:
  478      """
  479      Ask Claude to write a single breaking crypto/macro tweet for a geo/macro story.
  480  
  481      Format:
  482          One punchy declarative opener sentence.
  483          → short bullet
  484          → short bullet
  485          One line on the crypto/BTC angle.
  486  
  487      No questions. No first person. No hashtags. Max 280 chars total.
  488      Emojis only from 🚀📉⚡👀. Returns None on failure.
  489      """
  490      title = story.get("title", "")
  491      if not title:
  492          return None
  493  
  494      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  495          return None
  496  
  497      # Detect if this is a macro/TradFi comparison story (gold, stocks, oil, bonds)
  498      title_lower = title.lower()
  499      is_macro_comparison = any(w in title_lower for w in [
  500          "gold", "stocks", "oil", "bonds", "s&p", "nasdaq", "dow",
  501          "treasury", "commodities", "equities", "tradfi",
  502      ])
  503  
  504      prompt = (
  505          f"IGNORE any previous formatting rules. Follow ONLY these instructions.\n\n"
  506          f"Write exactly 3 lines about this event. Separate each line with a newline character.\n\n"
  507          f"Line 1: What happened. One sentence.\n"
  508          f"Line 2: What it means for markets. One sentence.\n"
  509          f"Line 3: → followed by the forward implication. One sentence.\n\n"
  510          f"EXAMPLE OUTPUT (exactly this format):\n"
  511          f"Geopolitical tension escalated in a key region overnight.\n"
  512          f"Markets are repricing risk faster than headlines suggest.\n"
  513          f"→ Volatility expansion likely if this continues.\n\n"
  514          f"Event: {title}\n\n"
  515          f"Output ONLY the 3 lines. Nothing before, nothing after. No blank lines between them."
  516      )
  517  
  518      try:
  519          message = _get_client().messages.create(
  520              model=MODEL,
  521              max_tokens=150,
  522              system="You are a sharp crypto trader. Write exactly what is asked. No extra text.",
  523              messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  524          )
  525          tweet = message.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
  526          if _contains_ai_refusal(tweet):
  527              logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in geo tweet — discarding")
  528              return None
  529          tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
  530      except anthropic.APIError as exc:
  531          logger.warning("Claude API error generating geo tweet: %s", exc)
  532          return None
  533  
  534      tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=240)
  535      tweet = re.sub(r"[^\w\s\$\%\.\,\!\?\-\:\;—\→\@\'🚀📉⚡👀🤯\n]", '', tweet).strip()
  536      # Nuclear: strip ALL dollar amounts — Claude fabricates prices despite prompt bans
  537      if not is_macro_comparison:
  538          tweet = re.sub(r'\$[\d,\.]+[KkMmBb]?', '', tweet)
  539      tweet = re.sub(r'\s{2,}', ' ', tweet).strip()
  540      return tweet
  541  
  542  
  543  # Regex to detect a direct quote: "..." with a known powerful person nearby
  544  _QUOTE_PERSON_RE = re.compile(
  545      r'(Fink|Saylor|CZ|Vitalik|Musk|Dimon|Powell|Trump|Gensler|Atkins|Bukele|'
  546      r'Wood|Lutnick|Ramaswamy|Bezos|Zuckerberg|Cathie\s+Wood|Larry\s+Fink|'
  547      r'Michael\s+Saylor|Elon\s+Musk|Jamie\s+Dimon)',
  548      re.IGNORECASE,
  549  )
  550  _DIRECT_QUOTE_RE = re.compile(r'["\u201c](.+?)["\u201d]')
  551  
  552  
  553  def story_has_power_quote(story: dict) -> bool:
  554      """Return True if a story contains a direct quote from a named powerful person."""
  555      title = story.get("title", "")
  556      commentary = story.get("commentary", "") or ""
  557      text = f"{title} {commentary}"
  558      return bool(_DIRECT_QUOTE_RE.search(text) and _QUOTE_PERSON_RE.search(text))
  559  
  560  
  561  def generate_quote_style_tweet(story: dict) -> str | None:
  562      """
  563      Generate a quote-style tweet when a news story contains a direct quote
  564      from a named powerful person.
  565  
  566      Format:
  567          🚨 [PERSON] JUST SAID:
  568          "[exact short quote]"
  569          [1-2 sentence analyst take on what it means for crypto]
  570  
  571      No price figures. Max 220 chars. Returns None on failure.
  572      """
  573      title = story.get("title", "")
  574      commentary = story.get("commentary", "") or ""
  575      text = f"{title} {commentary}"
  576  
  577      # Extract person and quote
  578      person_match = _QUOTE_PERSON_RE.search(text)
  579      quote_match = _DIRECT_QUOTE_RE.search(text)
  580      if not person_match or not quote_match:
  581          return None
  582  
  583      person = person_match.group(0).upper()
  584      raw_quote = quote_match.group(1)
  585  
  586      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  587          return None
  588  
  589      prompt = (
  590          f"Write a tweet in this EXACT format:\n\n"
  591          f'🚨 {person} JUST SAID:\n\n'
  592          f'\"[short version of this quote: {raw_quote}]\"\n\n'
  593          f"[What this means for crypto. One sentence. Direct.]\n\n"
  594          f"Rules:\n"
  595          f"- Quote max 80 chars. Capture the key phrase only.\n"
  596          f"- The take must connect to crypto impact. No hedging.\n"
  597          f"- No price figures. No hashtags. No URLs. No questions.\n"
  598          f"- Max 220 chars. Trader voice.\n\n"
  599          f"Story: {title}"
  600      )
  601  
  602      try:
  603          message = _get_client().messages.create(
  604              model=MODEL,
  605              max_tokens=120,
  606              system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  607              messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  608          )
  609          tweet = message.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
  610          if _contains_ai_refusal(tweet):
  611              logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in quote-style tweet — discarding")
  612              return None
  613          tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
  614      except anthropic.APIError as exc:
  615          logger.warning("Claude API error generating quote-style tweet: %s", exc)
  616          return None
  617  
  618      tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=220)
  619      # Strip fabricated dollar amounts
  620      tweet = re.sub(r'\$[\d,\.]+[KkMmBb]?', '', tweet)
  621      tweet = re.sub(r'\s{2,}', ' ', tweet).strip()
  622      return tweet
  623  
  624  
  625  def generate_news_tweet(story: dict, *, high_conviction: bool = False) -> str | None:
  626      """
  627      Ask Claude to write a news tweet for a single crypto story.
  628  
  629      If high_conviction is True (score >= 8), the prompt uses stronger
  630      directional language with explicit bullish/bearish bias and clear
  631      opportunity/risk framing.
  632      Falls back to a plain formatted string if the API call fails.
  633      """
  634      title = story.get("title", "")
  635      hashtags = _pick_hashtags(story)
  636  
  637      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  638          logger.debug("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – using plain news tweet format")
  639          return _plain_news_tweet(title, hashtags)
  640  
  641      # Fetch live BTC price for context
  642      btc_data = _fetch_btc_data()
  643      btc_price = btc_data.get("usd")
  644      pct_24h = btc_data.get("usd_24h_change")
  645      if btc_price and btc_price > 0:
  646          sign = "+" if pct_24h and pct_24h > 0 else ""
  647          pct_str = f" ({sign}{pct_24h:.1f}% 24h)" if pct_24h is not None else ""
  648          price_context = f"BTC is currently at ${btc_price:,.0f}{pct_str}."
  649      else:
  650          price_context = ""
  651  
  652      conviction_block = ""
  653      if high_conviction:
  654          conviction_block = (
  655              "\nHIGH CONVICTION MODE — this story scored 8+:\n"
  656              "- Take a clear directional stance (bullish or bearish). No neutrality.\n"
  657              "- Use stronger wording: 'likely', 'expect', 'this sets up for'. Reduce uncertainty.\n"
  658              "- Highlight risk OR opportunity — at least one must be present.\n"
  659              "- Mention what traders should watch or do next. Be specific about levels or catalysts.\n"
  660              "- Make the reader feel they NEED to pay attention right now.\n"
  661          )
  662  
  663      use_bullet = high_conviction
  664  
  665      if use_bullet:
  666          prompt = (
  667              f"Write a breaking crypto news tweet in ALL CAPS bullet style.\n\n"
  668              f"EXACT format (use → for bullets, blank line before bullets):\n"
  669              f"⚡ [HEADLINE IN ALL CAPS]\n\n"
  670              f"→ [KEY FACT — one short line in caps]\n"
  671              f"→ [IMPLICATION — one short line in caps]\n"
  672              f"→ [CRYPTO IMPACT — one short line in caps]\n\n"
  673              f"GOOD example:\n"
  674              f"⚡ SEC APPROVES SPOT ETH ETF\n\n"
  675              f"→ BLACKROCK AND FIDELITY FILINGS GREENLIT\n"
  676              f"→ ETH UP 8% IN MINUTES AFTER ANNOUNCEMENT\n"
  677              f"→ INSTITUTIONAL FLOODGATES NOW OPEN FOR ETH\n\n"
  678              f"Rules:\n"
  679              f"- ALL text in caps. Bullets use → prefix\n"
  680              f"- Never invent price levels. Only use numbers from the headline or: {price_context}\n"
  681              f"- Never include URLs, links, or source attributions\n"
  682              f"- Never start with 'BITCOIN' — vary the opening\n"
  683              f"- Where genuinely applicable, include a brief historical comparison e.g. 'LAST TIME WE SAW THIS WAS...' or 'SIMILAR TO THE 2021 DEFI RUN' — never forced\n"
  684              f"- Do not repeat the headline in different words. Extract the insight BEHIND the headline\n"
  685              f"- No hashtags. Max 240 chars total.\n"
  686              f"CLOSING LINE (mandatory, mixed case, NOT caps): One sharp analytical "
  687              f"sentence. Must include a forward-looking implication or directional bias. "
  688              f"No summaries. No hedging. Max 14 words.\n"
  689              f"{conviction_block}"
  690              f"{_engagement_directive()}\n"
  691              f"Headline: {title}\n\n"
  692              f"Output ONLY the tweet text, nothing else."
  693          )
  694      else:
  695          prompt = (
  696              f"Write a breaking crypto news tweet. Exactly 3 lines, blank line between each.\n\n"
  697              f"Line 1: THE HEADLINE — caps or near-caps, punchy, no fluff. Max 1 emoji at the very start.\n"
  698              f"Line 2: ONE concrete fact or number that matters. Not a restatement.\n"
  699              f"CLOSING LINE (mandatory, mixed case, NOT caps): One sharp analytical "
  700              f"sentence. Must include a forward-looking implication or directional bias. "
  701              f"No summaries. No hedging. Max 14 words.\n\n"
  702              f"GOOD example (each line is ONE sentence):\n"
  703              f"\"⚡ BTC REJECTED AT $70.6K\n\n"
  704              f"Bears have controlled every bounce for 5 days straight.\n\n"
  705              f"$68K breaks and this thing heads straight to $65K.\"\n\n"
  706              f"Rules:\n"
  707              f"- Never invent price levels. Only use numbers from the headline or: {price_context}\n"
  708              f"- Never include URLs, links, or source attributions\n"
  709              f"- Never use 'this signals', 'this suggests', 'this indicates'\n"
  710              f"- Never start with 'Bitcoin' — vary the opening\n"
  711              f"- Where genuinely applicable, include a brief historical comparison e.g. 'Last time we saw this was...' or 'Similar to the 2021 DeFi run' — never forced\n"
  712              f"- Do not repeat the headline in different words. Extract the insight BEHIND the headline\n"
  713              f"- No hashtags. Max 220 chars total.\n"
  714              f"{conviction_block}"
  715              f"{_engagement_directive()}\n"
  716              f"Headline: {title}\n\n"
  717              f"Output ONLY the tweet text, nothing else."
  718          )
  719  
  720      last_exc: anthropic.APIError | None = None
  721      for attempt in range(1, 4):
  722          try:
  723              message = _get_client().messages.create(
  724                  model=MODEL,
  725                  max_tokens=150,
  726                  system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  727                  messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  728              )
  729              tweet = message.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
  730              if _contains_ai_refusal(tweet):
  731                  logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in news tweet — retry %d", attempt)
  732                  time.sleep(2)
  733                  continue
  734              if "SKIP" in tweet:
  735                  logger.info("Claude returned SKIP for news tweet — skipping story")
  736                  return None
  737              tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
  738              tweet = re.sub(r'https?://\S+', '', tweet).strip()
  739              tweet = _ensure_line_breaks(tweet)
  740              tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)
  741              # Regen check: reject weak/banned openers on first attempt
  742              if _needs_regen(tweet) and attempt < 3:
  743                  logger.info("[AI] Weak opener detected — regenerating (attempt %d)", attempt)
  744                  time.sleep(2)
  745                  continue
  746              logger.info("[AI] High-conviction tweet generated")
  747              return tweet
  748          except anthropic.APIError as exc:
  749              last_exc = exc
  750              logger.warning("Claude API error (attempt %d/3) generating news tweet: %s", attempt, exc)
  751              if attempt < 3:
  752                  time.sleep(5)
  753  
  754      logger.warning("All 3 attempts failed – falling back to plain tweet")
  755      return _plain_news_tweet(title, hashtags)
  756  
  757  
  758  def generate_morning_recap(headlines: list[str]) -> str:
  759      """
  760      Morning recap tweet — insight-driven, not a data dump.
  761      3-part structure: what happened → what it means → what to watch.
  762      Falls back to interpretation-style fallback if Claude fails.
  763      """
  764      _FALLBACK = "⚡ Market is compressing after overnight moves — expansion likely follows."
  765  
  766      if not headlines:
  767          return _FALLBACK
  768  
  769      numbered = "\n".join(f"{i+1}. {h}" for i, h in enumerate(headlines[:3]))
  770  
  771      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  772          logger.debug("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – using fallback")
  773          return _FALLBACK
  774  
  775      prompt = (
  776          "Write a morning crypto recap as a single insight-driven tweet. "
  777          "3 lines, blank line between each.\n\n"
  778          "Line 1 — WHAT HAPPENED: The dominant overnight move or theme only. "
  779          "Not a data dump — one sharp observation. Don't list coins.\n"
  780          "Line 2 — WHAT IT MEANS: Interpretation — strength/weakness, "
  781          "positioning shift, or conviction read.\n"
  782          "Line 3 — WHAT TO WATCH: Forward implication — a level, scenario, "
  783          "or condition that decides what happens next.\n\n"
  784          "Rules:\n"
  785          "- Do NOT output raw price bullets like 'BTC $66K (+0.4%)'. "
  786          "All data must be paired with meaning.\n"
  787          "- The tweet MUST answer 'What matters next?'\n"
  788          "- Use ONLY info from the headlines below — never invent prices\n"
  789          "- Max 240 chars total. No hashtags. No URLs. No hedging.\n"
  790          "- Max 1 emoji at start. Allowed: ⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀\n\n"
  791          f"Headlines:\n{numbered}\n\n"
  792          "Output ONLY the tweet, nothing else."
  793      )
  794  
  795      for attempt in range(1, 4):
  796          try:
  797              message = _get_client().messages.create(
  798                  model=MODEL,
  799                  max_tokens=120,
  800                  system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  801                  messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  802              )
  803              tweet = message.content[0].text.strip()
  804              if _contains_ai_refusal(tweet):
  805                  logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in morning recap — retry %d", attempt)
  806                  time.sleep(2)
  807                  continue
  808              tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
  809              tweet = _ensure_line_breaks(tweet)
  810              tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)
  811              if _needs_regen(tweet) and attempt < 3:
  812                  logger.info("[AI] Morning recap weak — retry %d", attempt)
  813                  time.sleep(2)
  814                  continue
  815              logger.info("[AI] Morning recap generated: %.80s", tweet)
  816              return tweet
  817          except anthropic.APIError as exc:
  818              logger.warning("Claude API error for morning recap (attempt %d): %s", attempt, exc)
  819              if attempt < 3:
  820                  time.sleep(2)
  821  
  822      logger.warning("All 3 morning recap attempts failed — using fallback")
  823      return _FALLBACK
  824  
  825  
  826  def generate_thread(topic: str, n_tweets: int = 3) -> list[str]:
  827      """
  828      Ask Claude to write a 3-tweet Twitter thread on `topic`.
  829  
  830      Each tweet stands alone but flows into the next. No numbering prefixes.
  831      Tweet 1: bold thesis + data point. Tweet 2: evidence/numbers.
  832      Tweet 3: directional conclusion with timeframe.
  833  
  834      Returns a list of tweet strings (each ≤200 chars).
  835      Falls back to an empty list on failure.
  836      """
  837      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  838          logger.warning("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – cannot generate thread")
  839          return []
  840  
  841      n = max(3, n_tweets)  # minimum 3
  842  
  843      prompt = (
  844          f"Write a {n}-tweet thread about: {topic}\n\n"
  845          "Output exactly 3 lines, one tweet per line. No numbering.\n\n"
  846          "Tweet 1: Bold opening claim. ALL CAPS or near-caps first phrase. "
  847          "One concrete number. Make people stop scrolling.\n\n"
  848          "Tweet 2: The evidence. One specific data point, metric, or on-chain signal. "
  849          "Short sentences. Trader-to-trader voice.\n\n"
  850          "Tweet 3: The punchline. Directional call with a timeframe. "
  851          "Full conviction. End with something screenshot-worthy.\n\n"
  852          "Rules:\n"
  853          "- No numbering (no '1/', '2/', etc.)\n"
  854          "- No questions. No hedging. No 'signals', 'suggests', 'indicates'\n"
  855          "- No hashtags. No URLs\n"
  856          "- Each tweet under 220 chars\n"
  857          "- Max 1 emoji per tweet, at the start only. Allowed: ⚡🚨📉👀\n"
  858          "- Never start with 'Bitcoin'\n"
  859          "- Write like a trader, not a journalist\n"
  860          "- Output ONLY the 3 tweet lines, nothing else"
  861      )
  862  
  863      try:
  864          message = _get_client().messages.create(
  865              model=MODEL,
  866              max_tokens=400,
  867              system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  868              messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  869          )
  870          raw = message.content[0].text.strip()
  871          if _contains_ai_refusal(raw):
  872              logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in thread — discarding")
  873              return []
  874          raw = _strip_unwanted_lines(raw)
  875          tweets = [line.strip() for line in raw.splitlines() if line.strip()]
  876          tweets = [_truncate_tweet(t, limit=220) if len(t) > 220 else t for t in tweets]
  877          tweets = [re.sub(r"[^\w\s\$\%\.\,\!\?\-\:\;—\@\'🚀📉⚡👀⚠️\n]", '', t).strip() for t in tweets]
  878          logger.info("Generated thread with %d tweets on: %s", len(tweets), topic)
  879          return tweets
  880      except anthropic.APIError as exc:
  881          logger.warning("Claude API error generating thread: %s", exc)
  882          return []
  883  
  884  
  885  def generate_hot_take(context: str = "") -> str | None:
  886      """
  887      Generate a punchy opinion/hot-take tweet.
  888  
  889      Fetches real BTC price data first. Returns None (skipping the tweet) if
  890      price data is unavailable — never generates content with fabricated prices.
  891  
  892      Layout:
  893          [Punchy fact or observation. Short. Emphasis word.]
  894  
  895          [Context or what it means. One sentence.]
  896  
  897          [The call. What happens next. Direct.]
  898      """
  899      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  900          logger.warning("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – cannot generate hot take")
  901          return None
  902  
  903      # Require real price data — never let Claude fabricate price levels.
  904      btc_data = _fetch_btc_data()
  905      price = btc_data.get("usd")
  906      if not price or price <= 0:
  907          logger.warning("generate_hot_take: no real BTC price available — skipping to avoid fabricated prices")
  908          return None
  909  
  910      pct_24h = btc_data.get("usd_24h_change")
  911      sign = "+" if pct_24h and pct_24h > 0 else ""
  912      pct_str = f" ({sign}{pct_24h:.1f}% 24h)" if pct_24h is not None else ""
  913      price_line = f"BTC: ${price:,.0f}{pct_str}"
  914  
  915      context_block = f"\nLive data:\n{price_line}"
  916      if context:
  917          context_block += f"\n{context}"
  918  
  919      prompt = (
  920          f"You are writing a crypto market tweet.\n\n"
  921          f"STRICT FORMAT — NO EXCEPTIONS:\n"
  922          f"Line 1: One sentence stating the market situation\n"
  923          f"Line 2: One sentence explaining what it means\n"
  924          f"Line 3: One sentence starting with → giving the forward implication or price level\n\n"
  925          f"YOU MUST:\n"
  926          f"- Output EXACTLY 3 lines\n"
  927          f"- Each line must be a full sentence\n"
  928          f"- Each line must be on a new line\n"
  929          f"- Line 3 MUST start with →\n\n"
  930          f"IF YOU OUTPUT 1 OR 2 LINES, THE RESPONSE IS INVALID.\n\n"
  931          f"STYLE:\n"
  932          f"- Sharp trader tone\n"
  933          f"- Confident, declarative\n"
  934          f"- No analyst language\n\n"
  935          f"BANNED PHRASES:\n"
  936          f"- weak participation\n"
  937          f"- conviction is missing\n"
  938          f"- risk-off pressure\n"
  939          f"- bearish sentiment\n"
  940          f"- market uncertainty\n"
  941          f"- mixed signals\n\n"
  942          f"EXAMPLE:\n"
  943          f"Resistance rejected three times and sellers aren't letting up.\n"
  944          f"Structure is weakening on every bounce attempt.\n"
  945          f"→ Lose $67,500 and this flushes to $65K.\n\n"
  946          f"Market context:{context_block}\n\n"
  947          f"Now write the tweet."
  948      )
  949  
  950      try:
  951          message = _get_client().messages.create(
  952              model=MODEL,
  953              max_tokens=150,
  954              system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  955              messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  956          )
  957          text = message.content[0].text.strip()
  958          if _contains_ai_refusal(text):
  959              logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in hot take — discarding")
  960              return None
  961          if text.startswith('"') and text.endswith('"'):
  962              text = text[1:-1]
  963          if text.startswith("'") and text.endswith("'"):
  964              text = text[1:-1]
  965          text = _strip_unwanted_lines(text)
  966          text = _strip_hashtags(text)
  967          text = _ensure_line_breaks(text)
  968          text = _truncate_tweet(text, limit=220)
  969          if _needs_regen(text):
  970              logger.info("[AI] Hot take weak opener — regenerating once")
  971              try:
  972                  message2 = _get_client().messages.create(
  973                      model=MODEL, max_tokens=150,
  974                      system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
  975                      messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  976                  )
  977                  text2 = message2.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
  978                  text2 = _strip_unwanted_lines(text2)
  979                  text2 = _strip_hashtags(text2)
  980                  text2 = _ensure_line_breaks(text2)
  981                  text2 = _truncate_tweet(text2, limit=220)
  982                  if not _needs_regen(text2):
  983                      text = text2
  984              except Exception:
  985                  pass  # keep original if regen fails
  986          logger.info("[AI] High-conviction tweet generated")
  987          return text
  988      except Exception as exc:
  989          logger.warning("Claude API call failed: %s", exc)
  990          return None
  991  
  992  
  993  
  994  
  995  def _ensure_line_breaks(text: str) -> str:
  996      """If the tweet is a wall of text with no blank lines, insert them — max 3 blocks.
  997  
  998      Only splits after complete sentences ending with . ! or ? followed by a space
  999      and an uppercase letter or emoji. Never splits mid-sentence.
 1000      """
 1001      # Already formatted — just normalise excessive breaks
 1002      if "\n\n" in text:
 1003          return re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', text)
 1004      # Skip arrow/bullet-style tweets
 1005      if re.search(r'[\n].*[→●]', text):
 1006          return text
 1007      # Only split after sentence-ending punctuation (.!?) followed by space + uppercase/emoji
 1008      # Negative lookbehind prevents splitting after abbreviations like "$1.5B" or "U.S."
 1009      parts = re.split(r'(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀])', text)
 1010      if len(parts) < 3:
 1011          return text
 1012      # Merge into exactly 3 blocks: first sentence, second sentence, everything else joined
 1013      blocks = [parts[0], parts[1], " ".join(parts[2:])]
 1014      result = "\n\n".join(blocks)
 1015      if len(result) <= 280:
 1016          return result
 1017      return text
 1018  
 1019  
 1020  def _strip_nfa(text: str) -> str:
 1021      """Remove all NFA disclaimers (inline, trailing, standalone) from text."""
 1022      # Remove any line containing "NFA" as a word (handles emojis preceding it)
 1023      lines = text.splitlines()
 1024      lines = [l for l in lines if not re.search(r'\bNFA\b', l)]
 1025      text = "\n".join(lines)
 1026      # Clean up any double blank lines left behind
 1027      text = re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', text)
 1028      return text.strip()
 1029  
 1030  
 1031  def _strip_unwanted_lines(text: str) -> str:
 1032      """Remove junk lines (SKIP, ---, Reasoning, Why, ** headers), then strip NFA.
 1033      Nuclear: if 'SKIP' appears ANYWHERE in the final text, return empty string."""
 1034      lines = text.splitlines()
 1035      cleaned = []
 1036      for line in lines:
 1037          stripped = line.lstrip()
 1038          # Drop lines starting with SKIP, ---, Reasoning:, or **
 1039          if stripped.startswith("SKIP") or stripped.startswith("---") or stripped.startswith("Reasoning:") or stripped.startswith("**"):
 1040              continue
 1041          # Drop lines containing Why: or Reasoning:
 1042          if "Why:" in line or "Reasoning:" in line:
 1043              continue
 1044          # Drop lines containing --- (separator)
 1045          if "---" in line:
 1046              continue
 1047          cleaned.append(line)
 1048      text = "\n".join(cleaned).strip()
 1049      text = _strip_nfa(text)
 1050      # Nuclear SKIP check: if SKIP appears anywhere in the final text, reject entirely
 1051      if "SKIP" in text:
 1052          return ""
 1053      return text
 1054  
 1055  
 1056  def _clean_tweet(text: str) -> str:
 1057      """Collapse all whitespace (spaces, newlines, tabs) into single spaces."""
 1058      return " ".join(text.split())
 1059  
 1060  
 1061  def _strip_hashtags(text: str) -> str:
 1062      """Remove any hashtags the AI included despite instructions."""
 1063      # Remove standalone hashtag words (e.g. #Bitcoin, #BTC)
 1064      text = re.sub(r'\s*#\w+', '', text)
 1065      # Clean up any trailing whitespace or blank lines left behind
 1066      text = re.sub(r'\n\s*\n\s*$', '', text).strip()
 1067      return text
 1068  
 1069  
 1070  # Phrases the AI falls back on too often — reject and retry
 1071  _BANNED_STARTS = [
 1072      "worth noting", "it's worth noting", "interesting spot",
 1073      "interesting to see", "fun fact", "here's the thing",
 1074      "not gonna lie", "let's talk", "can we talk",
 1075      "quick thought", "hot take:", "i'll say this",
 1076  ]
 1077  
 1078  
 1079  def _is_too_similar(new_tweet: str) -> bool:
 1080      """Check if a new tweet is too similar to recent ones."""
 1081      if not _recent_tweets:
 1082          return False
 1083      new_lower = new_tweet.lower()
 1084      # Check for banned openings
 1085      for phrase in _BANNED_STARTS:
 1086          if new_lower.startswith(phrase):
 1087              return True
 1088      # Check for near-duplicate content with recent tweets
 1089      new_words = set(new_lower.split())
 1090      for recent in _recent_tweets[-5:]:
 1091          recent_words = set(recent.lower().split())
 1092          if not recent_words:
 1093              continue
 1094          overlap = len(new_words & recent_words) / max(len(new_words), len(recent_words))
 1095          if overlap > 0.6:
 1096              return True
 1097      return False
 1098

 1100  # ── System prompt for all tweet generation ──────────────────────────────────
 1101  
 1102  _SYSTEM = """You are @CoinWatchAlert — a sharp crypto trader account. Think Coin Bureau meets Zach XBT. You make calls, not commentary. People follow you to screenshot your predictions later.
 1103  
 1104  ABSOLUTE RULES:
 1105  - Under 275 characters per tweet
 1106  - ZERO hashtags. Zero URLs. Zero 'via' attributions
 1107  - Max 1 emoji per tweet, at the very start. Allowed: ⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀
 1108  - Only use price data provided — never fabricate numbers
 1109  - No "WAGMI", "LFG", "NFA", or crypto bro speak
 1110  - Do NOT wrap your response in quotes
 1111  
 1112  NEVER START WITH:
 1113  - "Bitcoin" / "Worth noting" / "Interesting" / "Fun fact" / "Hot take:" / "Let's talk" / "Quick thought"
 1114  
 1115  NEVER USE (banned phrases):
 1116  - "this signals" / "this suggests" / "this indicates" / "worth watching"
 1117  - "remains to be seen" / "time will tell" / "could go either way"
 1118  - "could" / "might" / "may" / "potentially" / "possibly" / "likely"
 1119  These are news feed phrases. You're a trader. Make a CALL.
 1120  
 1121  VOICE:
 1122  - Write like a trader texting a group chat. Short sentences. Max 15 words each.
 1123  - MAKE CALLS: "Breaks $X or dumps to $Y" — not "worth watching"
 1124  - Take a side. Every tweet has a DIRECTION. Never neutral.
 1125  - Be specific: price levels, timeframes, percentages
 1126  - Use contractions (don't, won't, can't)
 1127  - Write tweets people want to screenshot
 1128  
 1129  FORMATTING:
 1130  - 2-3 short blocks separated by blank lines. Never walls of text.
 1131  - One thought per line. Short > long.
 1132  
 1133    OPINIONS:
 1134      ⚡ BTC HOLDING $67.3K AFTER THAT 68K REJECTION
 1135  
 1136      Structure still weak — lower highs on the 4h.
 1137  
 1138      Reclaim 68.5k or this heads to 65k.
 1139  
 1140    RECAPS:
 1141      ● BTC $67,300 (+2.1%) 🟢
 1142      ● ETH $1,970 (+1.8%) 🟢
 1143      ● SOL $95.50 (+3.2%) 🟢
 1144  
 1145      Market tone: cautious risk-on
 1146  
 1147    RAW COMMENTARY:
 1148      PI bleeding -10.1% to $0.2028.
 1149      $2B market cap and still no real utility.
 1150      Below $0.19 and this goes to $0.15."""

 1164          ),
 1165      },
 1166      "alt_spotlight": {
 1167          "label": "Altcoin spotlight",
 1168          "instruction": (
 1169              "DO NOT MENTION BITCOIN AT ALL. Pick one alt from the data and make a CALL on it. "

 1171              "Start with the coin name. Be specific with targets. "
 1172              "Example: 'SOL at $95 and about to test $100. If it breaks, $120 is in play this month. "
 1173              "Volume says this one's real.' "

 1179          "instruction": (
 1180              "DO NOT write about any specific coin's price action. Write about the BIGGER "

 1182              "adoption, stablecoin supply. But don't just describe it — make a PREDICTION about "
 1183              "what it means for the market. Take a stance. "
 1184              "Example: 'Stablecoin supply hitting ATH while everyone's bearish. "
 1185              "Last time this set up, BTC rallied 40% in 3 months. Same setup, same trade.'"
 1186          ),
 1187      },
 1188      "contrarian_take": {
 1189          "label": "Contrarian / hot take",
 1190          "instruction": (
 1191              "Write a CONTRARIAN take that goes AGAINST the current sentiment. If the market "

 1193              "Disagree with something most of Crypto Twitter believes. "
 1194              "End with a dare or challenge: 'Screenshot this.' / 'Bookmark this tweet.' / "
 1195              "'Come back in 30 days.' Be bold — this is the tweet people remember."
 1196          ),
 1197      },
 1198      "trader_conviction": {
 1199          "label": "Conviction call",
 1200          "instruction": (
 1201              "Make a CONVICTION CALL that forces followers to take notice. Not vague — specific. "

 1257  
 1258  
 1259  def _pick_quote_category(recent_categories: list[str]) -> str:
 1260      """Pick a content category that hasn't been used recently.
 1261  
 1262      Non-BTC categories are weighted 2x to reduce Bitcoin dominance in the feed.
 1263      """
 1264      btc_focused = {"btc_price", "market_structure"}
 1265      weights = {k: 1 if k in btc_focused else 2 for k in QUOTE_CATEGORIES}
 1266      # Down-weight categories used in last 3 posts
 1267      for cat in recent_categories[-3:]:
 1268          if cat in weights:
 1269              weights[cat] = max(1, weights[cat] - 1)
 1270      categories = list(weights.keys())
 1271      weighted = [cat for cat in categories for _ in range(weights[cat])]
 1272      return random.choice(weighted)
 1273  
 1274  
 1275  # ── Core availability + raw Claude call ──────────────────────────────────────
 1276  
 1277  def is_available() -> bool:
 1278      """Return True if Anthropic API key is configured."""
 1279      return bool(config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)
 1280  
 1281  
 1282  def _call_claude(
 1283      system: str,
 1284      prompt: str,
 1285      max_tokens: int = 120,
 1286  ) -> str | None:
 1287      """Make a raw Claude API call. Returns text or None on failure."""
 1288      if not is_available():
 1289          return None
 1290      try:
 1291          message = _get_client().messages.create(
 1292              model=MODEL,
 1293              max_tokens=max_tokens,
 1294              system=system,
 1295              messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
 1296          )
 1297          text = message.content[0].text.strip()
 1298          if text.startswith('"') and text.endswith('"'):
 1299              text = text[1:-1]
 1300          if text.startswith("'") and text.endswith("'"):
 1301              text = text[1:-1]
 1302          return text or None
 1303      except Exception as exc:
 1304          logger.warning("Claude API call failed: %s", exc)
 1305          return None
 1306  
 1307  
 1308  # ── Tweet generators used by tweet_generators.py ──────────────────────────────
 1309  
 1310  def generate_quote_tweet(
 1311      price: float,
 1312      pct_24h: float,
 1313      pct_7d: float,
 1314      mcap: float,
 1315      coins: list[dict],
 1316  ) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
 1317      """
 1318      Generate a market analysis tweet using QUOTE_CATEGORIES rotation.
 1319      Returns (tweet_text, category_name). Falls back to ("", "failed").
 1320      Used by tweet_generators.generate_quote_tweet().
 1321      """
 1322      if not is_available():
 1323          return None, "unavailable"
 1324  
 1325      recent_cats = []
 1326      try:
 1327          import state as _state
 1328          recent_cats = _state.get_recent_content_categories(6)
 1329      except Exception:
 1330          pass
 1331  
 1332      category_key = _pick_quote_category(recent_cats)
 1333      category = QUOTE_CATEGORIES[category_key]
 1334  
 1335      # Build coin context string
 1336      coin_lines = []
 1337      for c in coins[:6]:
 1338          sym = c.get("symbol", "").upper()
 1339          p = c.get("current_price", 0)
 1340          pct = c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
 1341          sign = "+" if pct > 0 else ""
 1342          if p >= 1000:
 1343              p_str = f"${p:,.0f}"
 1344          elif p >= 1:
 1345              p_str = f"${p:,.2f}"
 1346          else:
 1347              p_str = f"${p:.4f}"
 1348          coin_lines.append(f"{sym}: {p_str} ({sign}{pct:.1f}%)")
 1349      coin_context = "\n".join(coin_lines)
 1350  
 1351      mcap_str = ""
 1352      if mcap >= 1e12:
 1353          mcap_str = f"BTC market cap: ${mcap / 1e12:.2f}T"
 1354      elif mcap >= 1e9:
 1355          mcap_str = f"BTC market cap: ${mcap / 1e9:.0f}B"
 1356  
 1357      sign_24h = "+" if pct_24h > 0 else ""
 1358      sign_7d = "+" if pct_7d > 0 else ""
 1359  
 1360      prompt = (
 1361          f"BTC: ${price:,.0f} ({sign_24h}{pct_24h:.1f}% 24h, {sign_7d}{pct_7d:.1f}% 7d)\n"
 1362          f"{mcap_str}\n\n"
 1363          f"Market:\n{coin_context}\n\n"
 1364          f"Task: {category['instruction']}\n\n"
 1365          f"Write exactly 3 lines with a blank line between each:\n"
 1366          f"Line 1: THE TAKE in caps or bold phrasing. Key data point.\n"
 1367          f"Line 2: One fact backing it. Short sentence.\n"
 1368          f"Line 3: The call — ONE complete sentence with direction and conviction. Never split across two lines.\n\n"
 1369          f"Trader voice. No hashtags. No URLs. No hedging. Under 260 chars."
 1370      )
 1371  
 1372      tweet = _call_claude_safe(_ANALYST_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=180)
 1373      if not tweet:
 1374          return None, category_key
 1375  
 1376      tweet = tweet.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1377      tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
 1378      tweet = _strip_hashtags(tweet)
 1379      tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=260)
 1380      tweet = re.sub(r"[^\w\s\$\%\.\,\!\?\-\:\;—\→\@\'🚀📉⚡👀\n]", '', tweet).strip()
 1381  
 1382      if _is_too_similar(tweet):
 1383          logger.info("Quote tweet too similar to recent — retrying with different category")
 1384          return None, category_key
 1385  
 1386      return tweet, category_key
 1387  
 1388  
 1389  def generate_opinion_tweet(
 1390      price: float,
 1391      pct_24h: float,
 1392      pct_7d: float,
 1393      coins: list[dict],
 1394      defi_context: str | None = None,
 1395  ) -> str | None:
 1396      """
 1397      Generate an opinionated market take. Used by tweet_generators.generate_opinion_tweet().
 1398      """
 1399      if not is_available():
 1400          return None
 1401  
 1402      sign_24h = "+" if pct_24h > 0 else ""
 1403      sign_7d = "+" if pct_7d > 0 else ""
 1404  
 1405      coin_lines = []
 1406      for c in coins[:4]:
 1407          sym = c.get("symbol", "").upper()
 1408          p = c.get("current_price", 0)
 1409          pct = c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
 1410          sign = "+" if pct > 0 else ""
 1411          if p >= 1000:
 1412              p_str = f"${p:,.0f}"
 1413          elif p >= 1:
 1414              p_str = f"${p:,.2f}"
 1415          else:
 1416              p_str = f"${p:.4f}"
 1417          coin_lines.append(f"{sym}: {p_str} ({sign}{pct:.1f}%)")
 1418  
 1419      defi_line = f"\n{defi_context}" if defi_context else ""
 1420  
 1421      prompt = (
 1422          f"BTC at ${price:,.0f} ({sign_24h}{pct_24h:.1f}% 24h, {sign_7d}{pct_7d:.1f}% 7d).\n\n"
 1423          f"Write a 1-2 sentence opinion. Direct statement, not analysis.\n\n"
 1424          f"Sound like a trader making a call, not an analyst explaining.\n"
 1425          f"Slight edge or controversy preferred. Make the reader feel challenged.\n"
 1426          f"Confident. Decisive. No hedging. No neutral tone.\n\n"
 1427          f"BANNED: suggests, could, might, possibly, appears, seems, indicates, "
 1428          f"bullish, bearish, looking strong, gaining momentum\n\n"
 1429          f"Style examples:\n"
 1430          f"'Most people in this market are exit liquidity.'\n"
 1431          f"'If you're waiting for confirmation, you're already late.'\n"
 1432          f"'Retail only shows up when risk is highest.'\n\n"
 1433          f"Rules:\n"
 1434          f"- 1-2 sentences MAX. No explanations.\n"
 1435          f"- Never start with 'Bitcoin' or a coin name.\n"
 1436          f"- No questions. No hashtags. No URLs.\n"
 1437          f"- Max 1 emoji at the start. Max 200 chars.\n"
 1438          f"Output ONLY the tweet, nothing else."
 1439      )
 1440  
 1441      for attempt in range(1, 4):
 1442          tweet = _call_claude_safe(_ANALYST_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=180)
 1443          if not tweet:
 1444              if attempt < 3:
 1445                  time.sleep(2)
 1446                  continue
 1447              return None
 1448  
 1449          tweet = tweet.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1450          tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
 1451          tweet = _strip_hashtags(tweet)
 1452          tweet = re.sub(r"[^\w\s\$\%\.\,\!\?\-\:\;\—\@\'🚀📉⚡👀\n]", '', tweet)
 1453          tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=280)
 1454  
 1455          if _needs_regen(tweet) and attempt < 3:
 1456              logger.info("[AI] Opinion tweet weak — retry %d", attempt)
 1457              time.sleep(2)
 1458              continue
 1459  
 1460          if _is_too_similar(tweet):
 1461              return None
 1462  
 1463          logger.info("[AI] Opinion tweet generated")
 1464          return tweet
 1465  
 1466      return None
 1467  
 1468  
 1469  def generate_market_open_tweet(
 1470      btc_price: float, btc_pct: float, eth_price: float, eth_pct: float
 1471  ) -> str | None:
 1472      """Generate a high-conviction market open tweet with sharp trader voice."""
 1473      if not is_available():
 1474          return None
 1475  
 1476      prompt = (
 1477          f"Write a sharp one-liner market open tweet.\n\n"
 1478          f"Format: ⚡ [asset name] [price level] — [what it means]\n\n"
 1479          f"Live data:\n"
 1480          f"BTC ${btc_price:,.0f} ({btc_pct:+.1f}%)\n"
 1481          f"ETH ${eth_price:,.0f} ({eth_pct:+.1f}%)\n\n"
 1482          f"Examples:\n"
 1483          f"'⚡ BTC holding $68k — compression building, expansion incoming'\n"
 1484          f"'⚡ ETH reclaimed $2,400 — first sign of strength in weeks'\n"
 1485          f"'⚡ BTC $67.2k — sellers exhausted, no follow-through on the drop'\n\n"
 1486          f"Rules:\n"
 1487          f"- Exactly ONE line. Starts with ⚡\n"
 1488          f"- Must include asset name and price level from the live data\n"
 1489          f"- Must include a clear read on what it means\n"
 1490          f"- No generic 'Markets open'. No fluff. No hedging.\n"
 1491          f"- Confident trader voice. Max 140 chars.\n\n"
 1492          f"Return ONLY the one-liner. Nothing else."
 1493      )
 1494  
 1495      result = _call_claude_safe(_ANALYST_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=80)
 1496      if not result:
 1497          return None
 1498      result = result.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1499      if len(result) > 140:
 1500          result = result[:137] + "..."
 1501      return result
 1502  
 1503  
 1504  def generate_rotation_tweet(coins: list[dict]) -> str | None:
 1505      """Generate a rotation/flow tweet showing where strength is moving."""
 1506      if not is_available():
 1507          return None
 1508  
 1509      coin_lines = "\n".join([f"{c['symbol']}: {c['pct']:+.1f}%" for c in coins[:6]])
 1510  
 1511      prompt = (
 1512          f"Write a high-conviction crypto rotation tweet in a sharp trader voice.\n\n"
 1513          f"You have this live price data:\n{coin_lines}\n\n"
 1514          f"Rules:\n"
 1515          f"- Max 240 characters\n"
 1516          f"- EXACTLY 3 lines\n"
 1517          f"- No emojis\n"
 1518          f"- No generic phrases\n\n"
 1519          f"Structure:\n"
 1520          f"Line 1: Where is strength rotating TO right now "
 1521          f"(name 1-2 coins showing relative strength)\n"
 1522          f"Line 2: Which coins are lagging or showing weakness\n"
 1523          f"Line 3: What this rotation signals — positioning or flow implication\n\n"
 1524          f"Tone: sounds like smart money tracking flow, not retail watching prices.\n\n"
 1525          f"Hard constraint: MUST reference specific coins from the data. "
 1526          f"MUST imply where money is moving.\n\n"
 1527          f"Return ONLY the tweet. No explanation."
 1528      )
 1529  
 1530      result = _call_claude_safe(_ANALYST_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=120)
 1531      if not result or len(result) < 20:
 1532          return None
 1533      result = result.strip().replace("\\n", "\n")
 1534      if len(result) > 240:
 1535          result = result[:237] + "..."
 1536      return result
 1537  
 1538  
 1539  def generate_opinion_bomb() -> str | None:
 1540      """Generate a single-sentence conviction tweet. No data, no chart, pure edge.
 1541  
 1542      Must challenge conventional thinking or reframe market behaviour.
 1543      Exactly 1 sentence. No questions. No explanations.
 1544      """
 1545      if not is_available():
 1546          return None
 1547  
 1548      prompt = (
 1549          "Write exactly ONE sentence about crypto markets.\n\n"
 1550          "It must:\n"
 1551          "- Challenge conventional thinking or reframe market behaviour\n"
 1552          "- Sound like pure conviction from a trader who has seen everything\n"
 1553          "- Be a direct statement, not analysis or explanation\n"
 1554          "- Make the reader stop scrolling and think\n\n"
 1555          "BANNED: suggests, could, might, possibly, appears, seems, indicates, "
 1556          "bullish, bearish, looking strong, gaining momentum\n\n"
 1557          "Style examples:\n"
 1558          "'Volume spikes before price — always.'\n"
 1559          "'Most altcoins are just liquidity traps.'\n"
 1560          "'Breakdowns happen quietly first.'\n"
 1561          "'The best trades feel wrong when you enter them.'\n"
 1562          "'Leverage doesn't create winners — it accelerates outcomes.'\n\n"
 1563          "Rules:\n"
 1564          "- Exactly 1 sentence. No second sentence. No explanation.\n"
 1565          "- No data references. No price levels. No coin names.\n"
 1566          "- No questions. No hashtags. No URLs.\n"
 1567          "- Max 1 emoji at start. Max 140 chars.\n"
 1568          "Output ONLY the sentence, nothing else."
 1569      )
 1570  
 1571      for attempt in range(1, 4):
 1572          tweet = _call_claude_safe(_ANALYST_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=60)
 1573          if not tweet:
 1574              if attempt < 3:
 1575                  time.sleep(2)
 1576                  continue
 1577              return None
 1578  
 1579          tweet = tweet.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1580          tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
 1581          tweet = _strip_hashtags(tweet)
 1582          tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=140)
 1583  
 1584          if _needs_regen(tweet) and attempt < 3:
 1585              logger.info("[AI] Opinion bomb weak — retry %d", attempt)
 1586              time.sleep(2)
 1587              continue
 1588  
 1589          logger.info("[AI] Opinion bomb generated: %s", tweet)
 1590          return tweet
 1591  
 1592      return None
 1593  
 1594  
 1595  def generate_engagement_tweet(
 1596      price: float,
 1597      pct_24h: float,
 1598      pct_7d: float,
 1599      coins: list[dict],
 1600  ) -> str | None:
 1601      """
 1602      Generate a bold, opinionated engagement tweet. Used by tweet_generators.generate_engagement_tweet().
 1603      """
 1604      if not is_available():
 1605          return None
 1606  
 1607      prompt = (
 1608          f"BTC at ${price:,.0f} ({pct_24h:+.1f}% 24h, {pct_7d:+.1f}% 7d).\n\n"
 1609          f"Write EXACTLY 3 lines separated by newlines.\n\n"
 1610          f"Line 1: ⚡ + DECLARATION. Write as if you already know the likely outcome. "
 1611          f"Slightly provocative — make people want to reply.\n"
 1612          f"Line 2: Specific market insight with context. What it actually means.\n"
 1613          f"Line 3: Start with → then a SPECIFIC price level or trigger. "
 1614          f"e.g. '→ $68,200 is the line — break it or fade back to $66K'\n\n"
 1615          f"BANNED: weak participation, conviction is missing, risk-off pressure, "
 1616          f"bearish sentiment, market uncertainty, mixed signals, watch the reaction, "
 1617          f"market is compressing (without a level)\n\n"
 1618          f"Rules:\n"
 1619          f"- Write like you already have edge, not like you're reporting.\n"
 1620          f"- Short confident sentences. No filler.\n"
 1621          f"- Never start with 'Bitcoin'. Vary the opening.\n"
 1622          f"- No hashtags. No URLs. No hedging.\n"
 1623          f"- Each line must be a complete sentence. Do NOT merge lines into one.\n"
 1624          f"- Final line MUST start with → and have a numeric level or conditional trigger.\n"
 1625          f"- Max 1 emoji at the start. Max 280 chars.\n\n"
 1626          f"Return exactly 3 lines separated by newline characters. Do not combine into one line."
 1627      )
 1628  
 1629      tweet = _call_claude_safe(_ANALYST_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=180)
 1630      if not tweet:
 1631          return None
 1632  
 1633      tweet = tweet.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1634      tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
 1635      tweet = _strip_hashtags(tweet)
 1636      tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=280)
 1637  
 1638      return tweet

 1650  
 1651  _REPLY_SYSTEM = (
 1652      "You are CoinWatchAlert replying to a high-visibility crypto tweet. "
 1653      "Tone: calm, analytical, slightly cryptic. Observational not reactive. "
 1654      "Confident not excited. You see what others miss.\n\n"
 1655      "EACH REPLY MUST CONTAIN ONE OF:\n"
 1656      "- CONTRAST: 'Everyone's watching X, but Y is moving'\n"
 1657      "- REVERSAL: 'Looks obvious — usually isn't'\n"
 1658      "- HIDDEN SIGNAL: 'Focus on reaction / liquidity / positioning, not headline'\n\n"
 1659      "FORBIDDEN WORDS: bullish, bearish, moon, huge, massive, big move, going crazy\n\n"
 1660      "RULES:\n"
 1661      "- 1-2 lines max. Under 200 chars.\n"
 1662      "- Never start with 'Great point', 'Exactly', 'Agree', 'This', 'So true'\n"
 1663      "- No hashtags. No URLs. No hedging.\n"
 1664      "- Add NEW insight — never repeat the original tweet.\n\n"
 1665      "EXAMPLES:\n"
 1666      "- 'Everyone's watching price. Liquidity's doing something else.'\n"
 1667      "- 'Panic always looks obvious in hindsight.'\n"
 1668      "- 'This is where people confuse momentum with strength.'\n"
 1669      "- 'The reaction matters more than the move itself.'\n\n"
 1670      "GOAL: Make readers curious enough to click the profile."
 1671  )

 1673  
 1674  def generate_reply(tweet_text: str) -> str | None:
 1675      """
 1676      Generate a calm, analytical reply to a high-visibility crypto tweet.
 1677  
 1678      Observational, slightly cryptic. 1-2 lines. Under 200 chars.
 1679      """
 1680      if not is_available():
 1681          return None
 1682  
 1683      question_directive = ""
 1684      if random.random() < 0.25:
 1685          question_directive = (
 1686              "\n- End with a short cryptic question "
 1687              "(e.g. 'Who's actually accumulating here?' / 'Is this the move or the setup?')"
 1688          )
 1689  
 1690      prompt = (
 1691          f"Reply to this tweet:\n\n\"{tweet_text}\"\n\n"
 1692          "Rules:\n"
 1693          "- 1-2 lines only. Under 200 chars. Every word must earn its place.\n"
 1694          "- Use one of: CONTRAST, REVERSAL, or HIDDEN SIGNAL framing\n"
 1695          "- Calm and analytical, not reactive or excited\n"
 1696          "- Never use: bullish, bearish, moon, huge, massive, big move, going crazy\n"
 1697          f"- Never start with 'Great point', 'Exactly', 'Agree', 'This'{question_directive}\n"
 1698          "Output ONLY the reply text, nothing else."
 1699      )
 1700  
 1701      result = _call_claude_safe(_REPLY_SYSTEM, prompt, max_tokens=120)
 1702      if not result:
 1703          return None
 1704      result = _strip_unwanted_lines(result)
 1705      result = _clean_tweet(result)
 1706      result = _strip_hashtags(result)
 1707      return _truncate_tweet(result, limit=200)

 1710  def generate_geopolitical_tweet(story: dict) -> list[str]:
 1711      """
 1712      Generate a 3-tweet thread for a macro/geopolitical news story.
 1713  
 1714      Tweet 1: The event + immediate market impact. Bold, declarative, data point if possible.
 1715      Tweet 2: The crypto/hard asset connection — why this moves BTC, oil, gold. Specific levels.
 1716      Tweet 3: "Bottom line:" — directional call with timeframe.
 1717  
 1718      No numbering. No questions. Under 200 chars each. Returns [] on failure.
 1719      """
 1720      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
 1721          logger.warning("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – cannot generate geopolitical thread")
 1722          return []
 1723  
 1724      title = story.get("title", "")
 1725      source = story.get("source", "")
 1726      commentary = story.get("commentary", "")
 1727  
 1728      context_block = f"Headline: {title}\nSource: {source}"
 1729      if commentary:
 1730          context_block += f"\nAnalyst note: {commentary}"
 1731  
 1732      prompt = (
 1733          f"Write a 3-tweet thread. One tweet per line, no numbering.\n\n"
 1734          f"{context_block}\n\n"
 1735          "Tweet 1: HOOK — what does this event MEAN, not just what happened. Tension or implication.\n\n"
 1736          "Tweet 2: The crypto connection. Interpret through positioning/liquidity lens. Not just 'BTC reacts'.\n\n"
 1737          "Tweet 3: 'Bottom line:' + directional call with conviction.\n\n"
 1738          "Rules:\n"
 1739          "- Data without interpretation is noise. Every fact must answer: what does this mean right now?\n"
 1740          "- No numbering. No questions. No hedging.\n"
 1741          "- No hashtags. No URLs. No 'via' credits.\n"
 1742          "- Never start with 'Bitcoin'.\n"
 1743          "- Each tweet under 200 chars.\n"
 1744          "- Max 1 emoji per tweet at start. Allowed: ⚡🚨📉👀\n"
 1745          "- Trader voice. Short sentences.\n"
 1746          "- Output ONLY the 3 tweet lines, nothing else"
 1747      )
 1748  
 1749      try:
 1750          message = _get_client().messages.create(
 1751              model=MODEL,
 1752              max_tokens=400,
 1753              system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
 1754              messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
 1755          )
 1756          raw = message.content[0].text.strip()
 1757          if _contains_ai_refusal(raw):
 1758              logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in geopolitical tweet — discarding")
 1759              return []
 1760          raw = _strip_unwanted_lines(raw)
 1761          tweets = [line.strip() for line in raw.splitlines() if line.strip()]
 1762          tweets = [_truncate_tweet(t, limit=200) if len(t) > 200 else t for t in tweets]
 1763          logger.info("Generated geopolitical thread (%d tweets): %.80s", len(tweets), title)
 1764          return tweets
 1765      except anthropic.APIError as exc:
 1766          logger.warning("Claude API error generating geopolitical thread: %s", exc)
 1767          return []
 1768
