 1770  # ── Volume anomaly tweet ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1771  
 1772  def generate_volume_anomaly_tweet(
 1773      symbol: str, price: float, vol_ratio: float, price_change: float
 1774  ) -> str | None:
 1775      """Generate an insight-driven tweet about a volume anomaly detection."""
 1776      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
 1777          logger.warning("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – cannot generate volume anomaly tweet")
 1778          return None
 1779  
 1780      from price_monitor import _format_price
 1781      price_str = _format_price(price)
 1782  
 1783      # Context framing based on vol_ratio and price direction
 1784      if vol_ratio > 3:
 1785          vol_context = "Aggressive volume expansion — 3x+ the 7-day average"
 1786      else:
 1787          vol_context = f"Volume running {vol_ratio:.1f}x the 7-day average"
 1788  
 1789      if price_change < -1:
 1790          direction_context = "Price dipping on heavy volume — classic absorption pattern"
 1791      elif price_change < 0.5:
 1792          direction_context = "Price flat despite volume surge — someone is positioning quietly"
 1793      else:
 1794          direction_context = "Slight upward drift with volume confirmation — early accumulation signal"
 1795  
 1796      prompt = (
 1797          f"{symbol} is showing a volume anomaly. Price: {price_str}.\n"
 1798          f"Volume: {vol_context}.\n"
 1799          f"Price change: {price_change:+.2f}% (barely moved).\n"
 1800          f"Pattern: {direction_context}.\n\n"
 1801          "Write a 3-line tweet. Blank line between each.\n\n"
 1802          "Line 1 — HOOK: Something is happening beneath the surface. Tension, not hype.\n"
 1803          "Line 2 — WHAT'S HAPPENING: Volume anomaly — big participation, small move. "
 1804          "Frame as early signal, not confirmation.\n"
 1805          "Line 3 — WHAT IT MEANS: Who is likely positioning and what it sets up.\n\n"
 1806          "Rules:\n"
 1807          "- Calm, observational, early-signal framing. Not hype.\n"
 1808          "- This is a detection, not a call. Frame as 'something worth watching develops.'\n"
 1809          "- Never start with the coin name.\n"
 1810          f"- Max {MAX_TWEET_LENGTH} chars. No hashtags. No URLs.\n"
 1811          "- Max 1 emoji at start. Allowed: ⚡👀\n"
 1812          "Output ONLY the tweet."
 1813      )
 1814  
 1815      for attempt in range(1, 4):
 1816          try:
 1817              message = _get_client().messages.create(
 1818                  model=MODEL,
 1819                  max_tokens=120,
 1820                  system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
 1821                  messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
 1822              )
 1823              tweet = message.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1824              if _contains_ai_refusal(tweet):
 1825                  logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in volume anomaly tweet — retry %d", attempt)
 1826                  time.sleep(2)
 1827                  continue
 1828              tweet = _strip_unwanted_lines(tweet)
 1829              tweet = _ensure_line_breaks(tweet)
 1830              tweet = _truncate_tweet(tweet, limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)
 1831              if _needs_regen(tweet) and attempt < 3:
 1832                  logger.info("[AI] Volume anomaly tweet weak — retry %d", attempt)
 1833                  time.sleep(2)
 1834                  continue
 1835              logger.info("[AI] Volume anomaly tweet generated for %s: %.80s", symbol, tweet)
 1836              return tweet
 1837          except Exception as exc:
 1838              logger.warning("Claude API error for volume anomaly tweet (attempt %d): %s",
 1839                             attempt, exc)
 1840              if attempt < 3:
 1841                  time.sleep(2)
 1842  
 1843      logger.warning("All 3 volume anomaly tweet attempts failed")
 1844      return None
 1845

 1847  # ── Narrative tweet ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1848  
 1849  def generate_narrative_tweet(
 1850      theme: str, story_count: int, summaries: list[str]
 1851  ) -> str | None:
 1852      """Generate a tweet about an emerging narrative detected from multiple sources."""
 1853      if not config.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
 1854          logger.warning("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set – cannot generate narrative tweet")
 1855          return None
 1856  
 1857      summary_block = "\n".join(f"- {s}" for s in summaries[:5])
 1858  
 1859      prompt = (
 1860          f"You are writing a crypto market narrative tweet.\n\n"
 1861          f"STRICT OUTPUT FORMAT — NO EXCEPTIONS:\n\n"
 1862          f"Line 1: One complete sentence describing what story the market is telling right now.\n"
 1863          f"Line 2: One complete sentence explaining what this means for positioning.\n"
 1864          f"Line 3: One complete sentence starting with → describing the forward implication.\n\n"
 1865          f"EXAMPLE:\n"
 1866          f"Liquidity rotation is driving altcoin rallies, not fundamentals.\n"
 1867          f"Late buyers are providing exit liquidity for early holders.\n"
 1868          f"→ When BTC dominance flips back up, alts will unwind fast.\n\n"
 1869          f"RULES:\n"
 1870          f"- Output EXACTLY 3 lines\n"
 1871          f"- Each line must be a full sentence\n"
 1872          f"- Line 3 MUST start with →\n"
 1873          f"- Do NOT include any extra text before or after\n"
 1874          f"- Do NOT use bullet points, headers, or emojis\n"
 1875          f"- Trader tone, concise and sharp\n\n"
 1876          f"Narrative topic: {theme}\n\n"
 1877          f"Before responding, check:\n"
 1878          f"- Does the output have exactly 3 lines?\n"
 1879          f"- Does line 3 start with →?\n\n"
 1880          f"If not, fix it.\n\n"
 1881          f"Output ONLY the 3 lines."
 1882      )
 1883  
 1884      for attempt in range(1, 4):
 1885          try:
 1886              message = _get_client().messages.create(
 1887                  model=MODEL,
 1888                  max_tokens=150,
 1889                  system=_ANALYST_SYSTEM,
 1890                  messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
 1891              )
 1892              text = message.content[0].text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
 1893              if _contains_ai_refusal(text):
 1894                  logger.warning("[SAFETY] AI refusal in narrative tweet — retry %d", attempt)
 1895                  time.sleep(2)
 1896                  continue
 1897              text = _strip_unwanted_lines(text)
 1898              text = _strip_hashtags(text)
 1899              text = _ensure_line_breaks(text)
 1900              text = _truncate_tweet(text, limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)
 1901              if _needs_regen(text) and attempt < 3:
 1902                  logger.info("[AI] Narrative tweet weak — retry %d", attempt)
 1903                  time.sleep(2)
 1904                  continue
 1905              logger.info("[AI] Narrative tweet generated for '%s': %.80s", theme, text)
 1906              return text
 1907          except Exception as exc:
 1908              logger.warning("Claude API error for narrative tweet (attempt %d): %s", attempt, exc)
 1909              if attempt < 3:
 1910                  time.sleep(2)
 1911  
 1912      logger.warning("All 3 narrative attempts failed — using fallback")
 1913      return _truncate_tweet("⚡ Market is compressing — expansion comes next.",
 1914                             limit=MAX_TWEET_LENGTH)


================================================================================
  FILE: bot.py
================================================================================

    1  #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2  """
    3  Crypto News Twitter Bot – main entry point.
    4  
    5  Per-type daily caps: news 8, trending 4, price alerts 3, quote 1.
    6  
    7  SCHEDULED (UK/London time):
    8    • 08:00  morning_recap
    9    • 12:00  opinion
   10    • 16:00  engagement
   11    • 19:00  evening_thread  (3 tweets)
   12    • 21:00  fear_greed
   13  
   14  INTERVAL-DRIVEN (with daily caps):
   15    • Price alerts  – every 5 min check  | max 3/day | 90 min cooldown | 5%+ 1h or 6%+ 24h move
   16    • News          – every 15 min check | max 4/day | 60 min cooldown between posts
   17    • Trending coin – every 2 h check    | max 1/day
   18    • Quote tweet   – every 2 h check    | max 1/day
   19  
   20  Usage:
   21      python bot.py            # run forever
   22      python bot.py --dry-run  # print to stdout instead of posting
   23  """
   24  from __future__ import annotations
   25  
   26  import argparse
   27  import datetime
   28  import functools
   29  import logging
   30  import os
   31  import re
   32  import random
   33  import signal
   34  import socket
   35  import sys
   36  import time
   37  import requests
   38  import requests.exceptions
   39  from schedule import Scheduler as _Scheduler
   40  from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
   41  
   42  import ai_writer
   43  import chart_generator
   44  import fear_greed
   45  import config
   46  import news_monitor
   47  import price_monitor
   48  import telegram_client
   49  import state
   50  import twitter_client
   51  import tweet_generators
   52  import trending_monitor
   53  import volume_anomaly_scanner
   54  import reply_engine
   55  
   56  _LONDON_TZ = ZoneInfo("Europe/London")
   57  
   58  # Logger created at module level; handlers are attached in main() after arg
   59  # parsing so we know whether stdout is being redirected.
   60  logger = logging.getLogger("bot")
   61  
   62  # ── Globals ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   63  DRY_RUN = False
   64  
   65  # ── Posting guards ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   66  _QUIET_HOURS_START = 0   # midnight UK
   67  _QUIET_HOURS_END   = 7   # 7am UK
   68  
   69  _MIN_TWEET_GAP = 480     # 8 min minimum between any two posts
   70  _TYPE_COOLDOWN = 3600    # 1 hour between same tweet type
   71  
   72  # ── Tweet structure validation ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   73  _MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES = 3
   74  _BANNED_TWEET_PATTERNS = [
   75      r"risk[- ]?off",
   76      r"risk[- ]?on",
   77      r"weak participation",
   78      r"conviction is missing",
   79      r"sentiment",
   80      r"uncertainty",
   81      r"mixed signals",
   82  ]
   83  _TOPIC_COOLDOWN_SECS = 7200  # 2 hours per topic group
   84  
   85  _last_emit_time: float = 0.0
   86  _last_emit_text: str = ""
   87  _type_last_emit: dict[str, float] = {}
   88  _recent_tweet_types: list[str] = []  # last 3 tweet types for variety enforcement
   89  _recent_emit_texts: list[str] = []   # last 10 tweets for content dedup
   90  
   91  # ── Topic dedup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   92  _TOPIC_KEYWORDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
   93      "btc", "bitcoin",
   94      "eth", "ethereum",
   95      "sol", "solana",
   96      "bnb", "xrp", "ripple",
   97      "ada", "cardano",
   98      "doge", "dogecoin",
   99      "avax", "avalanche",
  100      "dot", "polkadot",
  101      "link", "chainlink",
  102      "l2", "layer2", "arbitrum", "optimism", "base", "zksync", "starknet",
  103      "defi", "dex", "cex", "tvl", "yield", "liquidity", "staking",
  104      "nft", "bridge", "stablecoin", "usdt", "usdc", "dai",
  105      "halving", "etf", "altcoin", "memecoin", "whale", "liquidation",
  106      "sec", "regulation", "cftc",
  107  })
  108  
  109  _TOPIC_GROUPS: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
  110      "L2_DEFI":    frozenset({
  111          "l2", "layer2", "arbitrum", "optimism", "base", "zksync", "starknet",
  112          "defi", "dex", "tvl", "bridge", "yield", "liquidity",
  113      }),
  114      "REGULATION": frozenset({"sec", "regulation", "cftc", "legal", "lawsuit", "ban"}),
  115      "STABLECOIN": frozenset({"usdt", "usdc", "stablecoin", "dai", "peg"}),
  116  }
  117  
  118  _topic_group_last_post: dict[str, float] = {}
  119  _last_2_emit_topics: list[set[str]] = []
  120  
  121  
  122  def _is_quiet_hours() -> bool:
  123      now_uk = datetime.datetime.now(_LONDON_TZ)
  124      return _QUIET_HOURS_START <= now_uk.hour < _QUIET_HOURS_END
  125  
  126  
  127  def _extract_topics(text: str) -> set[str]:
  128      words = {w.lower() for w in re.findall(r'\b\w+\b', text)}
  129      return words & _TOPIC_KEYWORDS
  130  
  131  
  132  def _is_duplicate_content(text: str) -> bool:
  133      """True if >55% word overlap with any of the last 10 posts."""
  134      a = set(text.lower().split())
  135      if not a:
  136          return False
  137      for prev in _recent_emit_texts:
  138          b = set(prev.lower().split())
  139          if not b:
  140              continue
  141          overlap = len(a & b) / max(len(a), len(b))
  142          if overlap > 0.55:
  143              logger.debug("Duplicate content: %.1f%% overlap with recent tweet", overlap * 100)
  144              return True
  145      return False
  146  
  147  
  148  def _is_duplicate_topic(topics: set[str]) -> bool:
  149      """True if >=2 shared topic keywords with any of the last 4 posts."""
  150      if not topics:
  151          return False
  152      for prev in _last_2_emit_topics[-4:]:
  153          if len(topics & prev) >= 2:
  154              logger.debug("Duplicate topic: shared %s with recent post",
  155                           topics & prev)
  156              return True
  157      return False
  158  
  159  
  160  def _check_topic_cooldown(text: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
  161      """Return (blocked, reason) if a topic group is in its 2-hour window."""
  162      now = time.monotonic()
  163      words = _extract_topics(text)
  164      for group, keywords in _TOPIC_GROUPS.items():
  165          if words & keywords:
  166              last = _topic_group_last_post.get(group, 0.0)
  167              if last > 0 and (now - last) < _TOPIC_COOLDOWN_SECS:
  168                  remaining = int((_TOPIC_COOLDOWN_SECS - (now - last)) / 60)
  169                  return True, f"{group} cooldown ({remaining}m left)"
  170      return False, ""
  171  
  172  
  173  def _record_emit_state(text: str) -> None:
  174      """Update topic history, content history, and group cooldown timestamps."""
  175      global _last_2_emit_topics
  176      topics = _extract_topics(text)
  177      _last_2_emit_topics.append(topics)
  178      if len(_last_2_emit_topics) > 6:
  179          _last_2_emit_topics = _last_2_emit_topics[-6:]
  180      _recent_emit_texts.append(text)
  181      if len(_recent_emit_texts) > 10:
  182          _recent_emit_texts[:] = _recent_emit_texts[-10:]
  183      now = time.monotonic()
  184      for group, keywords in _TOPIC_GROUPS.items():
  185          if topics & keywords:
  186              _topic_group_last_post[group] = now
  187  
  188  
  189  # ── Private scheduler (NOT the global schedule.default_scheduler) ──────────────
  190  # Using an owned instance prevents double-registration if this module is ever
  191  # imported alongside running as __main__ — both would share the global scheduler
  192  # but have separate _schedule_configured flags, silently adding jobs twice.
  193  _scheduler = _Scheduler()
  194  
  195  
  196  def _safe(fn):
  197      """Wrap a scheduled job so any unhandled exception is logged, not fatal."""
  198      @functools.wraps(fn)
  199      def _wrapper():
  200          try:
  201              return fn()
  202          except Exception:
  203              logger.exception(
  204                  "Unhandled exception in scheduled job '%s' — job skipped, bot continues.",
  205                  fn.__name__,
  206              )
  207      return _wrapper
  208  
  209  
  210  
  211  _RETRY_EXCEPTIONS = (ConnectionError, socket.error, OSError, requests.exceptions.ConnectionError)
  212  
  213  
  214  def _post_with_retry(text: str, image_path: str | None = None, retries: int = 3) -> bool:
  215      """Post a tweet, retrying on connection errors with a 10s backoff."""
  216      for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
  217          try:
  218              return twitter_client.post_tweet(text, image_path=image_path)
  219          except _RETRY_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
  220              logger.warning("post_tweet connection error (attempt %d/%d): %s", attempt, retries, exc)
  221              if attempt < retries:
  222                  time.sleep(10)
  223      return False
  224  
  225  
  226  def _post_thread_with_retry(
  227      tweets: list[str],
  228      first_tweet_image_path: str | None = None,
  229      retries: int = 3,
  230  ) -> bool:
  231      """Post a thread, retrying on connection errors with a 10s backoff."""
  232      for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
  233          try:
  234              return twitter_client.post_thread(tweets, first_tweet_image_path=first_tweet_image_path)
  235          except _RETRY_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
  236              logger.warning("post_thread connection error (attempt %d/%d): %s", attempt, retries, exc)
  237              if attempt < retries:
  238                  time.sleep(10)
  239      return False
  240  
  241  
  242  # ── Core emit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  243  def _emit(
  244      text: str,
  245      tweet_type: str = "general",
  246      bypass_guard: bool = False,
  247      media_path: str | None = None,
  248  ) -> bool:
  249      """Post a tweet (or print in dry-run). Returns True if posted/printed."""
  250      global _last_emit_time, _last_emit_text
  251  
  252      if not text or not text.strip():
  253          logger.warning("_emit called with empty text — skipping")
  254          return False
  255  
  256      if text and _last_emit_text and text.strip() == _last_emit_text.strip():
  257          logger.warning("Duplicate tweet blocked — identical to last post")
  258          return False
  259  
  260      # Nuclear filter: block AI reasoning artifacts and skip markers before anything else
  261      _NUCLEAR_BLOCK = ("SKIP", "---", "**Reasoning")
  262      for _pat in _NUCLEAR_BLOCK:
  263          if _pat in text:
  264              logger.warning(
  265                  "_emit nuclear filter blocked [%s]: text contains %r — not posting",
  266                  tweet_type, _pat,
  267              )
  268              return False
  269  
  270      # Strip any URLs that slipped through — analyst accounts don't post links
  271      import re as _re
  272      text = _re.sub(r'https?://\S+', '', text).strip()
  273      # Collapse triple+ line breaks to double max (keeps 3-line format clean)
  274      text = _re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', text)
  275      # Trim truncated sentences — never post text that ends mid-sentence
  276      _ENDING_RE = _re.compile(r'.*[.!?⚡🚨📉🔴🟢👀🚀)\"]', _re.DOTALL)
  277      m = _ENDING_RE.match(text)
  278      if m and len(m.group(0)) < len(text):
  279          text = m.group(0).rstrip()
  280  
  281      # SAFETY: block AI refusal text from ever being posted
  282      _AI_REFUSAL = [
  283          "i need to", "i appreciate", "i cannot", "i'm unable",
  284          "as an ai", "my core directive", "conflicts with", "i must decline",
  285          "i can't generate", "i can't create", "against my guidelines",
  286          "i can't write", "i cannot write", "the instruction asks",
  287          "i'm not able to", "i won't be able", "i won't write",
  288          "this request", "i should not",
  289      ]
  290      if any(phrase in text.lower() for phrase in _AI_REFUSAL):
  291          logger.critical("[SAFETY] AI refusal leaked into tweet — BLOCKED: %.100s", text)
  292          return False
  293  
  294      if not state.can_tweet():
  295          logger.critical("Monthly tweet cap reached.")
  296          return False
  297  
  298      if DRY_RUN:
  299          img_note = f"  [image: {media_path}]" if media_path else ""
  300          print(f"\n{'─'*60}\n[DRY RUN] [{tweet_type}]{img_note}\n{text}\n{'─'*60}")
  301          _last_emit_text = text
  302          _last_emit_time = time.time()
  303          if tweet_type != "general":
  304              _type_last_emit[tweet_type] = _last_emit_time
  305          _recent_tweet_types.append(tweet_type)
  306          if len(_recent_tweet_types) > 3:
  307              _recent_tweet_types[:] = _recent_tweet_types[-3:]
  308          _record_emit_state(text)
  309          return True
  310  
  311      # Daily tweet cap
  312      if state.get_total_daily_tweets() >= config.DAILY_TWEET_CAP:
  313          logger.warning("Daily tweet cap (%d) reached — skipping [%s].",
  314                         config.DAILY_TWEET_CAP, tweet_type)
  315          return False
  316  
  317      if not bypass_guard and _is_quiet_hours():
  318          logger.info("Quiet hours — skipping: %.60s", text)
  319          return False
  320  
  321      if _is_duplicate_content(text):
  322          logger.info("Skipping — duplicate content: %.60s", text)
  323          return False
  324  
  325      if ai_writer._is_too_similar(text):
  326          logger.info("Skipping — too similar to recent tweet (persisted check): %.60s", text)
  327          return False
  328  
  329      topics = _extract_topics(text)
  330      if _is_duplicate_topic(topics):
  331          logger.info("Skipping — duplicate topic: %.60s", text)
  332          return False
  333  
  334      blocked, reason = _check_topic_cooldown(text)
  335      if blocked:
  336          logger.info("Skipping — %s: %.60s", reason, text)
  337          return False
  338  
  339      if tweet_type != "general":
  340          last_type = _type_last_emit.get(tweet_type, 0.0)
  341          if last_type > 0 and (time.time() - last_type) < _TYPE_COOLDOWN:
  342              mins_left = int((_TYPE_COOLDOWN - (time.time() - last_type)) / 60)
  343              logger.info("Skipping %s — type cooldown (%dm left): %.60s",
  344                          tweet_type, mins_left, text)
  345              return False
  346  
  347      # Block consecutive tweets of the same type.
  348      # Exempt types that must always fire on schedule.
  349      _CONSECUTIVE_EXEMPT = frozenset({
  350          "morning_recap", "market_open", "engagement", "evening_thread", "fear_greed",
  351      })
  352      if (
  353          _recent_tweet_types
  354          and _recent_tweet_types[-1] == tweet_type
  355          and tweet_type not in _CONSECUTIVE_EXEMPT
  356      ):
  357          logger.info("Skipping %s — same type as last tweet: %.60s", tweet_type, text)
  358          return False
  359  
  360      now = time.time()
  361      if _last_emit_time > 0 and (now - _last_emit_time) < _MIN_TWEET_GAP:
  362          mins_left = int((_MIN_TWEET_GAP - (now - _last_emit_time)) / 60)
  363          logger.info("Skipping — min gap (%dm left): %.60s", mins_left, text)
  364          return False
  365  
  366      # Image: use pre-fetched media_path if provided; otherwise generate chart by type
  367      # 35% of opinion_bomb and engagement tweets go text-only for variety
  368      img_path = media_path
  369      if img_path is None and tweet_type in ("opinion_bomb", "engagement") and random.random() < 0.35:
  370          logger.info("[TEXT-ONLY] Skipping chart for %s tweet (35%% text-only branch)", tweet_type)
  371          img_path = None  # explicit text-only
  372      elif img_path is None:
  373          try:
  374              img_path = _chart_for_tweet(text)
  375          except Exception as exc:
  376              logger.warning("Chart generation failed: %s", exc)
  377  
  378      posted = _post_with_retry(text, image_path=img_path)
  379      if posted:
  380          _last_emit_time = time.time()
  381          _last_emit_text = text
  382          if tweet_type != "general":
  383              _type_last_emit[tweet_type] = _last_emit_time
  384          _recent_tweet_types.append(tweet_type)
  385          if len(_recent_tweet_types) > 3:
  386              _recent_tweet_types[:] = _recent_tweet_types[-3:]
  387          state.record_tweet()
  388          state.increment_daily_count(tweet_type)
  389          ai_writer.record_recent_tweet(text)
  390          _record_emit_state(text)
  391          logger.info("Posted [%s]: %.80s", tweet_type, text)
  392  
  393          # Mirror to Telegram channel
  394          try:
  395              telegram_client.send_telegram(text, image_path=img_path)
  396          except Exception as exc:
  397              logger.warning("Telegram mirror failed (non-fatal): %s", exc)
  398  
  399      if img_path and img_path is not media_path:
  400          # Only unlink images we generated ourselves; caller-provided are cleaned up here too
  401          pass
  402      if img_path:
  403          try:
  404              os.unlink(img_path)
  405          except OSError:
  406              pass
  407  
  408      return bool(posted)
  409  
  410  
  411  # ── Daily slot guard ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  412  _fired_today: dict[str, datetime.date] = {}
  413  
  414  
  415  def _should_fire(slot: str, hour: int, *, minute: int | None = None) -> bool:
  416      now_uk = datetime.datetime.now(_LONDON_TZ)
  417      today = now_uk.date()
  418      if minute is not None:
  419          if now_uk.hour != hour or now_uk.minute != minute:
  420              logger.debug("_should_fire(%s): wrong time %02d:%02d (need %02d:%02d)",
  421                           slot, now_uk.hour, now_uk.minute, hour, minute)
  422              return False
  423      else:
  424          if now_uk.hour != hour:
  425              return False
  426      if _fired_today.get(slot) == today:
  427          logger.debug("_should_fire(%s): already fired in-memory for %s", slot, today)
  428          return False
  429      daily = state.get_daily_count(slot)
  430      if daily > 0:
  431          _fired_today[slot] = today
  432          logger.debug("_should_fire(%s): already fired in state (daily_count=%d) for %s",
  433                        slot, daily, today)
  434          return False
  435      # Immediately mark in-memory to prevent a second scheduler tick within
  436      # the same minute from passing the guard while the job is still running.
  437      _fired_today[slot] = today
  438      logger.info("_should_fire(%s): READY — hour=%d, marked in-memory to block duplicates", slot, hour)
  439      return True
  440  
  441  
  442  def _mark_slot_fired(slot: str) -> None:
  443      """Mark a timed slot as fired for today (call after successful post).
  444  
  445      Persists to state file immediately so a duplicate check within the
  446      same minute (before the in-memory dict is consulted) is blocked.
  447      """
  448      _fired_today[slot] = datetime.datetime.now(_LONDON_TZ).date()
  449      if state.get_daily_count(slot) == 0:
  450          state.increment_daily_count(slot)
  451  
  452  
  453  # ── Chart coin variety ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  454  # For general tweets (opinion, engagement, quote), occasionally show a non-BTC
  455  # chart so the feed doesn't look like a BTC-only account.
  456  _CHART_COIN_CHOICES: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
  457      ("bitcoin", "BTC"),
  458      ("bitcoin", "BTC"),
  459      ("bitcoin", "BTC"),      # 60% BTC
  460      ("ethereum", "ETH"),
  461      ("solana", "SOL"),
  462  ]
  463  
  464  
  465  def _pick_chart_coin() -> tuple[str, str]:
  466      """Return a (coin_id, symbol) for chart generation, weighted toward BTC."""
  467      return random.choice(_CHART_COIN_CHOICES)
  468  
  469  
  470  _MACRO_RE = re.compile(
  471      r'\b(stablecoin|USDT|USDC|dollar|inflation|Fed|macro|treasury|DeFi|onchain)\b',
  472      re.IGNORECASE,
  473  )
  474  
  475  
  476  # ── Coin lookup for chart matching ────────────────────────────────────────────
  477  # Maps keywords (uppercase) to (coingecko_id, symbol) for chart generation.
  478  _COIN_CHART_MAP: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
  479      "BTC": ("bitcoin", "BTC"), "BITCOIN": ("bitcoin", "BTC"),
  480      "ETH": ("ethereum", "ETH"), "ETHEREUM": ("ethereum", "ETH"),
  481      "SOL": ("solana", "SOL"), "SOLANA": ("solana", "SOL"),
  482      "BNB": ("binancecoin", "BNB"), "BINANCE COIN": ("binancecoin", "BNB"),
  483      "XRP": ("ripple", "XRP"), "RIPPLE": ("ripple", "XRP"),
  484      "ADA": ("cardano", "ADA"), "CARDANO": ("cardano", "ADA"),
  485      "DOGE": ("dogecoin", "DOGE"), "DOGECOIN": ("dogecoin", "DOGE"),
  486      "AVAX": ("avalanche-2", "AVAX"), "AVALANCHE": ("avalanche-2", "AVAX"),
  487      "DOT": ("polkadot", "DOT"), "POLKADOT": ("polkadot", "DOT"),
  488      "LINK": ("chainlink", "LINK"), "CHAINLINK": ("chainlink", "LINK"),
  489      "MATIC": ("matic-network", "MATIC"), "POLYGON": ("matic-network", "MATIC"),
  490      "UNI": ("uniswap", "UNI"), "UNISWAP": ("uniswap", "UNI"),
  491      "ATOM": ("cosmos", "ATOM"), "COSMOS": ("cosmos", "ATOM"),
  492      "LTC": ("litecoin", "LTC"), "LITECOIN": ("litecoin", "LTC"),
  493      "BCH": ("bitcoin-cash", "BCH"),
  494      "ALGO": ("algorand", "ALGO"), "ALGORAND": ("algorand", "ALGO"),
  495      "NEAR": ("near", "NEAR"),
  496      "FTM": ("fantom", "FTM"), "FANTOM": ("fantom", "FTM"),
  497      "APT": ("aptos", "APT"), "APTOS": ("aptos", "APT"),
  498      "ARB": ("arbitrum", "ARB"), "ARBITRUM": ("arbitrum", "ARB"),
  499      "OP": ("optimism", "OP"), "OPTIMISM": ("optimism", "OP"),
  500      "SUI": ("sui", "SUI"),
  501      "INJ": ("injective-protocol", "INJ"), "INJECTIVE": ("injective-protocol", "INJ"),
  502      "TIA": ("celestia", "TIA"), "CELESTIA": ("celestia", "TIA"),
  503      "SEI": ("sei-network", "SEI"),
  504      "TAO": ("bittensor", "TAO"), "BITTENSOR": ("bittensor", "TAO"),
  505      "HYPE": ("hyperliquid", "HYPE"), "HYPERLIQUID": ("hyperliquid", "HYPE"),
  506      "PEPE": ("pepe", "PEPE"),
  507      "SHIB": ("shiba-inu", "SHIB"),
  508      "WIF": ("dogwifcoin", "WIF"),
  509      "RENDER": ("render-token", "RENDER"), "RNDR": ("render-token", "RENDER"),
  510      "FET": ("fetch-ai", "FET"),
  511      "AAVE": ("aave", "AAVE"),
  512      "MKR": ("maker", "MKR"),
  513  }
  514  
  515  
  516  def _detect_coin_from_text(text: str) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
  517      """Scan text for coin mentions, return (coingecko_id, symbol) or None.
  518  
  519      Checks longer names first (e.g. 'ETHEREUM' before 'ETH') to avoid
  520      false-positive partial matches.  Uses word-boundary matching for
  521      short symbols (<=4 chars) to prevent matching 'OPTION' as 'OP'.
  522      Also handles $-prefixed tickers like $ETH, $BTC.
  523      """
  524      import re as _re
  525      upper = text.upper()
  526      for key in sorted(_COIN_CHART_MAP, key=len, reverse=True):
  527          if len(key) <= 4:
  528              # Match word boundary OR $-prefix (e.g. $ETH, $BTC)
  529              if _re.search(r'(?:\$|\b)' + _re.escape(key) + r'\b', upper):
  530                  logger.debug("Coin detected: %s → %s", key, _COIN_CHART_MAP[key])
  531                  return _COIN_CHART_MAP[key]
  532          else:
  533              if key in upper:
  534                  logger.debug("Coin detected: %s → %s", key, _COIN_CHART_MAP[key])
  535                  return _COIN_CHART_MAP[key]
  536      logger.debug("No coin detected in text: %.80s", text)
  537      return None
  538  
  539  
  540  # ── Chart asset rotation + timeframe variation ───────────────────────────────
  541  # BTC 60%, ETH 20%, SOL 20% — never same asset 3x in a row
  542  _CHART_ROTATION = [
  543      ("bitcoin", "BTC"), ("bitcoin", "BTC"), ("bitcoin", "BTC"),
  544      ("ethereum", "ETH"), ("solana", "SOL"),
  545  ]
  546  _chart_rotation_idx: int = 0
  547  _last_chart_asset: str = ""
  548  _last_chart_asset_streak: int = 0
  549  _CHART_TIMEFRAMES = [7, 7, 7, 14, 30]  # 7D most common, 14D/30D occasional
  550  
  551  
  552  def _pick_default_chart_asset() -> tuple[str, str]:
  553      """Pick next asset from rotation, enforcing max 2 consecutive repeats."""
  554      global _chart_rotation_idx, _last_chart_asset, _last_chart_asset_streak
  555      for _ in range(len(_CHART_ROTATION)):
  556          coin_id, symbol = _CHART_ROTATION[_chart_rotation_idx % len(_CHART_ROTATION)]
  557          _chart_rotation_idx += 1
  558          if symbol == _last_chart_asset and _last_chart_asset_streak >= 2:
  559              continue  # skip — would be 3rd in a row
  560          if symbol == _last_chart_asset:
  561              _last_chart_asset_streak += 1
  562          else:
  563              _last_chart_asset = symbol
  564              _last_chart_asset_streak = 1
  565          return coin_id, symbol
  566      # Fallback if all skipped (shouldn't happen)
  567      _last_chart_asset = "BTC"
  568      _last_chart_asset_streak = 1
  569      return "bitcoin", "BTC"
  570  
  571  
  572  def _chart_for_tweet(
  573      tweet_text: str,
  574      coin_id: str | None = None,
  575      symbol: str | None = None,
  576  ) -> str | None:
  577      """Pick the right chart based on tweet content keywords.
  578  
  579      Falls back to a branded text card if chart generation fails.
  580      """
  581      # Explicit coin passed (price alerts, trending)
  582      if coin_id and symbol:
  583          logger.debug("Chart request: explicit coin_id=%s symbol=%s", coin_id, symbol)
  584          chart = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(coin_id, symbol, 1)
  585          if chart:
  586              logger.info("Chart generated for %s: %s", symbol, chart)
  587              return chart
  588          logger.warning("Chart generation failed for %s/%s — posting text-only", symbol, coin_id)
  589          return None
  590  
  591      # Macro/geopolitical tweets get bar chart
  592      if _MACRO_RE.search(tweet_text):
  593          return chart_generator.generate_bar_change()
  594  
  595      # Auto-detect coin from tweet text — 40% chance to use rotated asset instead
  596      detected = _detect_coin_from_text(tweet_text)
  597      if detected and random.random() >= 0.40:
  598          days = random.choice(_CHART_TIMEFRAMES)
  599          chart = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(detected[0], detected[1], days)
  600          if chart:
  601              logger.info("Chart generated for %s (%dd): %s", detected[1], days, chart)
  602              return chart
  603          logger.warning("Chart generation failed for %s — posting text-only", detected[1])
  604          return None
  605  
  606      # Default: rotated asset with varied timeframe
  607      coin_id, symbol = _pick_default_chart_asset()
  608      days = random.choice(_CHART_TIMEFRAMES)
  609      chart = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(coin_id, symbol, days)
  610      if chart:
  611          logger.info("Chart generated for %s (%dd, rotated): %s", symbol, days, chart)
  612          return chart
  613      logger.warning("%s chart failed — posting text-only", symbol)
  614      return None
  615  
  616  
  617  # ── Jobs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  618  
  619  _PRICE_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECS = 5400  # 90 minutes between price alerts
  620  _last_price_alert_time: float = 0.0
  621  _last_price_alert_by_coin: dict[str, float] = {}  # per-coin 90-min cooldown
  622  
  623  
  624  def run_price_check() -> None:
  625      global _last_price_alert_time
  626      if state.get_daily_count("price_alert") >= config.PRICE_ALERT_DAILY_CAP:
  627          logger.debug("Price alert daily cap reached — skipping check.")
  628          return
  629      now = time.time()
  630      if _last_price_alert_time > 0 and (now - _last_price_alert_time) < _PRICE_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECS:
  631          mins_left = int((_PRICE_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECS - (now - _last_price_alert_time)) / 60)
  632          logger.debug("Price alert cooldown — %dm left.", mins_left)
  633          return
  634      logger.info("Running price check…")
  635      alerts = price_monitor.check_prices()
  636      if not alerts:
  637          logger.info("No significant price moves.")
  638          return
  639      for alert in alerts:
  640          if state.get_daily_count("price_alert") >= config.PRICE_ALERT_DAILY_CAP:
  641              logger.info("Price alert daily cap (%d) reached.", config.PRICE_ALERT_DAILY_CAP)
  642              break
  643          coin_id = alert["coin_id"]
  644          last_coin_time = _last_price_alert_by_coin.get(coin_id, 0.0)
  645          if last_coin_time > 0 and (now - last_coin_time) < _PRICE_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECS:
  646              mins_left = int((_PRICE_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECS - (now - last_coin_time)) / 60)
  647              logger.debug("Price alert coin cooldown for %s — %dm left.", alert["symbol"], mins_left)
  648              continue
  649          tweet = ai_writer.generate_price_alert_tweet(alert)
  650          if not tweet:
  651              continue
  652          logger.info("Price alert: %s %+.1f%%", alert["symbol"], alert["pct_change"])
  653          chart_path: str | None = None
  654          if random.random() < 0.5:
  655              try:
  656                  chart_path = _chart_for_tweet(tweet, coin_id=coin_id, symbol=alert["symbol"])
  657              except Exception as exc:
  658                  logger.warning("Price alert chart generation failed: %s", exc)
  659          posted = _emit(tweet, tweet_type="price_alert", media_path=chart_path)
  660          if posted:
  661              _last_price_alert_time = time.time()
  662              _last_price_alert_by_coin[coin_id] = _last_price_alert_time
  663          time.sleep(3)
  664  
  665  
  666  _last_news_emit_time: float = 0.0
  667  
  668  
  669  def _news_chart_coin(story: dict) -> tuple[str, str]:
  670      """Return (coin_id, symbol) for the chart that best fits the story."""
  671      text = story.get("title", "") + " " + story.get("url", "")
  672      detected = _detect_coin_from_text(text)
  673      if detected:
  674          return detected
  675      return "bitcoin", "BTC"
  676  
  677  
  678  def run_news_check() -> None:
  679      global _last_news_emit_time
  680      if state.get_daily_count("news") >= config.NEWS_DAILY_CAP:
  681          logger.debug("News daily cap reached — skipping check.")
  682          return
  683      now = time.time()
  684      if _last_news_emit_time > 0 and (now - _last_news_emit_time) < config.NEWS_COOLDOWN_SECS:
  685          mins_left = int((config.NEWS_COOLDOWN_SECS - (now - _last_news_emit_time)) / 60)
  686          logger.debug("News cooldown — %dm left.", mins_left)
  687          return
  688      logger.info("Running news check…")
  689      stories = news_monitor.check_news()
  690      if not stories:
  691          logger.info("No new stories.")
  692          return
  693      is_weekend = datetime.datetime.now().weekday() >= 5
  694      effective_cap = config.NEWS_DAILY_CAP + 4 if is_weekend else config.NEWS_DAILY_CAP
  695      for story in stories[:1]:   # max 1 per check-cycle (cap enforced across day)
  696          if state.get_daily_count("news") >= effective_cap:
  697              logger.info("News daily cap (%d) reached.", effective_cap)
  698              break
  699          # Score + generate commentary if not already done
  700          scored = news_monitor._ai_score_and_comment(story) if "score" not in story else story
  701          if scored is None:
  702              logger.debug("_ai_score_and_comment returned None for: %.60s", story['title'])
  703              continue
  704  
  705          # Feed into narrative clustering (all scored stories, regardless of score)
  706          news_monitor.feed_narrative(scored)
  707  
  708          # Skip low-quality stories — only post score 6+
  709          if scored.get("score", 0) < 7:
  710              logger.debug("Score %d too low (need 6+): %.60s",
  711                           scored.get("score", 0), scored.get("title", ""))
  712              continue
  713  
  714          # Geo/macro breaking news — single Claude tweet + branded dark graphic
  715          if (
  716              news_monitor.is_geo_macro_story(scored)
  717              and scored.get("score", 0) >= 6
  718              and state.get_daily_count("geo_news") < 3
  719          ):
  720              geo_tweet = None
  721              for attempt in range(_MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES):
  722                  geo_tweet = ai_writer.generate_geo_tweet(scored)
  723                  if not geo_tweet:
  724                      continue
  725                  lines = [l.strip() for l in geo_tweet.strip().split("\n") if l.strip()]
  726                  has_banned = any(re.search(p, geo_tweet.lower()) for p in _BANNED_TWEET_PATTERNS)
  727                  valid_structure = len(lines) == 3 and "→" in lines[2]
  728                  if valid_structure and not has_banned:
  729                      logger.info("Geo tweet passed validation")
  730                      break
  731                  if len(lines) != 3:
  732                      logger.warning("Geo tweet has %d lines, expected 3 — retrying (attempt %d)", len(lines), attempt + 1)
  733                  elif not lines[2].startswith("→"):
  734                      logger.warning("Geo tweet missing → line — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
  735                  if has_banned:
  736                      logger.warning("Geo tweet banned phrase detected — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
  737                  geo_tweet = None
  738              if geo_tweet:
  739                  _gc_id, _gc_sym = _news_chart_coin(scored)
  740                  chart_path: str | None = None
  741                  if random.random() < 0.5:
  742                      logger.info("[CHART] Geo news chart: %s", _gc_sym)
  743                      try:
  744                          chart_path = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(_gc_id, _gc_sym, 7)
  745                      except Exception as exc:
  746                          logger.warning("Geo chart generation failed: %s", exc)
  747                  logger.info("Geo news (score %d): %.80s",
  748                              scored.get("score", 0), scored.get("title", ""))
  749                  posted = _emit(geo_tweet, tweet_type="geo_news", media_path=chart_path)
  750                  if posted:
  751                      _last_news_emit_time = time.time()
  752                  time.sleep(3)
  753                  continue
  754  
  755          # Macro/geopolitical stories get a 3-tweet thread + BTC chart
  756          if news_monitor._is_macro_source(scored):
  757              tweets = ai_writer.generate_geopolitical_tweet(scored)
  758              if not tweets:
  759                  continue
  760              img_path: str | None = None
  761              _nc_id, _nc_sym = _news_chart_coin(scored)
  762              logger.info("[CHART] News chart: %s", _nc_sym)
  763              try:
  764                  img_path = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(_nc_id, _nc_sym, 7)
  765              except Exception as exc:
  766                  logger.warning("News chart generation failed for geo thread: %s", exc)
  767              logger.info("Geo thread (score %d): %.80s",
  768                          scored.get("score", 0), scored.get("title", ""))
  769              if DRY_RUN:
  770                  img_note = f"  [image: {img_path}]" if img_path else ""
  771                  print(f"\n{'─'*60}\n[DRY RUN] [geo-thread]{img_note}")
  772                  for i, t in enumerate(tweets, 1):
  773                      print(f"  [{i}] {t}")
  774                  print("─" * 60)
  775                  posted = True
  776              else:
  777                  posted = _post_thread_with_retry(tweets, first_tweet_image_path=img_path)
  778              if posted:
  779                  state.record_tweet("news")
  780                  _last_news_emit_time = time.time()
  781              time.sleep(3)
  782              continue
  783  
  784          # Quote-style tweet for high-score stories with a direct power quote
  785          if (
  786              scored.get("score", 0) >= 8
  787              and ai_writer.story_has_power_quote(scored)
  788          ):
  789              quote_tweet = ai_writer.generate_quote_style_tweet(scored)
  790              if quote_tweet:
  791                  img_path: str | None = None
  792                  _nc_id, _nc_sym = _news_chart_coin(scored)
  793                  logger.info("[CHART] News chart: %s", _nc_sym)
  794                  try:
  795                      img_path = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(_nc_id, _nc_sym, 7)
  796                  except Exception as exc:
  797                      logger.warning("News chart generation failed for quote tweet: %s", exc)
  798                  logger.info("Quote tweet (score %d): %.80s",
  799                              scored.get("score", 0), scored.get("title", ""))
  800                  posted = _emit(quote_tweet, tweet_type="news", media_path=img_path)
  801                  if posted:
  802                      _last_news_emit_time = time.time()
  803                  time.sleep(3)
  804                  continue
  805  
  806          is_high_conviction = scored.get("score", 0) >= 8
  807          tweet = ai_writer.generate_news_tweet(scored, high_conviction=is_high_conviction)
  808          if not tweet:
  809              tweet = news_monitor.format_news_tweet(scored)
  810          if not tweet:
  811              continue
  812          img_path: str | None = None
  813          if random.random() < 0.5:
  814              _nc_id, _nc_sym = _news_chart_coin(scored)
  815              logger.info("[CHART] News chart: %s", _nc_sym)
  816              try:
  817                  img_path = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(_nc_id, _nc_sym, 7)
  818              except Exception as exc:
  819                  logger.warning("News chart generation failed: %s", exc)
  820          logger.info("News (score %d): %.80s",
  821                      scored.get("score", 0), scored.get("title", ""))
  822          posted = _emit(tweet, tweet_type="news", media_path=img_path)
  823          if posted:
  824              _last_news_emit_time = time.time()
  825          time.sleep(3)
  826  
  827  
  828  _BOT_START_TIME: float = 0.0      # set in main() before entering the loop
  829  _last_trending_run: float = 0.0   # set in main(); guards 2h min gap between trending runs
  830  _trending_coin_cooldown: dict[str, float] = {}  # coin_id → timestamp, 6h per coin
  831  
  832  
  833  def run_trending_check() -> None:
  834      """Post about a trending coin outside our main watchlist. Max 4/day.
  835  
  836      Rate-limited to at most once per 2 hours, plus a 6-hour per-coin cooldown
  837      to prevent the same trending coin from appearing twice.
  838      """
  839      global _last_trending_run
  840      if state.get_daily_count("trending") >= config.TRENDING_DAILY_CAP:
  841          logger.debug("Trending daily cap reached — skipping check.")
  842          return
  843      now = time.time()
  844      if now - _last_trending_run < 7200:
  845          logger.debug(
  846              "Trending rate limit — %.0fm elapsed since last run (min 120m).",
  847              (now - _last_trending_run) / 60,
  848          )
  849          return
  850      _last_trending_run = now
  851      logger.info("Running trending check…")
  852      alerts = trending_monitor.check_trending()
  853      if not alerts:
  854          logger.info("No trending alerts.")
  855          return
  856      # Pick the first alert that isn't on per-coin cooldown
  857      alert = None
  858      for a in alerts:
  859          coin_id = a.get("id", a.get("symbol", ""))
  860          last_coin = _trending_coin_cooldown.get(coin_id, 0.0)
  861          if last_coin > 0 and (now - last_coin) < 21600:  # 6 hours
  862              logger.debug("Trending coin %s still on cooldown — skipping.", a.get("symbol"))
  863              continue
  864          alert = a
  865          break
  866      if not alert:
  867          logger.info("All trending coins on cooldown.")
  868          return
  869      tweet = trending_monitor.format_trending_tweet(alert)
  870      if tweet:
  871          img_path: str | None = None
  872          try:
  873              img_path = _chart_for_tweet(tweet, coin_id=alert["id"], symbol=alert["symbol"])
  874          except Exception as exc:
  875              logger.warning("Trending chart generation failed: %s", exc)
  876          logger.info("Trending: %s (%s, rank #%s)",
  877                      alert["symbol"], alert["source"],
  878                      alert.get("market_cap_rank", "?"))
  879          posted = _emit(tweet, tweet_type="trending", media_path=img_path)
  880          if posted:
  881              _trending_coin_cooldown[alert.get("id", alert.get("symbol", ""))] = now
  882  
  883  
  884  def run_volume_anomaly() -> None:
  885      """Scan for volume anomalies every 2 hours. Only post if anomaly detected."""
  886      if state.get_daily_count("volume_anomaly") >= 3:
  887          logger.debug("Volume anomaly daily cap (3) reached — skipping.")
  888          return
  889      logger.info("[ANOMALY] Running volume anomaly scan…")
  890      anomaly = volume_anomaly_scanner.scan()
  891      if not anomaly:
  892          logger.info("[ANOMALY] No anomalies detected this cycle.")
  893          return
  894  
  895      symbol = anomaly["symbol"]
  896      logger.info("[ANOMALY] %s: %.2fx volume, %.2f%% move",
  897                  symbol, anomaly["vol_ratio"], anomaly["price_change"])
  898  
  899      tweet = ai_writer.generate_volume_anomaly_tweet(
  900          symbol=symbol,
  901          price=anomaly["price"],
  902          vol_ratio=anomaly["vol_ratio"],
  903          price_change=anomaly["price_change"],
  904      )
  905      if not tweet:
  906          logger.warning("[ANOMALY] Tweet generation failed for %s", symbol)
  907          return
  908  
  909      # Generate chart for the anomaly coin
  910      coin_map = {"BTC": "bitcoin", "ETH": "ethereum", "SOL": "solana",
  911                  "XRP": "ripple", "BNB": "binancecoin", "ADA": "cardano",
  912                  "AVAX": "avalanche-2", "DOGE": "dogecoin", "LINK": "chainlink",
  913                  "DOT": "polkadot"}
  914      coin_id = coin_map.get(symbol, "bitcoin")
  915      chart_path: str | None = None
  916      try:
  917          chart_path = chart_generator.generate_line_fill(coin_id, symbol, 7)
  918      except Exception as exc:
  919          logger.warning("[ANOMALY] Chart generation failed for %s: %s", symbol, exc)
  920  
  921      posted = _emit(tweet, tweet_type="volume_anomaly", media_path=chart_path)
  922      if posted:
  923          state.increment_daily_count("volume_anomaly")
  924          logger.info("[ANOMALY] Posted volume anomaly tweet for %s", symbol)
  925

  930          logger.info("Quote tweet daily cap (%d) reached.", config.QUOTE_TWEET_DAILY_CAP)
  931          return
  932      tweet = tweet_generators.generate_quote_tweet()
  933      if tweet:
  934          media_path: str | None = None
  935          try:
  936              media_path = _chart_for_tweet(tweet)
  937          except Exception as exc:
  938              logger.warning("Quote tweet chart generation failed: %s", exc)
  939          _emit(tweet, tweet_type="quote", media_path=media_path)

  942  def run_morning_recap() -> None:
  943      if not _should_fire("morning_recap", 8):
  944          return
  945      _mark_slot_fired("morning_recap")
  946      logger.info("Running morning recap…")
  947      tweet = tweet_generators.generate_morning_recap()
  948      logger.debug("morning recap tweet_generators result: %s", "OK" if tweet else "None")
  949      if not tweet:
  950          headlines = news_monitor.fetch_latest_headlines(3)
  951          logger.debug("morning recap headlines fallback: %d headlines", len(headlines) if headlines else 0)
  952          if headlines:
  953              tweet = ai_writer.generate_morning_recap(headlines)
  954      if tweet:
  955          chart_path = None
  956          morning_coins = tweet_generators._last_morning_coins
  957          if morning_coins:
  958              for attempt in range(3):
  959                  chart_path = chart_generator.generate_morning_recap_chart(morning_coins)
  960                  if chart_path:
  961                      break
  962                  logger.warning("Chart generation attempt %d failed, retrying...", attempt + 1)
  963                  time.sleep(5)
  964              if not chart_path:
  965                  logger.warning("Morning recap chart failed after 3 attempts — trying bar_change fallback")
  966                  try:
  967                      chart_path = chart_generator.generate_bar_change()
  968                  except Exception as exc:
  969                      logger.warning("Bar change fallback also failed: %s", exc)
  970                  if not chart_path:
  971                      logger.warning("All chart fallbacks exhausted, posting without image")
  972          _emit(tweet, bypass_guard=True, tweet_type="morning_recap", media_path=chart_path)
  973      else:
  974          logger.warning("Morning recap generation failed — will retry next minute.")
  975

  977  def run_opinion_tweet() -> None:
  978      if not _should_fire("opinion", 12):
  979          return
  980      _mark_slot_fired("opinion")  # mark BEFORE posting to prevent any duplicate
  981      logger.info("Running opinion tweet (12:00)…")
  982      tweet = None
  983      for attempt in range(_MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES):
  984          tweet = tweet_generators.generate_opinion_tweet()
  985          if not tweet:
  986              continue
  987          lines = [l.strip() for l in tweet.strip().split("\n") if l.strip()]
  988          has_banned = any(re.search(p, tweet.lower()) for p in _BANNED_TWEET_PATTERNS)
  989          valid_structure = len(lines) >= 1
  990          if valid_structure and not has_banned:
  991              logger.info("Opinion tweet passed validation")
  992              break
  993          if len(lines) < 1:
  994              logger.warning("Opinion tweet empty — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
  995          if has_banned:
  996              logger.warning("Opinion tweet banned phrase detected — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
  997          tweet = None
  998      if tweet:
  999          media_path: str | None = None
 1000          try:
 1001              media_path = _chart_for_tweet(tweet)
 1002          except Exception as exc:
 1003              logger.warning("Opinion chart generation failed: %s", exc)
 1004          if not media_path:
 1005              time.sleep(10)
 1006              media_path = _chart_for_tweet(tweet)
 1007          _emit(tweet, bypass_guard=True, tweet_type="hot_take", media_path=media_path)
 1008      else:
 1009          logger.error("Opinion tweet failed validation after %d attempts — skipping", _MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES)

 1026  def run_engagement_tweet() -> None:
 1027      if not _should_fire("engagement", 16):
 1028          return
 1029      _mark_slot_fired("engagement")
 1030      logger.info("Running engagement tweet (16:00)…")
 1031      tweet = None
 1032      for attempt in range(_MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES):
 1033          tweet = tweet_generators.generate_engagement_tweet()
 1034          if not tweet:
 1035              continue
 1036          lines = [l.strip() for l in tweet.strip().split("\n") if l.strip()]
 1037          has_banned = any(re.search(p, tweet.lower()) for p in _BANNED_TWEET_PATTERNS)
 1038          valid_structure = len(lines) >= 1
 1039          if valid_structure and not has_banned:
 1040              logger.info("Engagement tweet passed validation")
 1041              break
 1042          if len(lines) < 1:
 1043              logger.warning("Engagement tweet empty — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
 1044          if has_banned:
 1045              logger.warning("Engagement tweet banned phrase detected — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
 1046          tweet = None

 1048          media_path: str | None = None
 1049          try:

 1058          logger.error("Engagement tweet failed validation after %d attempts — skipping", _MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES)

 1061  def run_market_open() -> None:
 1062      if not _should_fire("market_open", 9, minute=30):
 1063          return
 1064      _mark_slot_fired("market_open")
 1065      logger.info("Running market open tweet (09:30)…")
 1066      btc = tweet_generators._fetch_binance_coin("bitcoin")
 1067      eth = tweet_generators._fetch_binance_coin("ethereum")
 1068      if not btc:
 1069          logger.warning("Market open: no BTC data from Binance — skipping.")
 1070          return
 1071      btc_price = float(btc.get("current_price", 0))
 1072      btc_pct = float(btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h", 0))
 1073      eth_price = float(eth.get("current_price", 0)) if eth else 0.0
 1074      eth_pct = float(eth.get("price_change_percentage_24h", 0)) if eth else 0.0
 1075  
 1076      tweet = ai_writer.generate_market_open_tweet(
 1077          btc_price=btc_price,
 1078          btc_pct=btc_pct,
 1079          eth_price=eth_price,
 1080          eth_pct=eth_pct,
 1081      )
 1082  
 1083      if not tweet or len(tweet) < 20:
 1084          tweet = (
 1085              f"Range unclear — market waiting for direction.\n"
 1086              f"BTC ${btc_price:,.0f} ({btc_pct:+.1f}%) | ETH ${eth_price:,.0f} ({eth_pct:+.1f}%)\n"
 1087              f"Expansion follows compression."
 1088          )
 1089  
 1090      media_path: str | None = None
 1091      try:
 1092          media_path = _chart_for_tweet(tweet)
 1093      except Exception as exc:
 1094          logger.warning("Market open chart failed: %s", exc)
 1095      _emit(tweet, bypass_guard=True, tweet_type="market_open", media_path=media_path)

 1098  def run_rotation_check() -> None:
 1099      if not _should_fire("rotation_check", 10, minute=15):
 1100          return
 1101      _mark_slot_fired("rotation_check")
 1102      logger.info("Running 10:15 rotation check…")
 1103  
 1104      symbols = ["SOL", "BNB", "XRP", "ADA", "AVAX", "LINK", "INJ", "FET", "RENDER"]
 1105      coins = []
 1106      for symbol in symbols:
 1107          data = tweet_generators._fetch_binance_coin(symbol.lower())
 1108          if not data:
 1109              continue
 1110          coins.append({
 1111              "symbol": symbol,
 1112              "pct": float(data.get("price_change_percentage_24h", 0)),
 1113          })
 1114  
 1115      if not coins:
 1116          logger.warning("Rotation check: no coin data available — skipping.")
 1117          return
 1118  
 1119      tweet = ai_writer.generate_rotation_tweet(coins)
 1120      if not tweet:
 1121          tweet = (
 1122              "Rotation building under the surface.\n"
 1123              "Strength is not where most are looking.\n"
 1124              "Watch where momentum concentrates."
 1125          )
 1126  
 1127      _emit(tweet, bypass_guard=True, tweet_type="rotation")

 1130  def _fetch_binance_tickers() -> list[dict]:
 1131      """Fetch 24hr ticker data from Binance for the top 5 coins."""
 1132      symbols = ["BTCUSDT", "ETHUSDT", "SOLUSDT", "XRPUSDT", "BNBUSDT"]
 1133      names = {"BTCUSDT": "BTC", "ETHUSDT": "ETH", "SOLUSDT": "SOL",
 1134               "XRPUSDT": "XRP", "BNBUSDT": "BNB"}
 1135      results = []
 1136      for sym in symbols:
 1137          try:
 1138              resp = requests.get(
 1139                  "https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ticker/24hr",
 1140                  params={"symbol": sym},
 1141                  timeout=10,
 1142              )
 1143              resp.raise_for_status()
 1144              data = resp.json()
 1145              price = float(data["lastPrice"])
 1146              pct = float(data["priceChangePercent"])
 1147              results.append({"name": names[sym], "price": price, "pct": pct})
 1148          except Exception as exc:
 1149              logger.warning("Binance ticker fetch failed for %s: %s", sym, exc)
 1150      return results
 1151  
 1152  
 1153  def _build_market_check_tweet(label: str) -> str:
 1154      """Build a market check tweet from live Binance data."""
 1155      tickers = _fetch_binance_tickers()
 1156      if not tickers:
 1157          return ""
 1158      lines = []
 1159      green_count = 0
 1160      for t in tickers:
 1161          icon = "\U0001f7e2" if t["pct"] >= 0 else "\U0001f534"
 1162          sign = "+" if t["pct"] >= 0 else ""
 1163          if t["pct"] >= 0:
 1164              green_count += 1
 1165          if t["price"] >= 100:
 1166              price_str = f"${t['price']:,.0f}"
 1167          else:
 1168              price_str = f"${t['price']:.2f}"
 1169          lines.append(f"{icon} {t['name']} {price_str} ({sign}{t['pct']:.1f}%)")
 1170      total = len(tickers)
 1171  
 1172      # Get BTC price for the → level line
 1173      btc_ticker = next((t for t in tickers if t["name"] == "BTC"), None)
 1174      btc_p = btc_ticker["price"] if btc_ticker else 0
 1175  
 1176      if green_count >= 4:
 1177          summary = "Buyers running it. Broad strength across the board."
 1178          trigger = f"→ Hold ${btc_p:,.0f} and this keeps grinding higher." if btc_p else "→ Strength continues until structure breaks."
 1179      elif green_count == 3:
 1180          summary = "Split tape. No clear winner yet."
 1181          trigger = f"→ ${btc_p:,.0f} decides direction from here." if btc_p else "→ Next 4h candle decides."
 1182      elif green_count == 2:
 1183          summary = "Most of the board is red. Bounces getting sold."
 1184          trigger = f"→ Lose ${btc_p:,.0f} and downside accelerates." if btc_p else "→ Sellers stay in control until proven otherwise."
 1185      else:
 1186          summary = "Sellers dominating. No real bids showing up."
 1187          trigger = f"→ Below ${btc_p:,.0f} opens the flush." if btc_p else "→ Risk-off until proven otherwise."
 1188  
 1189      tweet = label + "\n\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n" + summary + "\n" + trigger
 1190      return tweet
 1191  
 1192  
 1193  def run_midmorning_check() -> None:
 1194      if not _should_fire("midmorning_check", 11):
 1195          return

 1199      if tweet:
 1200          _emit(tweet, bypass_guard=True, tweet_type="market_open", media_path=_chart_for_tweet(tweet))
 1201      else:
 1202          logger.warning("11:00 market check failed — skipping.")

 1217  # ── Narrative check ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1218  
 1219  _NARRATIVE_DAILY_CAP = 2
 1220  _narrative_cooldown: dict[str, float] = {}  # theme → last-posted timestamp
 1221  _NARRATIVE_COOLDOWN_SECS = 24 * 3600  # 24 hours per theme
 1222  
 1223  
 1224  def run_narrative_check() -> None:
 1225      """Check for emerging narratives every 2 hours. Max 2 per day, 24h per theme."""
 1226      if state.get_daily_count("narrative") >= _NARRATIVE_DAILY_CAP:
 1227          logger.debug("Narrative daily cap (%d) reached — skipping.", _NARRATIVE_DAILY_CAP)
 1228          return
 1229      narrative = news_monitor.check_narratives()
 1230      if not narrative:
 1231          return
 1232  
 1233      theme = narrative["theme"]
 1234  
 1235      # 24-hour per-theme cooldown
 1236      last_posted = _narrative_cooldown.get(theme, 0)
 1237      if time.time() - last_posted < _NARRATIVE_COOLDOWN_SECS:
 1238          hours_left = int((_NARRATIVE_COOLDOWN_SECS - (time.time() - last_posted)) / 3600)
 1239          logger.debug("Narrative '%s' on cooldown — %dh left.", theme, hours_left)
 1240          return
 1241  
 1242      logger.info("Emerging narrative detected: %s (%d stories, score %.1f)",
 1243                  theme, narrative["story_count"], narrative.get("narrative_score", 0))
 1244      tweet = None
 1245      for attempt in range(_MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES):
 1246          tweet = ai_writer.generate_narrative_tweet(
 1247              theme=narrative["theme"],
 1248              story_count=narrative["story_count"],
 1249              summaries=narrative["summaries"],
 1250          )
 1251          if not tweet:
 1252              continue
 1253          lines = [l.strip() for l in tweet.strip().split("\n") if l.strip()]
 1254          has_banned = any(re.search(p, tweet.lower()) for p in _BANNED_TWEET_PATTERNS)
 1255          valid_structure = len(lines) == 3
 1256          if valid_structure and not has_banned:
 1257              logger.info("Narrative tweet passed validation")
 1258              break
 1259          if len(lines) != 3:
 1260              logger.warning("Narrative tweet has %d lines, expected 3 — retrying (attempt %d)", len(lines), attempt + 1)
 1261          if has_banned:
 1262              logger.warning("Narrative tweet banned phrase detected — retrying (attempt %d)", attempt + 1)
 1263          tweet = None
 1264      if not tweet:
 1265          logger.error("Narrative tweet failed validation after %d attempts — skipping", _MAX_STRUCT_RETRIES)
 1266          return
 1267      _narrative_chart = _chart_for_tweet(tweet) if random.random() < 0.5 else None
 1268      posted = _emit(tweet, tweet_type="narrative", media_path=_narrative_chart)
 1269      if posted:
 1270          state.increment_daily_count("narrative")
 1271          _narrative_cooldown[theme] = time.time()
 1272          logger.info("Narrative tweet posted for: %s", theme)
 1273  
 1274  
 1275  
 1276  _evening_thread_topics = [
 1277      "Why Bitcoin hasn't hit $100k yet in 2026 — and what's actually holding it back",
 1278      "The real reason institutions are buying crypto right now (it's not what you think)",
 1279      "Ethereum is losing the narrative war in 2026 — here's the data",
 1280      "Why the next crypto leg up will look nothing like 2021",
 1281      "The stablecoin market just hit $170B — this is what it means for Bitcoin price",
 1282      "BlackRock's Bitcoin ETF is the most important thing to happen to crypto in a decade",
 1283      "Why most altcoins will never recover their 2021 highs",
 1284      "The Fed, inflation, and Bitcoin — how macro is driving every move right now",
 1285      "Crypto regulation is coming whether you like it or not — here's how to position",
 1286      "Why Bitcoin dominance rising is actually bearish for the broader market",
 1287      "What replaced DeFi summer — and why the new era is more important",
 1288      "Why on-chain data matters more than price action right now",
 1289      "Bitcoin post-halving economics — why this cycle is different from 2020",
 1290      "The war between CEX and DEX — who's winning in 2026 and why it matters",
 1291      "Why crypto Twitter is wrong about the current market cycle",
 1292  ]
 1293  _thread_topic_index: int = state.get_thread_topic_index()
