 1296  def run_evening_thread() -> None:
 1297      if not _should_fire("evening_thread", 19):
 1298          return
 1299      _mark_slot_fired("evening_thread")
 1300      global _thread_topic_index
 1301      topic = _evening_thread_topics[_thread_topic_index % len(_evening_thread_topics)]
 1302      _thread_topic_index += 1
 1303      state.set_thread_topic_index(_thread_topic_index)
 1304      logger.info("Running evening thread (19:00): %s", topic)
 1305      tweets = ai_writer.generate_thread(topic, n_tweets=3)
 1306      if not tweets:
 1307          logger.warning("Evening thread failed — skipping.")
 1308          return
 1309  
 1310      def _generate_chart_for_topic() -> str | None:
 1311          return _chart_for_tweet(topic)
 1312  
 1313      img_path: str | None = None
 1314      try:
 1315          for attempt in range(3):
 1316              try:
 1317                  img_path = _generate_chart_for_topic()
 1318              except Exception as e:
 1319                  logger.warning(f"Chart attempt {attempt+1} error: {e}")
 1320                  img_path = None
 1321              if img_path:
 1322                  break
 1323              logger.warning("Chart generation attempt %d failed, retrying...", attempt + 1)
 1324              time.sleep(5)
 1325          if not img_path:
 1326              logger.warning("Thread chart: None after 3 attempts, posting without image")
 1327      except Exception as exc:
 1328          logger.warning("Evening thread chart generation failed: %s", exc)
 1329  
 1330      logger.info("Thread chart: %s", img_path)
 1331  
 1332      if DRY_RUN:
 1333          img_note = f"  [chart: {img_path}]" if img_path else "  [no chart]"
 1334          print(f"\n{'─'*60}\n[DRY RUN] Evening thread ({len(tweets)} tweets){img_note}:")
 1335          for i, t in enumerate(tweets, 1):
 1336              print(f"  [{i}] {t}")
 1337          print('─'*60)
 1338      else:
 1339          ok = _post_thread_with_retry(tweets, first_tweet_image_path=img_path)
 1340          if ok:
 1341              state.record_tweet(len(tweets))
 1342              state.increment_daily_count("evening_thread", len(tweets))
 1343              logger.info("Evening thread posted (%d tweets).", len(tweets))
 1344          else:
 1345              logger.error("Evening thread failed.")
 1346  
 1347      if img_path:
 1348          try:
 1349              os.unlink(img_path)
 1350          except OSError:
 1351              pass
 1352  
 1353  
 1354  # ── Reply bot ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1355  _REPLY_ACCOUNTS = ["CoinBureau", "APompliano", "WuBlockchain", "CryptoCobain"]
 1356  _REPLY_COOLDOWN = 5400   # 90 minutes between replies
 1357  _last_reply_time: float = 0.0
 1358  
 1359  
 1360  def run_reply_check() -> None:
 1361      """Search recent tweets from target accounts and reply to the highest-engagement
 1362      one not yet replied to. Max REPLY_DAILY_CAP/day, 90-min cooldown between replies."""
 1363      global _last_reply_time
 1364  
 1365      if state.get_daily_count("reply") >= config.REPLY_DAILY_CAP:
 1366          logger.debug("Reply daily cap (%d) reached — skipping.", config.REPLY_DAILY_CAP)
 1367          return
 1368  
 1369      now = time.time()
 1370      if _last_reply_time > 0 and (now - _last_reply_time) < _REPLY_COOLDOWN:
 1371          mins_left = int((_REPLY_COOLDOWN - (now - _last_reply_time)) / 60)
 1372          logger.debug("Reply cooldown — %dm left.", mins_left)
 1373          return
 1374  
 1375      replied_ids = state.get_replied_ids()
 1376      candidates: list[dict] = []
 1377  
 1378      for account in _REPLY_ACCOUNTS:
 1379          tweets = twitter_client.search_recent_tweets(
 1380              query=f"from:{account} -is:retweet -is:reply",
 1381              max_results=10,
 1382          )
 1383          for tweet in tweets:
 1384              if tweet["id"] not in replied_ids:
 1385                  candidates.append(tweet)
 1386  
 1387      if not candidates:
 1388          logger.info("Reply check: no new tweets from target accounts.")
 1389          return
 1390  
 1391      # Pick highest engagement (likes + retweets)
 1392      best = max(candidates, key=lambda t: t["like_count"] + t["retweet_count"])
 1393      logger.info("Reply target (likes=%d, rts=%d): %.80s",
 1394                  best["like_count"], best["retweet_count"], best["text"])
 1395  
 1396      reply = ai_writer.generate_reply(best["text"])
 1397      if not reply:
 1398          logger.warning("Reply generation failed — skipping.")
 1399          return
 1400  
 1401      posted = twitter_client.post_tweet(reply, in_reply_to_tweet_id=best["id"])
 1402      if posted:
 1403          state.add_replied_id(best["id"])
 1404          state.increment_daily_count("reply")
 1405          _last_reply_time = time.time()
 1406          logger.info("Reply posted to tweet %s: %.80s", best["id"], reply)
 1407      else:
 1408          logger.warning("Reply post failed for tweet %s.", best["id"])
 1409  
 1410  
 1411  def run_fear_greed_tweet() -> None:
 1412      if not _should_fire("fear_greed", 21):
 1413          return
 1414      _mark_slot_fired("fear_greed")
 1415      logger.info("Running 21:00 Fear & Greed tweet…")
 1416      data = fear_greed.fetch_fear_greed()
 1417      if not data:
 1418          logger.warning("Fear & Greed fetch failed — skipping.")
 1419          return
 1420      if not fear_greed.should_post(data["value"]):
 1421          logger.info("Fear & Greed: cooldown or duplicate value — skipping.")
 1422          return
 1423      result = fear_greed.format_fear_greed_tweet(data)
 1424      if not result:
 1425          logger.warning("Fear & Greed formatting failed — skipping.")
 1426          return
 1427      tweet, img_path = result
 1428      posted = _emit(tweet, bypass_guard=True, tweet_type="fear_greed", media_path=img_path)
 1429      if posted:
 1430          fear_greed.record_posted(data)
 1431  
 1432  
 1433  # ── Scheduler ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1434  _schedule_configured: bool = False
 1435  
 1436  
 1437  def setup_schedule() -> None:
 1438      global _schedule_configured
 1439      if _schedule_configured:
 1440          logger.warning("setup_schedule() called more than once — ignoring duplicate.")
 1441          return
 1442      _schedule_configured = True
 1443  
 1444      # Interval-driven jobs — each wrapped in _safe so one failure can't kill the loop
 1445      _scheduler.every(5).minutes.do(_safe(run_price_check))
 1446      _scheduler.every(15).minutes.do(_safe(run_news_check))
 1447      # _scheduler.every(30).minutes.do(_safe(run_reply_check))
 1448      _scheduler.every(2).hours.do(_safe(run_volume_anomaly))
 1449      _scheduler.every(2).hours.do(_safe(run_quote_tweet))
 1450      _scheduler.every(2).hours.do(_safe(run_narrative_check))
 1451      # _scheduler.every(5).minutes.do(_safe(reply_engine.check_and_reply))
 1452  
 1453      # Time-of-day jobs (checked every minute; _should_fire enforces once/day)
 1454      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_morning_recap))
 1455      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_market_open))
 1456      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_rotation_check))
 1457      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_midmorning_check))
 1458      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_opinion_tweet))
 1459      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_opinion_bomb))
 1460      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_afternoon_take))
 1461      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_engagement_tweet))
 1462      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_evening_thread))
 1463      _scheduler.every(1).minutes.do(_safe(run_fear_greed_tweet))
 1464  
 1465      n_jobs = len(_scheduler.get_jobs())
 1466      logger.info(
 1467          "Scheduled %d jobs: price/5m | news/15m | anomaly/2h | quote/2h | "
 1468          "08:00 recap | 09:30 market-open | 11:00 midmorning | 12:00 opinion | "
 1469          "13:00 opinion_bomb | 14:00 afternoon | 16:00 engagement | 19:00 thread | "
 1470          "21:00 fear-greed  (UK time)",
 1471          n_jobs,
 1472      )
 1473  
 1474  
 1475  # ── Graceful shutdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1476  def _shutdown(signum, frame):  # noqa: ARG001
 1477      logger.info("Received signal %d – shutting down.", signum)
 1478      sys.exit(0)
 1479  
 1480  
 1481  signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _shutdown)
 1482  signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,  _shutdown)
 1483  
 1484  
 1485  
 1486  # ── Main ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1487  def main() -> None:
 1488      global DRY_RUN, _BOT_START_TIME, _last_trending_run
 1489  
 1490      # ── Single-instance guard (pid file) ───────────────────────────────────────
 1491      _LOCK_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "bot.pid")
 1492      if os.path.exists(_LOCK_FILE):
 1493          try:
 1494              with open(_LOCK_FILE) as f:
 1495                  old_pid_str = f.read().strip()
 1496              if old_pid_str:
 1497                  old_pid = int(old_pid_str)
 1498                  os.kill(old_pid, 0)  # signal 0 = check existence
 1499                  # If we reach here, the process is alive (or permission denied = also alive)
 1500                  logger.critical("Bot already running (PID %d). Exiting to prevent duplicates.", old_pid)
 1501                  print(f"ERROR: Bot already running (PID {old_pid}). Exiting.")
 1502                  sys.exit(1)
 1503          except ProcessLookupError:
 1504              logger.info("Stale bot.pid (PID %s not running) — taking over.", old_pid_str)
 1505          except PermissionError:
 1506              # os.kill raises PermissionError if PID exists but belongs to another user
 1507              logger.critical("Bot PID %s is alive (owned by another user). Exiting.", old_pid_str)
 1508              print(f"ERROR: Bot PID {old_pid_str} is alive (permission denied). Exiting.")
 1509              sys.exit(1)
 1510          except (ValueError, OSError):
 1511              logger.info("Invalid or stale bot.pid — overwriting.")
 1512      with open(_LOCK_FILE, "w") as f:
 1513          f.write(str(os.getpid()))
 1514      logger.info("PID lock acquired: %d → %s", os.getpid(), _LOCK_FILE)
 1515      import atexit
 1516      def _cleanup_pid():
 1517          try:
 1518              with open(_LOCK_FILE) as f:
 1519                  if f.read().strip() == str(os.getpid()):
 1520                      os.unlink(_LOCK_FILE)
 1521          except OSError:
 1522              pass
 1523      atexit.register(_cleanup_pid)
 1524  
 1525      parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Crypto News Twitter Bot")
 1526      parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
 1527                          help="Print tweets instead of posting")
 1528      parser.add_argument("--preview", action="store_true",
 1529                          help="Generate and print tonight's evening thread, then exit")
 1530      args = parser.parse_args()
 1531      DRY_RUN = args.dry_run
 1532  
 1533      if args.preview:
 1534          topic = _evening_thread_topics[_thread_topic_index % len(_evening_thread_topics)]
 1535          print(f"\nEvening thread preview — topic: {topic}\n{'─'*60}")
 1536          tweets = ai_writer.generate_thread(topic, n_tweets=3)
 1537          if not tweets:
 1538              print("Failed to generate thread (is ANTHROPIC_API_KEY set?)")
 1539              sys.exit(1)
 1540          for i, t in enumerate(tweets, 1):
 1541              print(f"\n[{i}] {t}")
 1542          print(f"\n{'─'*60}")
 1543          sys.exit(0)
 1544      _BOT_START_TIME = time.time()
 1545      _last_trending_run = _BOT_START_TIME  # first trending window starts now (2h grace)
 1546  
 1547      # ── Logging setup ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1548      # Always write to the log file.
 1549      # Only attach a StreamHandler when stdout is an interactive terminal OR in
 1550      # dry-run mode, so that `nohup python3 bot.py >> bot.log 2>&1` does
 1551      # not write every line twice (once from FileHandler, once from the stdout
 1552      # redirect hitting the same file).
 1553      _fmt = "%(asctime)s  %(levelname)-8s  %(name)s  %(message)s"
 1554      _handlers: list[logging.Handler] = [logging.FileHandler(config.LOG_FILE)]
 1555      if DRY_RUN or sys.stdout.isatty():
 1556          _handlers.append(logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout))
 1557      logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=_fmt, handlers=_handlers)
 1558  
 1559      if DRY_RUN:
 1560          logger.info("DRY RUN mode — no tweets will be posted.")
 1561  
 1562      logger.info("Crypto bot starting up…")
 1563  
 1564      if not DRY_RUN:
 1565          try:
 1566              twitter_client.get_client()
 1567              logger.info("Twitter credentials OK.")
 1568          except RuntimeError as exc:
 1569              logger.critical("Cannot start: %s", exc)
 1570              sys.exit(1)
 1571  
 1572      # Telegram startup ping
 1573      try:
 1574          if config.TELEGRAM_ENABLED and not DRY_RUN:
 1575              telegram_client.send_telegram("🤖 CoinWatchAlert bot started")
 1576              logger.info("Telegram startup message sent.")
 1577      except Exception as exc:
 1578          logger.warning("Telegram startup ping failed (non-fatal): %s", exc)
 1579  
 1580      setup_schedule()
 1581      jobs = _scheduler.get_jobs()
 1582      logger.info("Scheduler: %d jobs registered. Listing all:", len(jobs))
 1583      for i, job in enumerate(jobs, 1):
 1584          logger.info("  [%d] %s", i, job)
 1585      if len(jobs) != len(set(str(j) for j in jobs)):
 1586          logger.warning("DUPLICATE JOBS DETECTED — check setup_schedule()")
 1587  
 1588      # Immediate startup checks — _scheduler.every() fires AFTER the interval,
 1589      # so these are the only same-cycle executions (no duplicate firing).
 1590      try:
 1591          run_price_check()
 1592      except Exception:
 1593          logger.exception("Startup price check raised — continuing.")
 1594  
 1595      try:
 1596          run_news_check()
 1597      except Exception:
 1598          logger.exception("Startup news check raised — continuing.")
 1599  
 1600      logger.info("Entering main loop (Ctrl-C or SIGTERM to stop).")
 1601      try:
 1602          while True:
 1603              try:
 1604                  _scheduler.run_pending()
 1605              except Exception:
 1606                  logger.exception("Unexpected error inside run_pending — continuing.")
 1607              time.sleep(10)
 1608      except Exception:
 1609          logger.exception("Fatal unhandled exception — bot is stopping.")
 1610          sys.exit(1)
 1611  
 1612  
 1613  if __name__ == "__main__":
 1614      main()


================================================================================
  FILE: breakout_monitor.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Breakout monitor – detects when BTC crosses key psychological/technical levels.
    3  
    4  Checks every 5 minutes and fires alerts when price crosses significant round
    5  numbers or historically important levels. Cooldown prevents spam.
    6  """
    7  from __future__ import annotations
    8  
    9  import logging
   10  import time
   11  
   12  import requests
   13  
   14  import config
   15  import ai_writer
   16  
   17  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   18  
   19  # Key levels to watch (updated dynamically based on price range)
   20  _BTC_KEY_LEVELS = [
   21      50000, 55000, 60000, 62000, 64000, 65000, 66000, 67000, 68000,
   22      69000, 70000, 72000, 75000, 80000, 85000, 90000, 95000, 100000,
   23      105000, 110000, 120000, 125000, 150000,
   24  ]
   25  
   26  # ETH key levels
   27  _ETH_KEY_LEVELS = [
   28      1500, 1600, 1700, 1800, 1900, 2000, 2100, 2200, 2500, 3000,
   29      3500, 4000, 4500, 5000,
   30  ]
   31  
   32  # SOL key levels
   33  _SOL_KEY_LEVELS = [
   34      100, 120, 130, 140, 150, 160, 170, 180, 190, 200, 225, 250, 300,
   35  ]
   36  
   37  _LEVEL_MAP = {
   38      "bitcoin": _BTC_KEY_LEVELS,
   39      "ethereum": _ETH_KEY_LEVELS,
   40      "solana": _SOL_KEY_LEVELS,
   41  }
   42  
   43  _SYMBOL_MAP = {
   44      "bitcoin": "BTC",
   45      "ethereum": "ETH",
   46      "solana": "SOL",
   47  }
   48  
   49  # Track last known price and cooldowns
   50  _last_prices: dict[str, float] = {}
   51  _level_cooldowns: dict[str, float] = {}  # "coin:level" -> timestamp
   52  
   53  
   54  def _fetch_current_prices() -> dict[str, float]:
   55      """Fetch current prices for BTC, ETH, SOL."""
   56      try:
   57          resp = requests.get(
   58              f"{config.COINGECKO_BASE}/simple/price",
   59              params={
   60                  "ids": "bitcoin,ethereum,solana",
   61                  "vs_currencies": "usd",
   62              },
   63              timeout=10,
   64          )
   65          resp.raise_for_status()
   66          data = resp.json()
   67          return {
   68              coin: data[coin]["usd"]
   69              for coin in data
   70              if "usd" in data[coin]
   71          }
   72      except (requests.RequestException, KeyError) as exc:
   73          logger.warning("Breakout price fetch failed: %s", exc)
   74          return {}
   75  
   76  
   77  def _cooldown_ok(coin_id: str, level: int) -> bool:
   78      """Check if a level alert is off cooldown."""
   79      key = f"{coin_id}:{level}"
   80      last = _level_cooldowns.get(key, 0)
   81      return (time.time() - last) >= config.BREAKOUT_COOLDOWN
   82  
   83  
   84  def _record_level(coin_id: str, level: int) -> None:
   85      _level_cooldowns[f"{coin_id}:{level}"] = time.time()
   86  
   87  
   88  def check_breakouts() -> list[dict]:
   89      """
   90      Check if any tracked coins have crossed key levels since last check.
   91      Returns list of breakout alert dicts.
   92      """
   93      prices = _fetch_current_prices()
   94      if not prices:
   95          return []
   96  
   97      alerts = []
   98  
   99      for coin_id, current_price in prices.items():
  100          levels = _LEVEL_MAP.get(coin_id, [])
  101          symbol = _SYMBOL_MAP.get(coin_id, coin_id.upper())
  102          last_price = _last_prices.get(coin_id)
  103  
  104          if last_price is None:
  105              _last_prices[coin_id] = current_price
  106              continue
  107  
  108          for level in levels:
  109              # Check if price crossed this level (in either direction)
  110              crossed_up = last_price < level <= current_price
  111              crossed_down = last_price > level >= current_price
  112  
  113              if (crossed_up or crossed_down) and _cooldown_ok(coin_id, level):
  114                  direction = "above" if crossed_up else "below"
  115                  alerts.append({
  116                      "coin_id": coin_id,
  117                      "symbol": symbol,
  118                      "level": level,
  119                      "current_price": current_price,
  120                      "direction": direction,
  121                      "crossed_up": crossed_up,
  122                  })
  123                  _record_level(coin_id, level)
  124  
  125          _last_prices[coin_id] = current_price
  126  
  127      return alerts
  128  
  129  
  130  def format_breakout_tweet(alert: dict) -> str | None:
  131      """Generate a tweet about a key level breakout."""
  132      symbol = alert["symbol"]
  133      level = alert["level"]
  134      price = alert["current_price"]
  135      direction = alert["direction"]
  136      crossed_up = alert["crossed_up"]
  137  
  138      emoji = "🟢" if crossed_up else "🔴"
  139      action = "broke above" if crossed_up else "dropped below"
  140  
  141      # Format level nicely
  142      if level >= 1000:
  143          level_str = f"${level:,.0f}"
  144      else:
  145          level_str = f"${level}"
  146  
  147      price_str = f"${price:,.0f}" if price >= 1000 else f"${price:,.2f}"
  148  
  149      if ai_writer.is_available():
  150          prompt = f"""Write a BREAKING tweet — {symbol} just {action} {level_str}.
  151  
  152  Current price: {price_str}
  153  Direction: {'bullish breakout' if crossed_up else 'bearish breakdown'}
  154  Key level: {level_str}
  155  
  156  The tweet should:
  157  - Lead with the level break — this is urgent, time-sensitive
  158  - Say what the next level to watch is (pick a reasonable one)
  159  - Sound like a trader calling it out in real-time
  160  - Short and punchy — under 250 characters
  161  - NO hashtags
  162  {ai_writer._get_recent_context()}
  163  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  164  
  165          system = """You are @CoinWatchAlert. When price breaks a key level, you're the first to call it. Sharp, urgent, data-driven. No hashtags."""
  166  
  167          tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  168          if tweet and len(tweet) <= 275:
  169              return tweet
  170  
  171      # Template fallback
  172      next_levels = {
  173          True: [l for l in _LEVEL_MAP.get(alert["coin_id"], []) if l > level],
  174          False: [l for l in _LEVEL_MAP.get(alert["coin_id"], []) if l < level],
  175      }
  176      next_list = next_levels[crossed_up]
  177      next_level = next_list[0] if next_list else None
  178  
  179      lines = [
  180          f"⚡ {symbol} just {action} {level_str}",
  181          f"",
  182          f"{emoji} Currently at {price_str}",
  183      ]
  184  
  185      if next_level:
  186          next_str = f"${next_level:,.0f}" if next_level >= 1000 else f"${next_level}"
  187          watching = "resistance" if crossed_up else "support"
  188          lines.extend(["", f"🎯 Next {watching}: {next_str}"])
  189  
  190      return "\n".join(lines)

  FILE: chart_generator.py

    1  """
    2  Chart generator – creates varied price chart images for tweets.
    3  
    4  Uses matplotlib to generate multiple chart styles so the feed never
    5  posts the same look twice in a row.
    6  
    7  Chart styles (6 types, rotated):
    8    1. line_fill     – classic line with gradient fill (original style)
    9    2. candlestick   – OHLC candlestick bars
   10    3. multi_coin    – multi-coin % comparison overlay
   11    4. volume_price  – dual-axis price line + volume bars
   12    5. momentum      – price with RSI subplot
   13    6. bar_change    – horizontal bar chart of top movers by % change
   14  
   15  Charts are saved as temporary PNGs and cleaned up after posting.
   16  """

  176      ("SOL", "SOLUSDT"), ("XRP", "XRPUSDT"), ("ADA", "ADAUSDT"),

  182  _BINANCE_TICKER_URL = "https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ticker/24hr"

  184  
  185  def _coin_id_to_binance(coin_id: str) -> str | None:
  186      """Map a CoinGecko coin_id to a Binance trading pair symbol."""
  187      return _BINANCE_SYMBOL_MAP.get(coin_id)
  188  
  189  
  190  def _days_to_binance_interval(days: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
  191      """Return (interval, limit) covering the requested number of days."""
  192      if days <= 1:
  193          return "1h", 24
  194      elif days <= 14:
  195          return "1h", days * 24
  196      elif days <= 30:
  197          return "4h", days * 6
  198      else:
  199          return "1d", min(days, 1000)
  200  
  201  
  202  def _ensure_chart_dir() -> None:
  203      os.makedirs(_CHART_DIR, exist_ok=True)
  204  
  205  
  206  def _cleanup_old_charts() -> None:
  207      """Remove chart images older than 1 hour."""
  208      if not os.path.exists(_CHART_DIR):
  209          return
  210      cutoff = time.time() - 3600
  211      for f in os.listdir(_CHART_DIR):
  212          path = os.path.join(_CHART_DIR, f)
  213          try:
  214              if os.path.getmtime(path) < cutoff:
  215                  os.remove(path)
  216          except OSError:
  217              pass
  218  
  219  
  220  # ── Rotation tracking ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  221  
  222  def _load_rotation() -> dict:
  223      if not os.path.exists(_ROTATION_FILE):
  224          return {"last_styles": [], "last_coin": ""}
  225      try:
  226          with open(_ROTATION_FILE) as f:
  227              return json.load(f)
  228      except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
  229          return {"last_styles": [], "last_coin": ""}
  230  
  231  
  232  def _save_rotation(data: dict) -> None:
  233      try:
  234          with open(_ROTATION_FILE, "w") as f:
  235              json.dump(data, f)
  236      except OSError:
  237          pass
  238  
  239  
  240  def get_next_chart_style() -> str:
  241      """Pick the next chart style, avoiding the last 3 used styles."""
  242      rotation = _load_rotation()
  243      recent = rotation.get("last_styles", [])[-3:]  # avoid last 3
  244  
  245      available = [s for s in CHART_STYLES if s not in recent]
  246      if not available:
  247          available = CHART_STYLES  # all exhausted, reset
  248  
  249      return random.choice(available)
  250  
  251  
  252  def record_chart_style(style: str, coin: str = "") -> None:
  253      """Record which style was just used."""
  254      rotation = _load_rotation()
  255      last_styles = rotation.get("last_styles", [])
  256      last_styles.append(style)
  257      # Keep last 6
  258      if len(last_styles) > 6:
  259          last_styles = last_styles[-6:]
  260      rotation["last_styles"] = last_styles
  261      if coin:
  262          rotation["last_coin"] = coin
  263      _save_rotation(rotation)
  264

  266  # ── Data fetching ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  267  
  268  def fetch_price_history(coin_id: str, days: int = 7) -> list[tuple[float, float]] | None:
  269      """
  270      Fetch price history from Binance klines.
  271      Returns list of (timestamp_ms, close_price) tuples.
  272      """
  273      pair = _coin_id_to_binance(coin_id)
  274      if not pair:
  275          logger.warning("No Binance pair for coin_id '%s'", coin_id)
  276          return None
  277      interval, limit = _days_to_binance_interval(days)
  278      try:
  279          resp = requests.get(
  280              _BINANCE_KLINES_URL,
  281              params={"symbol": pair, "interval": interval, "limit": limit},
  282              timeout=15,
  283          )
  284          resp.raise_for_status()
  285          klines = resp.json()
  286          return [(float(k[0]), float(k[4])) for k in klines]
  287      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  288          logger.warning("Failed to fetch price history for %s: %s", coin_id, exc)
  289          return None
  290  
  291  
  292  def _fetch_market_chart_full(coin_id: str, days: int = 7) -> dict | None:
  293      """Fetch prices + volumes from Binance klines."""
  294      pair = _coin_id_to_binance(coin_id)
  295      if not pair:
  296          logger.warning("No Binance pair for coin_id '%s' — chart will fail", coin_id)
  297          return None
  298      logger.debug("Fetching Binance klines: pair=%s, coin_id=%s, days=%d", pair, coin_id, days)
  299      interval, limit = _days_to_binance_interval(days)
  300      try:
  301          resp = requests.get(
  302              _BINANCE_KLINES_URL,
  303              params={"symbol": pair, "interval": interval, "limit": limit},
  304              timeout=15,
  305          )
  306          resp.raise_for_status()
  307          klines = resp.json()
  308  
  309          # Binance error responses are dicts, not lists
  310          if isinstance(klines, dict):
  311              logger.warning("Binance returned error for %s: %s", pair, klines)
  312              return None
  313          if not klines:
  314              logger.warning("Binance returned empty klines for %s", pair)
  315              return None
  316  
  317          logger.debug("Binance klines received: pair=%s, count=%d, "
  318                       "first_close=%s, last_close=%s",
  319                       pair, len(klines), klines[0][4], klines[-1][4])
  320  
  321          prices = [[float(k[0]), float(k[4])] for k in klines]
  322          volumes = [[float(k[0]), float(k[5])] for k in klines]
  323  
  324          # Validate prices are real numbers, not all zero
  325          close_prices = [p[1] for p in prices]
  326          if not close_prices or all(p == 0 for p in close_prices):
  327              logger.warning("Binance returned all-zero prices for %s", pair)
  328              return None
  329  
  330          return {"prices": prices, "volumes": volumes}
  331      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  332          logger.warning("Failed to fetch market chart for %s: %s", coin_id, exc)
  333          return None
  334  
  335  
  336  def _fetch_ohlc(coin_id: str, days: int = 7) -> list | None:
  337      """Fetch OHLC data from Binance klines. Returns [[ts, o, h, l, c], ...]."""
  338      pair = _coin_id_to_binance(coin_id)
  339      if not pair:
  340          logger.warning("No Binance pair for coin_id '%s'", coin_id)
  341          return None
  342      interval, limit = _days_to_binance_interval(days)
  343      try:
  344          resp = requests.get(
  345              _BINANCE_KLINES_URL,
  346              params={"symbol": pair, "interval": interval, "limit": limit},
  347              timeout=15,
  348          )
  349          resp.raise_for_status()
  350          klines = resp.json()
  351          return [[float(k[0]), float(k[1]), float(k[2]), float(k[3]), float(k[4])] for k in klines]
  352      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  353          logger.warning("Failed to fetch OHLC for %s: %s", coin_id, exc)
  354          return None
  355  
  356  
  357  def _fetch_top_movers() -> list[dict] | None:
  358      """Fetch 24h change for top coins from Binance ticker."""
  359      pairs = [p for _, p in _BINANCE_TOP_COINS]
  360      try:
  361          resp = requests.get(
  362              _BINANCE_TICKER_URL,
  363              params={"symbols": json.dumps(pairs)},
  364              timeout=15,
  365          )
  366          resp.raise_for_status()
  367          tickers = resp.json()
  368          return [
  369              {
  370                  "symbol": t["symbol"].replace("USDT", ""),
  371                  "price_change_percentage_24h": float(t["priceChangePercent"]),
  372              }
  373              for t in tickers
  374          ]
  375      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  376          logger.warning("Failed to fetch top movers: %s", exc)
  377          return None

  380  # ── Shared styling helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  381  
  382  def _style_ax(ax, date_fmt="%b %d"):
  383      """Apply shared dark theme to an axis."""
  384      import matplotlib.dates as mdates
  385      ax.tick_params(colors=_TEXT, labelsize=10)
  386      ax.spines["top"].set_visible(False)
  387      ax.spines["right"].set_visible(False)
  388      ax.spines["bottom"].set_color(_AXIS)
  389      ax.spines["left"].set_color(_AXIS)
  390      ax.grid(True, alpha=0.15, color=_GRID)
  391      if date_fmt:
  392          ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(mdates.DateFormatter(date_fmt))
  393  
  394  
  395  def _watermark(ax):
  396      ax.text(0.99, 0.02, "@CoinWatchAlert", transform=ax.transAxes,
  397              fontsize=9, color="#555555", ha="right", va="bottom", alpha=0.7)
  398  
  399  
  400  def _draw_grid_dots(ax, nx: int = 30, ny: int = 20, alpha: float = 0.06) -> None:
  401      """Draw a subtle grid dot texture for depth."""
  402      import numpy as np
  403      for x in np.linspace(0.02, 0.98, nx):
  404          for y in np.linspace(0.02, 0.98, ny):
  405              ax.plot(x, y, '.', color='white', markersize=0.5, alpha=alpha,
  406                      transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=0)
  407  
  408  
  409  def _save_fig(fig, name: str) -> str:
  410      filepath = os.path.join(_CHART_DIR, f"{name}_{int(time.time())}.png")
  411      try:
  412          fig.tight_layout()
  413      except Exception:
  414          pass
  415      fig.savefig(filepath, dpi=200, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor=_BG)
  416      import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  417      plt.close(fig)
  418      logger.info("Generated chart: %s", filepath)
  419      return filepath
  420  
  421  
  422  def _price_fmt(x, _=None):
  423      return f"${x:,.0f}" if x >= 1000 else f"${x:,.2f}"
  424  
  425  
  426  # ── Chart style 1: Line with fill (original) ────────────────────────────────
  427  
  428  def generate_line_fill(coin_id: str, symbol: str, days: int = 7) -> str | None:
  429      """Unified chart renderer — all chart generation routes through here."""
  430      logger.info("[CHART] UNIFIED: %s (%s, %dd)", symbol, coin_id, days)
  431      try:
  432          import matplotlib
  433          matplotlib.use("Agg")
  434          import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  435          import matplotlib.gridspec as gridspec
  436          import numpy as np
  437          from datetime import datetime, timezone
  438      except ImportError:
  439          return None
  440  
  441      try:
  442          logger.debug("generate_line_fill called: coin_id=%s, symbol=%s, days=%d",
  443                       coin_id, symbol, days)
  444          data = _fetch_market_chart_full(coin_id, days)
  445          if not data or not data["prices"] or len(data["prices"]) < 10:
  446              logger.warning("generate_line_fill: insufficient data for %s/%s "
  447                            "(data=%s, points=%d)", symbol, coin_id,
  448                            "present" if data else "None",
  449                            len(data.get("prices", [])) if data else 0)
  450              return None
  451  
  452          _ensure_chart_dir()
  453          _cleanup_old_charts()
  454  
  455          times = [datetime.fromtimestamp(p[0] / 1000, tz=timezone.utc) for p in data["prices"]]
  456          values = [p[1] for p in data["prices"]]
  457          volumes = [v[1] for v in data["volumes"]] if data.get("volumes") else None
  458  
  459          # Validate plottable data
  460          if not values or not times or len(values) != len(times):
  461              logger.warning("generate_line_fill: times/values mismatch or empty "
  462                            "(times=%d, values=%d) for %s",
  463                            len(times) if times else 0,
  464                            len(values) if values else 0, symbol)
  465              return None
  466          if len(values) < 10:
  467              logger.warning("generate_line_fill: not enough data points "
  468                            "(%d < 10) for %s — using fallback", len(values), symbol)
  469              return None
  470          if all(v == 0 for v in values):
  471              logger.warning("generate_line_fill: all-zero values for %s — skipping", symbol)
  472              return None
  473          if max(values) == min(values):
  474              logger.warning("generate_line_fill: flat price data (all=%.8f) for %s "
  475                            "— using fallback instead of blank-looking chart",
  476                            values[0], symbol)
  477              return None
  478  
  479          logger.debug("generate_line_fill plotting: %s %d points, "
  480                       "first=%.8f last=%.8f hi=%.8f lo=%.8f",
  481                       symbol, len(values), values[0], values[-1],
  482                       max(values), min(values))
  483  
  484          is_up = values[-1] >= values[0]
  485          accent = _ACCENT_GREEN if is_up else _ACCENT_RED
  486          arrow = "▲" if is_up else "▼"
  487          pct = ((values[-1] - values[0]) / values[0]) * 100
  488          price_str = _price_fmt(values[-1])
  489          period = {1: "24H", 7: "7D", 14: "14D", 30: "30D", 90: "90D"}.get(days, f"{days}D")
  490  
  491          # Find high/low
  492          hi_idx = np.argmax(values)
  493          lo_idx = np.argmin(values)
  494          open_price = values[0]
  495  
  496          # ── Chart type selection ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  497          _type_roll = random.random()
  498          if _type_roll < 0.60:
  499              _chart_type = "filled"       # 60% — line + fill + volume
  500          elif _type_roll < 0.85:
  501              _chart_type = "candlestick"  # 25% — candlestick + volume
  502          else:
  503              _chart_type = "minimalist"   # 15% — clean line only, no volume
  504  
  505          # Occasionally wider aspect for more context (20% chance)
  506          _fig_width = 18 if random.random() < 0.20 else 16
  507  
  508          # Layout: header panel + chart + optional volume
  509          fig = plt.figure(figsize=(_fig_width, 9), dpi=100, facecolor=_BG)
  510          _show_volume = volumes and _chart_type != "minimalist"
  511          if _show_volume:
  512              gs = gridspec.GridSpec(3, 1, height_ratios=[1.2, 5, 1.5], hspace=0.08,
  513                                    figure=fig, left=0.08, right=0.95, top=0.95, bottom=0.06)
  514          else:
  515              gs = gridspec.GridSpec(2, 1, height_ratios=[1.2, 5], hspace=0.08,
  516                                    figure=fig, left=0.08, right=0.95, top=0.95, bottom=0.06)
  517  
  518          # ── Header panel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  519          ax_hdr = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])
  520          ax_hdr.set_facecolor(_BG)
  521          ax_hdr.axis("off")
  522          ax_hdr.text(0.0, 0.5, symbol, transform=ax_hdr.transAxes,
  523                      fontsize=48, fontweight="bold", color="white", va="center")
  524          ax_hdr.text(0.22, 0.55, price_str, transform=ax_hdr.transAxes,
  525                      fontsize=32, color="white", va="center")
  526          ax_hdr.text(0.22, 0.15, f"{arrow} {pct:+.2f}%  {period}",
  527                      transform=ax_hdr.transAxes,
  528                      fontsize=18, fontweight="bold", color=accent, va="center")
  529          ax_hdr.text(1.0, 0.5, "@CoinWatchAlert", transform=ax_hdr.transAxes,
  530                      fontsize=9, color="#555555", ha="right", va="center")
  531  
  532          # ── Direction color — 4-tier with amber for sideways ───────────────
  533          price_change_pct = ((values[-1] - values[0]) / values[0]) * 100
  534          if price_change_pct > 2:
  535              line_color = "#00E676"
  536              fill_color = "#00E67626"
  537          elif price_change_pct < -2:
  538              line_color = "#FF3D57"
  539              fill_color = "#FF3D5726"
  540          elif abs(price_change_pct) < 0.5:
  541              line_color = "#F5A623"    # amber for flat/sideways
  542              fill_color = "#F5A62326"
  543          else:
  544              line_color = "#FFD600"
  545              fill_color = "#FFD60026"
  546  
  547          # ── Main chart ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  548          ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])
  549          ax.set_facecolor(_BG)
  550  
  551          min_val = min(values)
  552          max_val = max(values)
  553  
  554          logger.info("[CHART DEBUG] %s: %d values, first=%.4f, last=%.4f, "
  555                      "min=%.4f, max=%.4f",
  556                      symbol, len(values), values[0], values[-1], min_val, max_val)
  557  
  558          if not values or len(values) < 10:
  559              logger.warning("[CHART DEBUG] INVALID VALUES (%d) — using fallback", len(values))
  560              plt.close(fig)
  561              return None
  562  
  563          if min_val == max_val:
  564              logger.warning("[CHART DEBUG] FLAT DATA (all=%.8f) — fallback", min_val)
  565              plt.close(fig)
  566              return None
  567  
  568          # Force axis ranges — exaggerate small moves so charts never look flat
  569          range_val = max_val - min_val
  570          if range_val < (max_val * 0.02):
  571              # Very small movement — exaggerate heavily
  572              padding = range_val * 0.8
  573          else:
  574              padding = range_val * 0.2
  575          ax.set_ylim(min_val - padding, max_val + padding)
  576          ax.set_xlim(times[0], times[-1])
  577  
  578          # ── Price rendering — varies by _chart_type ────────────────────────
  579          if _chart_type == "filled":
  580              ax.plot(times, values, color=line_color, linewidth=10, alpha=0.15, zorder=2, solid_capstyle="round")
  581              ax.plot(times, values, color=line_color, linewidth=4, alpha=1.0, zorder=3, solid_capstyle="round")
  582              ax.fill_between(times, values, min_val, color=fill_color, zorder=1)
  583          elif _chart_type == "candlestick":
  584              ohlc = _fetch_ohlc(coin_id, days)
  585              if ohlc and len(ohlc) >= 10:
  586                  from datetime import datetime as _dt, timezone as _tz
  587                  from matplotlib.patches import Rectangle
  588                  from matplotlib.dates import date2num
  589                  bar_width = (times[-1] - times[0]).total_seconds() / len(ohlc) / 86400 * 0.6
  590                  for candle in ohlc:
  591                      ts, o, h, l, c = candle
  592                      t = _dt.fromtimestamp(ts / 1000, tz=_tz.utc)
  593                      color = "#00E676" if c >= o else "#FF3D57"
  594                      ax.plot([t, t], [l, h], color=color, linewidth=0.8, zorder=2)
  595                      body_lo, body_hi = min(o, c), max(o, c)
  596                      if body_hi == body_lo:
  597                          body_hi = body_lo + (h - l) * 0.01
  598                      rect = Rectangle((date2num(t) - bar_width / 2, body_lo),
  599                                       bar_width, body_hi - body_lo,
  600                                       facecolor=color, edgecolor=color, zorder=3)
  601                      ax.add_patch(rect)
  602                  ax.xaxis_date()
  603              else:
  604                  # Fallback to filled if no OHLC
  605                  ax.plot(times, values, color=line_color, linewidth=10, alpha=0.15, zorder=2, solid_capstyle="round")
  606                  ax.plot(times, values, color=line_color, linewidth=4, alpha=1.0, zorder=3, solid_capstyle="round")
  607                  ax.fill_between(times, values, min_val, color=fill_color, zorder=1)
  608                  _chart_type = "filled-fallback"
  609          else:  # minimalist — clean line, no fill, no volume
  610              ax.plot(times, values, color=line_color, linewidth=2, alpha=1.0, zorder=3, solid_capstyle="round")
  611  
  612          logger.info("[CHART DEBUG] %s: type=%s, width=%d, ylim=(%.4f, %.4f)",
  613                      symbol, _chart_type, _fig_width, *ax.get_ylim())
  614  
  615          # Big price text — top right, impossible to miss
  616          ax.text(0.98, 0.90, _price_fmt(values[-1]),
  617                  transform=ax.transAxes, ha="right", va="top",
  618                  fontsize=22, fontweight="bold", color="white", zorder=8)
  619          # Percentage change below price
  620          ax.text(0.98, 0.78, f"{arrow} {pct:+.2f}%",
  621                  transform=ax.transAxes, ha="right", va="top",
  622                  fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", color=line_color, zorder=8)
  623  
  624          # High/low markers — full opacity
  625          ax.plot(times[hi_idx], values[hi_idx], 'o', color="#00FFAA",
  626                  markersize=7, alpha=1.0, zorder=5)
  627          ax.annotate(f"H {_price_fmt(values[hi_idx])}", (times[hi_idx], values[hi_idx]),
  628                      textcoords="offset points", xytext=(8, 8),
  629                      fontsize=9, fontweight="bold", color="#00FFAA", zorder=5)
  630          ax.plot(times[lo_idx], values[lo_idx], 'o', color="#FF4D4D",
  631                  markersize=7, alpha=1.0, zorder=5)
  632          ax.annotate(f"L {_price_fmt(values[lo_idx])}", (times[lo_idx], values[lo_idx]),
  633                      textcoords="offset points", xytext=(8, -12),
  634                      fontsize=9, fontweight="bold", color="#FF4D4D", zorder=5)
  635  
  636          # Minimal chrome — remove clutter
  637          ax.set_xticks([])
  638          ax.set_yticks([])
  639          for spine in ax.spines.values():
  640              spine.set_visible(False)
  641          ax.grid(True, alpha=0.03, color=_GRID)
  642  
  643          # ── Dynamic headline overlay — rotating labels with cooldown ─────────
  644          if price_change_pct < -2:
  645              pool = _HEADLINES_BEARISH
  646          elif price_change_pct > 2:
  647              pool = _HEADLINES_BULLISH
  648          else:
  649              pool = _HEADLINES_NEUTRAL
  650  
  651          # Filter out recently used labels
  652          fresh = [h for h in pool if h not in _recent_labels]
  653          if not fresh:
  654              fresh = pool  # fallback if all used recently
  655          headline = random.choice(fresh)
  656          _recent_labels.append(headline)
  657  
  658          # Vary annotation position
  659          _pos_roll = random.random()
  660          if _pos_roll < 0.60:
  661              _hx, _hy, _ha, _hva = 0.50, 0.97, "center", "top"      # top-centre (default)
  662          elif _pos_roll < 0.80:
  663              _hx, _hy, _ha, _hva = 0.02, 0.04, "left", "bottom"     # bottom-left
  664          else:
  665              _hx, _hy, _ha, _hva = 0.98, 0.97, "right", "top"       # top-right
  666  
  667          ax.text(_hx, _hy, headline,
  668                  transform=ax.transAxes, ha=_ha, va=_hva,
  669                  fontsize=16, fontweight="bold", color="white", alpha=0.9,
  670                  zorder=9)
  671  
  672          # ── Support line at minimum price ────────────────────────────────────
  673          ax.axhline(min_val, color="#555555", linewidth=0.8, linestyle="--",
  674                     alpha=0.4, zorder=1)
  675          ax.text(0.02, min_val, " support", fontsize=7, color="#666666",
  676                  va="bottom", transform=ax.get_yaxis_transform(), zorder=6)
  677  
  678          # ── Volume panel — minimal, with occasional purple accent ─────────
  679          if _show_volume:
  680              _vol_purple = random.random() < 0.20  # 20% chance of purple volume bars
  681              ax_vol = fig.add_subplot(gs[2], sharex=ax)
  682              ax_vol.set_facecolor(_BG)
  683              if _vol_purple:
  684                  vol_colors = ["#9b59b650"] * len(values)
  685              else:
  686                  vol_colors = [line_color + "50" if i == 0 or values[i] >= values[i-1]
  687                               else _ACCENT_RED + "50"
  688                               for i in range(len(values))]
  689              ax_vol.bar(times, volumes[:len(times)], width=(times[-1] - times[0]).total_seconds() / len(times) / 86400 * 0.8,
  690                        color=vol_colors[:len(times)], zorder=2)
  691              ax_vol.set_xticks([])
  692              ax_vol.set_yticks([])
  693              for spine in ax_vol.spines.values():
  694                  spine.set_visible(False)
  695  
  696          plt.tight_layout()
  697          filepath = os.path.join(_CHART_DIR, f"line_{symbol}_{days}d_{int(time.time())}.png")
  698          fig.savefig(filepath, dpi=100, facecolor=_BG, bbox_inches="tight")
  699          plt.close(fig)
  700  
  701          # Verify file was actually written and has content
  702          file_size = os.path.getsize(filepath)
  703          logger.info("[CHART DEBUG] %s: saved %s (%d bytes)", symbol, filepath, file_size)
  704          if file_size < 1000:
  705              logger.warning("[CHART DEBUG] %s: file suspiciously small (%d bytes) — "
  706                            "chart may be blank", symbol, file_size)
  707          return filepath
  708      except Exception as exc:
  709          logger.warning("generate_line_fill(%s, %s, %s) failed: %s", coin_id, symbol, days, exc)
  710          try:
  711              plt.close("all")
  712          except Exception:
  713              pass
  714          return None
  715

  721      """
  722      logger.info("[CHART HIT] generate_fallback_card(%s)", symbol)
  723      return None

  772  # ── Chart style 6: Bar chart of top movers ───────────────────────────────────
  773  
  774  def generate_bar_change() -> str | None:
  775      """Horizontal bar chart showing 24h % change for top coins."""
  776      logger.info("[CHART HIT] generate_bar_change()")
  777      try:
  778          import matplotlib
  779          matplotlib.use("Agg")
  780          import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  781      except ImportError:
  782          return None
  783  
  784      coins = _fetch_top_movers()
  785      if not coins or len(coins) < 5:
  786          return None
  787  
  788      _ensure_chart_dir()
  789      _cleanup_old_charts()
  790  
  791      # Take top 10 by market cap, sort by 24h change
  792      top = coins[:10]
  793      top.sort(key=lambda c: c.get("price_change_percentage_24h", 0) or 0)
  794  
  795      symbols = [c.get("symbol", "").upper() for c in top]
  796      changes = [c.get("price_change_percentage_24h", 0) or 0 for c in top]
  797      colors = [_GREEN if c >= 0 else _RED for c in changes]
  798  
  799      fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))
  800      fig.patch.set_facecolor(_BG)
  801      ax.set_facecolor(_BG)
  802  
  803      bars = ax.barh(symbols, changes, color=colors, height=0.6, edgecolor="none")
  804  
  805      # Add % labels on bars
  806      for bar, change in zip(bars, changes):
  807          x = bar.get_width()
  808          ax.text(x + (0.3 if x >= 0 else -0.3), bar.get_y() + bar.get_height() / 2,
  809                  f"{change:+.1f}%", va="center", ha="left" if x >= 0 else "right",
  810                  color="white", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
  811  
  812      ax.axvline(x=0, color=_GRID, linewidth=0.8)
  813      ax.set_title("Top 10 Coins — 24h Change", color="white", fontsize=16,
  814                   fontweight="bold", pad=15)
  815      ax.tick_params(colors=_TEXT, labelsize=11)
  816      ax.spines["top"].set_visible(False)
  817      ax.spines["right"].set_visible(False)
  818      ax.spines["bottom"].set_color(_AXIS)
  819      ax.spines["left"].set_color(_AXIS)
  820      ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(plt.FuncFormatter(lambda x, _: f"{x:+.0f}%"))
  821      ax.grid(True, alpha=0.1, axis="x", color=_GRID)
  822  
  823      _watermark(ax)
  824      return _save_fig(fig, "bar_24h")
  825

  839  # ── News card image generator ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  840  
  841  def _binance_ticker_price(pair: str) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
  842      """Fetch last price + 24h % change from Binance."""
  843      try:
  844          resp = requests.get(_BINANCE_TICKER_URL, params={"symbol": pair}, timeout=10)
  845          resp.raise_for_status()
  846          d = resp.json()
  847          return float(d["lastPrice"]), float(d["priceChangePercent"])
  848      except Exception:
  849          return None, None
  850  
  851  
  852  def _fetch_fear_greed_value() -> tuple[int | None, str | None]:
  853      """Fetch current Fear & Greed index value and classification."""
  854      try:
  855          r = requests.get("https://api.alternative.me/fng/",
  856                           params={"limit": 1, "format": "json"}, timeout=8)
  857          r.raise_for_status()
  858          entry = r.json().get("data", [{}])[0]
  859          return int(entry["value"]), entry.get("value_classification", "")
  860      except Exception:
  861          return None, None

  863  
  864  def _pil_word_wrap(draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, text: str, font: ImageFont.FreeTypeFont,
  865                     max_width: int, max_lines: int = 3) -> list[str]:
  866      """Word-wrap text to fit within max_width, returning up to max_lines."""
  867      words = text.split()
  868      lines: list[str] = []
  869      current = ""
  870      for word in words:
  871          test = f"{current} {word}".strip()
  872          bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), test, font=font)
  873          if bbox[2] - bbox[0] > max_width and current:
  874              lines.append(current)
  875              current = word
  876              if len(lines) >= max_lines:
  877                  break
  878          else:
  879              current = test
  880      if current and len(lines) < max_lines:
  881          lines.append(current)
  882      # Truncate last line with ellipsis if needed
  883      if len(lines) == max_lines:
  884          while lines[-1]:
  885              bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), lines[-1] + "...", font=font)
  886              if bbox[2] - bbox[0] <= max_width:
  887                  break
  888              lines[-1] = lines[-1][:-1]
  889          if lines[-1] != text.split("\n")[-1]:
  890              lines[-1] = lines[-1].rstrip() + "..."
  891      return lines[:max_lines]
  892  
  893  
  894  def _draw_pill_badge(draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, xy: tuple[int, int], text: str,
  895                       font: ImageFont.FreeTypeFont, bg_color: tuple, text_color: tuple,
  896                       padding: tuple[int, int] = (16, 6)) -> int:
  897      """Draw a rounded pill badge. Returns the right-edge x coordinate."""
  898      bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), text, font=font)
  899      tw, th = bbox[2] - bbox[0], bbox[3] - bbox[1]
  900      x, y = xy
  901      pill_w = tw + padding[0] * 2
  902      pill_h = th + padding[1] * 2
  903      draw.rounded_rectangle([x, y, x + pill_w, y + pill_h], radius=pill_h // 2,
  904                             fill=bg_color)
  905      draw.text((x + padding[0], y + padding[1] - 2), text, font=font, fill=text_color)
  906      return x + pill_w
  907  
  908  
  909  def _draw_ticker_item(draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, x: int, y: int, label: str,
  910                        price: float | None, pct: float | None,
  911                        font_label: ImageFont.FreeTypeFont,
  912                        font_price: ImageFont.FreeTypeFont,
  913                        font_pct: ImageFont.FreeTypeFont) -> int:
  914      """Draw a single ticker item (label + price + %change). Returns right edge x."""
  915      draw.text((x, y), label, font=font_label, fill=_PIL_MUTED)
  916      lbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), label, font=font_label)
  917      cx = x + (lbox[2] - lbox[0]) + 10
  918      if price is not None:
  919          price_str = f"${price:,.0f}" if price >= 1000 else f"${price:,.2f}"
  920          draw.text((cx, y), price_str, font=font_price, fill=_PIL_WHITE)
  921          pbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), price_str, font=font_price)
  922          cx += (pbox[2] - pbox[0]) + 10
  923      if pct is not None:
  924          arrow = "\u25B2" if pct >= 0 else "\u25BC"
  925          pct_str = f"{arrow}{pct:+.1f}%"
  926          color = _PIL_GREEN if pct >= 0 else _PIL_RED
  927          draw.text((cx, y), pct_str, font=font_pct, fill=color)
  928          pbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), pct_str, font=font_pct)
  929          cx += (pbox[2] - pbox[0])
  930      return cx
  931
