 1046  # ── Legacy API (kept for breakout_monitor compatibility) ─────────────────────
 1047  
 1048  def _fetch_sparkline(coin_id: str, days: int = 7) -> list[float] | None:
 1049      """Fetch 7-day close prices for a mini sparkline. Returns list of floats."""
 1050      history = fetch_price_history(coin_id, days)
 1051      if not history or len(history) < 5:
 1052          return None
 1053      return [p[1] for p in history]
 1054  
 1055  
 1056  def _draw_sparkline(ax, prices: list[float], colour: str, x0: float, y0: float,
 1057                      w: float, h: float) -> None:
 1058      """Draw a tiny sparkline in axes-coordinate space."""
 1059      import numpy as np
 1060      n = len(prices)
 1061      xs = np.linspace(x0, x0 + w, n)
 1062      mn, mx = min(prices), max(prices)
 1063      rng = mx - mn if mx != mn else 1.0
 1064      ys = [y0 + (p - mn) / rng * h for p in prices]
 1065      ax.plot(xs, ys, color=colour, linewidth=1.2, solid_capstyle="round",
 1066              transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=5)
 1067      ax.fill_between(xs, y0, ys, color=colour, alpha=0.10,
 1068                      transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=4)
 1069  
 1070  
 1071  _COIN_ID_MAP = {
 1072      "BTC": "bitcoin", "ETH": "ethereum", "SOL": "solana",
 1073      "XRP": "ripple", "BNB": "binancecoin", "ADA": "cardano",
 1074      "DOGE": "dogecoin", "AVAX": "avalanche-2", "DOT": "polkadot",
 1075      "LINK": "chainlink",
 1076  }
 1077  
 1078  
 1079  def generate_morning_recap_chart(coins: list[dict]) -> str | None:
 1080      """Terminal-style morning recap: grid panels, massive numbers, sparklines, volume bars."""
 1081      logger.info("[CHART HIT] generate_morning_recap_chart(%d coins)", len(coins))
 1082      try:
 1083          import matplotlib
 1084          matplotlib.use("Agg")
 1085          import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
 1086          import matplotlib.patches as mpatches
 1087          import numpy as np
 1088      except ImportError:
 1089          logger.warning("matplotlib not available — cannot generate morning recap chart")
 1090          return None
 1091  
 1092      try:
 1093          _ensure_chart_dir()
 1094          _cleanup_old_charts()
 1095  
 1096          top5 = coins[:5]
 1097          if not top5:
 1098              return None
 1099  
 1100          def _coin_pct(c: dict) -> float:
 1101              return (
 1102                  c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency")
 1103                  or c.get("price_change_percentage_24h")
 1104                  or c.get("pct_24h")
 1105                  or c.get("change_24h")
 1106                  or 0
 1107              )
 1108  
 1109          # 1600×900 @ 200 DPI
 1110          fig = plt.figure(figsize=(10.67, 6), facecolor=_BG)
 1111          ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])
 1112          ax.set_xlim(0, 1)
 1113          ax.set_ylim(0, 1)
 1114          ax.set_facecolor(_BG)
 1115          ax.axis("off")
 1116          _draw_grid_dots(ax, nx=50, ny=30, alpha=0.03)
 1117  
 1118          # ── Title bar with accent ────────────────────────────────────────────
 1119          ax.axhline(0.97, xmin=0.02, xmax=0.98, color=_GOLD, linewidth=2, clip_on=False)
 1120          ax.text(0.03, 0.93, "M A R K E T   O V E R V I E W",
 1121                  transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=18, fontweight="bold", color="white",
 1122                  va="top", ha="left")
 1123          import datetime as _dt
 1124          now_str = _dt.datetime.utcnow().strftime("%b %d %Y  %H:%M UTC")
 1125          ax.text(0.97, 0.93, now_str, transform=ax.transAxes,
 1126                  fontsize=10, color=_MUTED, va="top", ha="right")
 1127  
 1128          # ── Hero coin (BTC or first coin) — large panel left ────────────────
 1129          hero = top5[0]
 1130          h_sym = hero.get("symbol", "???").upper()
 1131          h_price = hero.get("current_price", 0) or 0
 1132          h_pct = _coin_pct(hero)
 1133          h_color = _ACCENT_GREEN if h_pct >= 0 else _ACCENT_RED
 1134          h_arrow = "▲" if h_pct >= 0 else "▼"
 1135  
 1136          # Hero panel background
 1137          hero_bg = mpatches.FancyBboxPatch(
 1138              (0.02, 0.42), 0.46, 0.46, boxstyle="round,pad=0.01",
 1139              facecolor=_PANEL, edgecolor=_BORDER, linewidth=1,
 1140              transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=1,
 1141          )
 1142          ax.add_patch(hero_bg)
 1143  
 1144          ax.text(0.05, 0.84, h_sym, transform=ax.transAxes,
 1145                  fontsize=56, fontweight="bold", color="white", va="top", zorder=5)
 1146          ax.text(0.05, 0.65, _price_fmt(h_price), transform=ax.transAxes,
 1147                  fontsize=38, color="white", va="top", zorder=5)
 1148          ax.text(0.05, 0.52, f"{h_arrow} {h_pct:+.2f}%", transform=ax.transAxes,
 1149                  fontsize=24, fontweight="bold", color=h_color, va="top", zorder=5)
 1150  
 1151          # Hero sparkline
 1152          h_coin_id = _COIN_ID_MAP.get(h_sym, hero.get("id", ""))
 1153          h_spark = _fetch_sparkline(h_coin_id) if h_coin_id else None
 1154          if h_spark:
 1155              _draw_sparkline(ax, h_spark, h_color, x0=0.25, y0=0.44, w=0.21, h=0.18)
 1156  
 1157          # ── Right panel — remaining coins in grid ────────────────────────────
 1158          right_coins = top5[1:5]
 1159          grid_x0 = 0.52
 1160          grid_w = 0.23
 1161          grid_h = 0.21
 1162          gap = 0.02
 1163  
 1164          positions = [
 1165              (grid_x0, 0.67),                    # top-left
 1166              (grid_x0 + grid_w + gap, 0.67),     # top-right
 1167              (grid_x0, 0.42),                     # bottom-left
 1168              (grid_x0 + grid_w + gap, 0.42),      # bottom-right
 1169          ]
 1170  
 1171          for idx, coin in enumerate(right_coins):
 1172              if idx >= len(positions):
 1173                  break
 1174              px, py = positions[idx]
 1175              sym = coin.get("symbol", "???").upper()
 1176              cprice = coin.get("current_price", 0) or 0
 1177              cpct = _coin_pct(coin)
 1178              ccolor = _ACCENT_GREEN if cpct >= 0 else _ACCENT_RED
 1179              carrow = "▲" if cpct >= 0 else "▼"
 1180  
 1181              # Panel bg
 1182              panel = mpatches.FancyBboxPatch(
 1183                  (px, py), grid_w, grid_h, boxstyle="round,pad=0.008",
 1184                  facecolor=_PANEL, edgecolor=_BORDER, linewidth=0.8,
 1185                  transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=1,
 1186              )
 1187              ax.add_patch(panel)
 1188  
 1189              ax.text(px + 0.015, py + grid_h - 0.025, sym, transform=ax.transAxes,
 1190                      fontsize=16, fontweight="bold", color="white", va="top", zorder=5)
 1191              ax.text(px + 0.015, py + grid_h * 0.45, _price_fmt(cprice),
 1192                      transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=14, color="white", va="center", zorder=5)
 1193              ax.text(px + 0.015, py + 0.025, f"{carrow} {cpct:+.1f}%",
 1194                      transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=13, fontweight="bold",
 1195                      color=ccolor, va="bottom", zorder=5)
 1196  
 1197              # Tiny sparkline
 1198              c_id = _COIN_ID_MAP.get(sym, coin.get("id", ""))
 1199              c_spark = _fetch_sparkline(c_id) if c_id else None
 1200              if c_spark:
 1201                  _draw_sparkline(ax, c_spark, ccolor,
 1202                                  x0=px + grid_w * 0.5, y0=py + 0.02,
 1203                                  w=grid_w * 0.45, h=grid_h * 0.5)
 1204  
 1205          # ── Summary bar at bottom ────────────────────────────────────────────
 1206          bar_bg = mpatches.FancyBboxPatch(
 1207              (0.02, 0.04), 0.96, 0.32, boxstyle="round,pad=0.01",
 1208              facecolor=_PANEL, edgecolor=_BORDER, linewidth=1,
 1209              transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=1,
 1210          )
 1211          ax.add_patch(bar_bg)
 1212  
 1213          # Horizontal bar chart for all 5 coins
 1214          max_abs_pct = max(abs(_coin_pct(c)) for c in top5) or 1.0
 1215          bar_y_start = 0.30
 1216          bar_row_h = 0.05
 1217          for i, coin in enumerate(top5):
 1218              by = bar_y_start - i * bar_row_h
 1219              sym = coin.get("symbol", "???").upper()
 1220              cpct = _coin_pct(coin)
 1221              ccolor = _ACCENT_GREEN if cpct >= 0 else _ACCENT_RED
 1222              bar_w = (abs(cpct) / max_abs_pct) * 0.45
 1223  
 1224              ax.text(0.04, by, sym, transform=ax.transAxes,
 1225                      fontsize=10, fontweight="bold", color="white", va="center", zorder=5)
 1226              bar_rect = mpatches.FancyBboxPatch(
 1227                  (0.12, by - 0.015), bar_w, 0.03,
 1228                  boxstyle="round,pad=0.002", facecolor=ccolor + "40",
 1229                  edgecolor=ccolor, linewidth=0.6,
 1230                  transform=ax.transAxes, zorder=4,
 1231              )
 1232              ax.add_patch(bar_rect)
 1233              ax.text(0.12 + bar_w + 0.01, by, f"{cpct:+.1f}%",
 1234                      transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=9, fontweight="bold",
 1235                      color=ccolor, va="center", zorder=5)
 1236  
 1237          # Green/red count
 1238          green_n = sum(1 for c in top5 if _coin_pct(c) >= 0)
 1239          ax.text(0.85, 0.15, f"{green_n}/{len(top5)} green",
 1240                  transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=12, fontweight="bold",
 1241                  color=_ACCENT_GREEN if green_n > len(top5) // 2 else _ACCENT_RED,
 1242                  ha="center", va="center", zorder=5)
 1243  
 1244          # ── Watermark ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 1245          ax.text(0.97, 0.01, "@CoinWatchAlert", transform=ax.transAxes,
 1246                  fontsize=9, color="#555555", va="bottom", ha="right")
 1247  
 1248          filepath = os.path.join(_CHART_DIR, f"morning_recap_{int(time.time())}.png")
 1249          fig.savefig(filepath, dpi=200, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor=_BG)
 1250          plt.close(fig)
 1251          logger.info("Generated morning recap chart: %s", filepath)
 1252          return filepath
 1253      except Exception as exc:
 1254          logger.warning("generate_morning_recap_chart failed: %s", exc)
 1255          return None
 1256  
 1257  
 1258  def generate_fear_greed_gauge(value: int, classification: str) -> str | None:
 1259      """Large semicircle gauge with gradient zones, needle, and historical comparison."""
 1260      logger.info("[CHART HIT] generate_fear_greed_gauge(%d, %s)", value, classification)
 1261      try:
 1262          import matplotlib
 1263          matplotlib.use("Agg")
 1264          import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
 1265          import matplotlib.patches as mpatches
 1266          import numpy as np
 1267      except ImportError:
 1268          logger.warning("matplotlib not available — cannot generate fear/greed gauge")
 1269          return None
 1270  
 1271      try:
 1272          _ensure_chart_dir()
 1273          _cleanup_old_charts()
 1274  
 1275          # Fetch historical values for comparison
 1276          last_week_val: int | None = None
 1277          last_month_val: int | None = None
 1278          try:
 1279              fg_resp = requests.get(
 1280                  "https://api.alternative.me/fng/",
 1281                  params={"limit": 31, "format": "json"},
 1282                  timeout=10,
 1283              )
 1284              fg_resp.raise_for_status()
 1285              fg_history = fg_resp.json().get("data", [])
 1286              if len(fg_history) >= 7:
 1287                  last_week_val = int(fg_history[6]["value"])
 1288              if len(fg_history) >= 30:
 1289                  last_month_val = int(fg_history[29]["value"])
 1290          except Exception:
 1291              pass
 1292  
 1293          # 1600×900 @ 200 DPI — wider figure to maintain 1600px+ after equal aspect
 1294          fig = plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6), facecolor=_BG)
 1295          ax = fig.add_axes([0.08, 0.02, 0.84, 0.96])
 1296          ax.set_xlim(-1.5, 1.5)
 1297          ax.set_ylim(-0.85, 1.4)
 1298          ax.set_aspect("equal")
 1299          ax.set_facecolor(_BG)
 1300          ax.axis("off")
 1301  
 1302          # Title
 1303          ax.text(0, 1.32, "FEAR & GREED INDEX", ha="center", va="top",
 1304                  fontsize=20, fontweight="bold", color="white")
 1305  
 1306          # Zone definitions
 1307          zones = [
 1308              (0,  20, "#ff4444", "Extreme\nFear"),
 1309              (20, 40, "#ff8844", "Fear"),
 1310              (40, 60, "#F5A623", "Neutral"),
 1311              (60, 80, "#66ddaa", "Greed"),
 1312              (80, 100, "#00C896", "Extreme\nGreed"),
 1313          ]
 1314  
 1315          def _val_to_angle(v: float) -> float:
 1316              return 180.0 - v * 1.8
 1317  
 1318          outer_r = 1.05
 1319          inner_r = 0.65
 1320  
 1321          for start_v, end_v, colour, label in zones:
 1322              theta1 = _val_to_angle(end_v)
 1323              theta2 = _val_to_angle(start_v)
 1324              wedge = mpatches.Wedge(
 1325                  center=(0, 0), r=outer_r,
 1326                  theta1=theta1, theta2=theta2,
 1327                  width=outer_r - inner_r,
 1328                  facecolor=colour, edgecolor=_BG, linewidth=2,
 1329              )
 1330              ax.add_patch(wedge)
 1331  
 1332              # Zone label
 1333              mid_v = (start_v + end_v) / 2
 1334              mid_rad = np.radians(_val_to_angle(mid_v))
 1335              lx = 1.22 * np.cos(mid_rad)
 1336              ly = 1.22 * np.sin(mid_rad)
 1337              ax.text(lx, ly, label, ha="center", va="center",
 1338                      fontsize=9, color=_MUTED, linespacing=1.1)
 1339  
 1340          # Tick marks around the gauge
 1341          for tv in range(0, 101, 10):
 1342              angle_rad = np.radians(_val_to_angle(tv))
 1343              x1 = (outer_r + 0.02) * np.cos(angle_rad)
 1344              y1 = (outer_r + 0.02) * np.sin(angle_rad)
 1345              x2 = (outer_r + 0.06) * np.cos(angle_rad)
 1346              y2 = (outer_r + 0.06) * np.sin(angle_rad)
 1347              ax.plot([x1, x2], [y1, y2], color=_MUTED, linewidth=1, zorder=3)
 1348  
 1349          # Needle — thick, with glow
 1350          needle_rad = np.radians(_val_to_angle(value))
 1351          needle_len = 0.90
 1352          nx = needle_len * np.cos(needle_rad)
 1353          ny = needle_len * np.sin(needle_rad)
 1354          # Glow
 1355          ax.plot([0, nx], [0, ny], color="white", linewidth=4, alpha=0.15, zorder=4)
 1356          # Needle line
 1357          ax.annotate("", xy=(nx, ny), xytext=(0, 0),
 1358                      arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="->,head_width=0.06,head_length=0.08",
 1359                                      color="white", lw=3), zorder=5)
 1360          pivot = plt.Circle((0, 0), 0.05, color="white", zorder=6)
 1361          ax.add_patch(pivot)
 1362  
 1363          # Centre value — massive
 1364          # Determine color from zones
 1365          val_color = "#ffcc00"
 1366          for sv, ev, col, _ in zones:
 1367              if sv <= value <= ev:
 1368                  val_color = col
 1369                  break
 1370  
 1371          # Value readout — below the gauge with clear separation from needle
 1372          ax.text(0, -0.25, str(value), ha="center", va="top",
 1373                  fontsize=72, fontweight="bold", color=val_color)
 1374          ax.text(0, -0.50, classification.upper(), ha="center", va="top",
 1375                  fontsize=18, fontweight="bold", color=_MUTED)
 1376  
 1377          # Historical comparison panel
 1378          hist_parts = []
 1379          if last_week_val is not None:
 1380              hist_parts.append(f"Last week: {last_week_val}")
 1381          if last_month_val is not None:
 1382              hist_parts.append(f"Last month: {last_month_val}")
 1383          if hist_parts:
 1384              ax.text(0, -0.65, "  |  ".join(hist_parts), ha="center", va="top",
 1385                      fontsize=12, color=_MUTED)
 1386  
 1387          # Watermark
 1388          ax.text(1.35, -0.80, "@CoinWatchAlert", ha="right", va="bottom",
 1389                  fontsize=9, color="#555555")
 1390  
 1391          filepath = os.path.join(_CHART_DIR, f"fear_greed_{int(time.time())}.png")
 1392          fig.savefig(filepath, dpi=200, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor=_BG)
 1393          plt.close(fig)
 1394          logger.info("Generated fear/greed gauge: %s", filepath)
 1395          return filepath
 1396  
 1397      except Exception as exc:
 1398          logger.warning("Fear & Greed gauge generation failed: %s", exc)
 1399          return None
 1400  
 1401  
 1402  def generate_geo_chart(story: dict) -> str | None:
 1403      """Pillow-based breaking-news card composited onto the brand template."""
 1404      logger.info("[CHART HIT] generate_geo_chart(%.40s)", story.get("title", "?"))
 1405      return None


================================================================================
  FILE: cmc_monitor.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  CoinMarketCap monitor – polls the CMC free API for top gainers/losers
    3  and market breadth data to generate varied tweet content.
    4  
    5  Free tier: 10,000 credits/month (~333 calls/day).
    6  Sign up at https://coinmarketcap.com/api/ for a free API key.
    7  
    8  Endpoints used (all free tier):
    9    - /v1/cryptocurrency/listings/latest  (1 credit)
   10    - /v1/global-metrics/quotes/latest    (1 credit)
   11  """
   12  from __future__ import annotations
   13  
   14  import logging
   15  import time
   16  
   17  import requests
   18  
   19  import config
   20  import ai_writer
   21  import state
   22  
   23  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   24  
   25  # Cache to avoid tweeting about the same mover twice in a row
   26  _recent_movers: set[str] = set()
   27  _MAX_RECENT_MOVERS = 20
   28  
   29  
   30  # Reverse lookup: symbol → CoinGecko ID (for cross-source dedup)
   31  _SYMBOL_TO_CG = {v: k for k, v in config.COINS.items()}
   32  
   33  
   34  def _symbol_to_coingecko(symbol: str) -> str | None:
   35      """Map a CMC symbol back to a CoinGecko ID, or None if not tracked."""
   36      return _SYMBOL_TO_CG.get(symbol.upper())
   37  
   38  
   39  def _headers() -> dict:
   40      return {
   41          "X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": config.CMC_API_KEY,
   42          "Accept": "application/json",
   43      }
   44  
   45  
   46  def is_available() -> bool:
   47      """Check if CMC API key is configured."""
   48      return bool(config.CMC_API_KEY)
   49  
   50  
   51  def fetch_top_coins() -> list[dict] | None:
   52      """
   53      Fetch top N coins by market cap from CoinMarketCap.
   54      Returns list of coin dicts with price, % changes, market cap, volume.
   55      """
   56      if not config.CMC_API_KEY:
   57          logger.info("CMC_API_KEY not set — CoinMarketCap features disabled.")
   58          return None
   59  
   60      url = f"{config.CMC_BASE}/v1/cryptocurrency/listings/latest"
   61      params = {
   62          "start": 1,
   63          "limit": config.CMC_TOP_N,
   64          "convert": "USD",
   65          "sort": "market_cap",
   66          "sort_dir": "desc",
   67      }
   68  
   69      for attempt in range(3):
   70          try:
   71              resp = requests.get(url, headers=_headers(), params=params, timeout=15)
   72              if resp.status_code == 429:
   73                  wait = 2 ** (attempt + 1)
   74                  logger.warning("CMC rate limited (429), retrying in %ds…", wait)
   75                  time.sleep(wait)
   76                  continue
   77              if resp.status_code == 401:
   78                  logger.error("CMC API key invalid (401). Check CMC_API_KEY in .env")
   79                  return None
   80              resp.raise_for_status()
   81              data = resp.json()
   82              return data.get("data", [])
   83          except requests.RequestException as exc:
   84              logger.warning("CMC fetch failed (attempt %d/3): %s", attempt + 1, exc)
   85              if attempt < 2:
   86                  time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))
   87      return None
   88  
   89  
   90  def fetch_global_metrics() -> dict | None:
   91      """
   92      Fetch global crypto market metrics (total market cap, BTC dominance, etc.).
   93      """
   94      if not config.CMC_API_KEY:
   95          return None
   96  
   97      url = f"{config.CMC_BASE}/v1/global-metrics/quotes/latest"
   98      try:
   99          resp = requests.get(url, headers=_headers(), timeout=15)
  100          resp.raise_for_status()
  101          data = resp.json()
  102          return data.get("data", {})
  103      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  104          logger.warning("CMC global metrics fetch failed: %s", exc)
  105          return None
  106  
  107  
  108  def get_top_gainers(coins: list[dict], n: int = 5) -> list[dict]:
  109      """Return top N gainers by 24h % change from the listings data."""
  110      valid = [c for c in coins if c.get("quote", {}).get("USD", {}).get("percent_change_24h") is not None]
  111      return sorted(valid, key=lambda c: c["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"], reverse=True)[:n]
  112  
  113  
  114  def get_top_losers(coins: list[dict], n: int = 5) -> list[dict]:
  115      """Return top N losers by 24h % change from the listings data."""
  116      valid = [c for c in coins if c.get("quote", {}).get("USD", {}).get("percent_change_24h") is not None]
  117      return sorted(valid, key=lambda c: c["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"])[:n]
  118  
  119  
  120  def get_big_movers(coins: list[dict], threshold: float | None = None) -> list[dict]:
  121      """Return coins moving more than threshold% in 24h (either direction)."""
  122      threshold = threshold or config.CMC_MOVER_THRESHOLD
  123      movers = []
  124      for c in coins:
  125          pct = c.get("quote", {}).get("USD", {}).get("percent_change_24h")
  126          if pct is not None and abs(pct) >= threshold:
  127              movers.append(c)
  128      return sorted(movers, key=lambda c: abs(c["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"]), reverse=True)
  129  
  130  
  131  def get_market_breadth(coins: list[dict]) -> dict:
  132      """Calculate how many coins are green vs red."""
  133      green = red = flat = 0
  134      for c in coins:
  135          pct = c.get("quote", {}).get("USD", {}).get("percent_change_24h")
  136          if pct is None:
  137              continue
  138          if pct > 0.5:
  139              green += 1
  140          elif pct < -0.5:
  141              red += 1
  142          else:
  143              flat += 1
  144      return {"green": green, "red": red, "flat": flat, "total": green + red + flat}
  145  
  146  
  147  # ── Tweet formatters ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  148  
  149  def _fmt_price(val: float) -> str:
  150      if val >= 1000:
  151          return f"${val:,.0f}"
  152      if val >= 1:
  153          return f"${val:,.2f}"
  154      if val >= 0.01:
  155          return f"${val:.4f}"
  156      return f"${val:.6f}"
  157  
  158  
  159  def _fmt_pct(val: float) -> str:
  160      return f"{'+' if val > 0 else ''}{val:.1f}%"
  161  
  162  
  163  def format_movers_tweet(coins: list[dict]) -> str | None:
  164      """Generate a tweet about today's biggest movers from the top 100."""
  165      gainers = get_top_gainers(coins, 3)
  166      losers = get_top_losers(coins, 2)
  167  
  168      if not gainers:
  169          return None
  170  
  171      lines = ["📊 Top 100 movers today:", ""]
  172  
  173      for c in gainers:
  174          sym = c["symbol"]
  175          pct = c["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"]
  176          price = c["quote"]["USD"]["price"]
  177          lines.append(f"→ 🟢 {sym}: {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct)})")
  178  
  179      if losers:
  180          lines.append("")
  181          for c in losers[:2]:
  182              sym = c["symbol"]
  183              pct = c["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"]
  184              price = c["quote"]["USD"]["price"]
  185              lines.append(f"→ 🔴 {sym}: {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct)})")
  186  
  187      breadth = get_market_breadth(coins)
  188      lines.extend(["", f"{breadth['green']}/{breadth['total']} coins green"])
  189  
  190      tweet = "\n".join(lines)
  191      if len(tweet) > 275:
  192          tweet = tweet[:272].rsplit("\n", 1)[0] + "…"
  193      return tweet
  194  
  195  
  196  def format_spotlight_tweet(coin: dict) -> str | None:
  197      """Generate an AI tweet spotlighting a coin having a big day."""
  198      sym = coin["symbol"]
  199      name = coin["name"]
  200      quote = coin.get("quote", {}).get("USD", {})
  201      pct_24h = quote.get("percent_change_24h", 0)
  202      pct_7d = quote.get("percent_change_7d", 0)
  203      price = quote.get("price", 0)
  204      mcap = quote.get("market_cap", 0)
  205      rank = coin.get("cmc_rank", 0)
  206  
  207      # Skip if already tweeted about this coin recently (local set + global cooldown)
  208      if sym in _recent_movers:
  209          return None
  210      # Cross-check with price monitor cooldown — avoid duplicate coverage
  211      coin_gecko_id = _symbol_to_coingecko(sym)
  212      if coin_gecko_id and not state.coin_global_cooldown_ok(coin_gecko_id):
  213          logger.debug("Skipping %s spotlight — global coin cooldown active", sym)
  214          return None
  215      _recent_movers.add(sym)
  216      if len(_recent_movers) > _MAX_RECENT_MOVERS:
  217          _recent_movers.pop()
  218      # Record this coin in global cooldown so price_monitor won't duplicate it
  219      if coin_gecko_id:
  220          state.record_coin_alert(coin_gecko_id)
  221  
  222      direction = "pumping" if pct_24h > 0 else "dumping"
  223      mcap_str = f"${mcap / 1e9:.1f}B" if mcap >= 1e9 else f"${mcap / 1e6:.0f}M"
  224  
  225      if not ai_writer.is_available():
  226          emoji = "🟢" if pct_24h > 0 else "🔴"
  227          return (
  228              f"{emoji} {sym} {_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} today — {_fmt_price(price)}\n"
  229              f"\n"
  230              f"Rank #{rank} | MCap: {mcap_str} | 7d: {_fmt_pct(pct_7d)}"
  231          )
  232  
  233      prompt = f"""Write a tweet about {name} ({sym}) {direction} today.
  234  
  235  {sym}: {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} 24h, {_fmt_pct(pct_7d)} 7d)
  236  Rank: #{rank} | Market cap: {mcap_str}
  237  
  238  The tweet should:
  239  - Lead with {sym}, NOT Bitcoin
  240  - Sound like a trader who just spotted this move
  241  - Include the actual numbers
  242  - Under 275 characters
  243  - NO hashtags
  244  {ai_writer._get_recent_context()}
  245  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  246  
  247      system = """You are @CoinWatchAlert. You cover the WHOLE crypto market, not just Bitcoin. When an altcoin is moving, you call it out fast with data. No hashtags, no filler."""
  248  
  249      return ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  250  
  251  
  252  def format_breadth_tweet(coins: list[dict]) -> str | None:
  253      """Generate a market breadth tweet showing overall market health."""
  254      breadth = get_market_breadth(coins)
  255      if breadth["total"] < 50:
  256          return None
  257  
  258      gainers = get_top_gainers(coins, 1)
  259      losers = get_top_losers(coins, 1)
  260  
  261      if not ai_writer.is_available():
  262          top_g = gainers[0] if gainers else None
  263          top_l = losers[0] if losers else None
  264          lines = [f"📊 Market breadth: {breadth['green']}/{breadth['total']} coins green today", ""]
  265          if top_g:
  266              sym = top_g["symbol"]
  267              pct = top_g["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"]
  268              lines.append(f"→ Leading: {sym} {_fmt_pct(pct)}")
  269          if top_l:
  270              sym = top_l["symbol"]
  271              pct = top_l["quote"]["USD"]["percent_change_24h"]
  272              lines.append(f"→ Lagging: {sym} {_fmt_pct(pct)}")
  273          return "\n".join(lines)
  274  
  275      # Build data for AI
  276      top_3_gain = ", ".join(f"{c['symbol']} {_fmt_pct(c['quote']['USD']['percent_change_24h'])}" for c in gainers[:3])
  277      top_2_lose = ", ".join(f"{c['symbol']} {_fmt_pct(c['quote']['USD']['percent_change_24h'])}" for c in losers[:2])
  278  
  279      prompt = f"""Write a tweet about today's crypto market breadth.
  280  
  281  Market data (top 100 coins):
  282  - Green: {breadth['green']} | Red: {breadth['red']} | Flat: {breadth['flat']}
  283  - Top gainers: {top_3_gain}
  284  - Top losers: {top_2_lose}
  285  
  286  The tweet should:
  287  - Lead with the market breadth stat, not BTC
  288  - Mention 1-2 interesting movers by name
  289  - Give your read on what this means (risk-on? rotation? sell-off?)
  290  - Under 275 characters
  291  - NO hashtags
  292  {ai_writer._get_recent_context()}
  293  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  294  
  295      system = """You are @CoinWatchAlert. You watch the WHOLE market — all 100 top coins — not just BTC. When you talk about market breadth, you read the room. No hashtags, no filler phrases like "Worth noting"."""
  296  
  297      return ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)

================================================================================
  FILE: defi_monitor.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  DeFi monitor – tracks TVL data from DefiLlama (free, no API key).
    3  
    4  Provides:
    5    - Total DeFi TVL and daily change
    6    - Chain TVL rankings (Ethereum, Solana, BSC, etc.)
    7    - Protocol TVL movers (big gains/losses)
    8    - Data for DeFi-focused tweets
    9  """
   10  from __future__ import annotations
   11  
   12  import logging
   13  import time
   14  
   15  import requests
   16  
   17  import ai_writer
   18  
   19  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   20  
   21  _DEFILLAMA_BASE = "https://api.llama.fi"
   22  
   23  # Cache to avoid hammering the API
   24  _cache: dict = {}
   25  _CACHE_TTL = 1800  # 30 minutes
   26  
   27  
   28  def _cached_get(url: str, cache_key: str) -> dict | list | None:
   29      """Fetch with simple cache."""
   30      now = time.time()
   31      if cache_key in _cache and (now - _cache[cache_key]["time"]) < _CACHE_TTL:
   32          return _cache[cache_key]["data"]
   33      try:
   34          resp = requests.get(url, timeout=15)
   35          resp.raise_for_status()
   36          data = resp.json()
   37          _cache[cache_key] = {"data": data, "time": now}
   38          return data
   39      except requests.RequestException as exc:
   40          logger.warning("DefiLlama fetch failed (%s): %s", cache_key, exc)
   41          return None
   42  
   43  
   44  def fetch_total_tvl() -> dict | None:
   45      """Fetch current total DeFi TVL across all chains."""
   46      data = _cached_get(f"{_DEFILLAMA_BASE}/v2/historicalChainTvl", "total_tvl")
   47      if not data or not isinstance(data, list) or len(data) < 2:
   48          return None
   49      current = data[-1]
   50      previous = data[-2]
   51      tvl = current.get("tvl", 0)
   52      prev_tvl = previous.get("tvl", 0)
   53      change = tvl - prev_tvl
   54      pct = (change / prev_tvl * 100) if prev_tvl > 0 else 0
   55      return {
   56          "tvl": tvl,
   57          "change_24h": change,
   58          "pct_24h": pct,
   59      }
   60  
   61  
   62  def fetch_chain_tvls() -> list[dict]:
   63      """Fetch TVL by chain, sorted by TVL descending."""
   64      data = _cached_get(f"{_DEFILLAMA_BASE}/v2/chains", "chain_tvls")
   65      if not data or not isinstance(data, list):
   66          return []
   67      chains = []
   68      for chain in data[:15]:
   69          chains.append({
   70              "name": chain.get("name", "?"),
   71              "tvl": chain.get("tvl", 0),
   72              "change_1d": chain.get("change_1d", 0),
   73              "change_7d": chain.get("change_7d", 0),
   74          })
   75      chains.sort(key=lambda c: c["tvl"], reverse=True)
   76      return chains
   77  
   78  
   79  def fetch_protocol_movers() -> list[dict]:
   80      """Find protocols with the biggest TVL changes (up or down)."""
   81      data = _cached_get(f"{_DEFILLAMA_BASE}/protocols", "protocols")
   82      if not data or not isinstance(data, list):
   83          return []
   84  
   85      protocols = []
   86      for p in data[:200]:
   87          tvl = p.get("tvl", 0)
   88          change = p.get("change_1d", 0)
   89          if tvl >= 50_000_000 and abs(change or 0) >= 5.0:  # $50M+ TVL, 5%+ move
   90              protocols.append({
   91                  "name": p.get("name", "?"),
   92                  "symbol": p.get("symbol", "?").upper() if p.get("symbol") else "?",
   93                  "tvl": tvl,
   94                  "change_1d": change or 0,
   95                  "chain": p.get("chain", "Multi"),
   96                  "category": p.get("category", ""),
   97              })
   98  
   99      protocols.sort(key=lambda p: abs(p["change_1d"]), reverse=True)
  100      return protocols[:5]
  101  
  102  
  103  def fetch_dex_volumes() -> dict | None:
  104      """Fetch 24h DEX volume data."""
  105      data = _cached_get(f"{_DEFILLAMA_BASE}/overview/dexs?excludeTotalDataChart=true&excludeTotalDataChartBreakdown=true&dataType=dailyVolume", "dex_volumes")
  106      if not data:
  107          return None
  108      total = data.get("totalDataChart", [])
  109      total_24h = data.get("total24h", 0)
  110      total_change = data.get("change_1d", 0)
  111  
  112      # Top DEXs
  113      protocols = data.get("protocols", [])
  114      top_dexes = []
  115      for p in sorted(protocols, key=lambda x: x.get("total24h", 0) or 0, reverse=True)[:5]:
  116          top_dexes.append({
  117              "name": p.get("name", "?"),
  118              "volume_24h": p.get("total24h", 0) or 0,
  119              "change_1d": p.get("change_1d", 0) or 0,
  120          })
  121  
  122      return {
  123          "total_24h": total_24h,
  124          "change_1d": total_change,
  125          "top_dexes": top_dexes,
  126      }
  127  
  128  
  129  # ── Tweet formatting ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  130  
  131  def generate_defi_tweet() -> str | None:
  132      """
  133      Generate a DeFi-focused tweet using TVL data.
  134      Picks the most interesting angle from available data.
  135      """
  136      import random
  137  
  138      # Gather data
  139      total = fetch_total_tvl()
  140      chains = fetch_chain_tvls()
  141      movers = fetch_protocol_movers()
  142  
  143      if not total and not chains:
  144          return None
  145  
  146      # Pick an angle
  147      angles = []
  148      if total:
  149          angles.append("tvl_overview")
  150      if chains and len(chains) >= 3:
  151          angles.append("chain_comparison")
  152      if movers:
  153          angles.append("protocol_mover")
  154  
  155      if not angles:
  156          return None
  157  
  158      angle = random.choice(angles)
  159  
  160      if angle == "tvl_overview" and total:
  161          tvl_b = total["tvl"] / 1e9
  162          pct = total["pct_24h"]
  163  
  164          if ai_writer.is_available():
  165              prompt = f"""Write a tweet about DeFi TVL.
  166  
  167  Total DeFi TVL: ${tvl_b:.1f}B ({pct:+.1f}% 24h)
  168  
  169  DO NOT mention Bitcoin. This is about DeFi.
  170  Comment on what TVL levels signal — is money flowing in or out?
  171  Keep it under 275 chars. NO hashtags.
  172  
  173  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  174              system = "You are @CoinWatchAlert. You track DeFi data closely. No hashtags."
  175              ai_tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  176              if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  177                  return ai_tweet
  178  
  179          emoji = "🟢" if pct > 0 else "🔴"
  180          return (
  181              f"📊 DeFi TVL: ${tvl_b:.1f}B ({pct:+.1f}% 24h)\n\n"
  182              f"{emoji} {'Capital flowing in — bullish signal for DeFi.' if pct > 0 else 'Capital leaving — risk-off mode in DeFi.'}"
  183          )
  184  
  185      elif angle == "chain_comparison" and chains:
  186          top3 = chains[:3]
  187          chain_lines = ""
  188          for c in top3:
  189              tvl_b = c["tvl"] / 1e9
  190              change = c.get("change_1d", 0)
  191              chain_lines += f"  {c['name']}: ${tvl_b:.1f}B ({change:+.1f}%)\n"
  192  
  193          if ai_writer.is_available():
  194              prompt = f"""Write a tweet comparing chain TVLs.
  195  
  196  Top chains by TVL:
  197  {chain_lines}
  198  
  199  Compare the chains — who's gaining, who's losing. Make it interesting.
  200  DO NOT mention Bitcoin price. This is about DeFi/L1 competition.
  201  Keep it under 275 chars. NO hashtags.
  202  
  203  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  204              system = "You are @CoinWatchAlert. You track the L1 war through TVL data."
  205              ai_tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  206              if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  207                  return ai_tweet
  208  
  209          lines = ["📊 Chain TVL check:", ""]
  210          for c in top3:
  211              tvl_b = c["tvl"] / 1e9
  212              lines.append(f"→ {c['name']}: ${tvl_b:.1f}B")
  213          return "\n".join(lines)
  214  
  215      elif angle == "protocol_mover" and movers:
  216          mover = movers[0]
  217          tvl_m = mover["tvl"] / 1e6
  218          change = mover["change_1d"]
  219          emoji = "🟢" if change > 0 else "🔴"
  220  
  221          if ai_writer.is_available():
  222              prompt = f"""Write a tweet about a DeFi protocol making a big move.
  223  
  224  Protocol: {mover['name']} ({mover['symbol']})
  225  TVL: ${tvl_m:.0f}M ({change:+.1f}% 24h)
  226  Chain: {mover['chain']}
  227  Category: {mover['category']}
  228  
  229  Why might TVL be surging or dropping? Speculation is fine.
  230  Keep it under 275 chars. NO hashtags.
  231  
  232  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  233              system = "You are @CoinWatchAlert. You spot DeFi protocol moves before CT catches on."
  234              ai_tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  235              if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  236                  return ai_tweet
  237  
  238          return (
  239              f"📊 {mover['name']} ({mover['symbol']}) TVL {change:+.1f}% today\n\n"
  240              f"{emoji} ${tvl_m:.0f}M locked on {mover['chain']}\n\n"
  241              f"🎯 {'Big inflows — someone knows something.' if change > 0 else 'Capital exiting — watch for more.'}"
  242          )
  243  
  244      return None


================================================================================
  FILE: delete_ai_refusal_tweets.py
================================================================================

    1  #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2  """Delete tweets containing AI refusal language.
    3  
    4  Run on production machine:
    5      python3 delete_ai_refusal_tweets.py
    6  
    7  Fetches recent tweets from the authenticated account and deletes any
    8  that contain leaked AI refusal phrases.
    9  """
   10  import tweepy
   11  import config
   12  
   13  AI_REFUSAL_PHRASES = [
   14      "i need to",
   15      "i appreciate",
   16      "i cannot",
   17      "i'm unable",
   18      "as an ai",
   19      "my core directive",
   20      "conflicts with",
   21      "i must decline",
   22      "i can't generate",
   23      "i can't create",
   24      "against my guidelines",
   25      "i'm not able",
   26  ]
   27  
   28  def main():
   29      client = tweepy.Client(
   30          bearer_token=config.TWITTER_BEARER_TOKEN,
   31          consumer_key=config.TWITTER_API_KEY,
   32          consumer_secret=config.TWITTER_API_SECRET,
   33          access_token=config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN,
   34          access_token_secret=config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN_SECRET,
   35      )
   36  
   37      me = client.get_me()
   38      uid = me.data.id
   39      print(f"Authenticated as: {me.data.username} (ID: {uid})")
   40  
   41      tweets = client.get_users_tweets(uid, max_results=100, tweet_fields=["created_at", "text"])
   42      if not tweets.data:
   43          print("No tweets found.")
   44          return
   45  
   46      deleted = 0
   47      for t in tweets.data:
   48          text_lower = t.text.lower()
   49          if any(phrase in text_lower for phrase in AI_REFUSAL_PHRASES):
   50              print(f"\n  DELETING: {t.id}")
   51              print(f"  Text: {t.text[:120]}")
   52              print(f"  Created: {t.created_at}")
   53              try:
   54                  client.delete_tweet(t.id)
   55                  deleted += 1
   56                  print("  ✓ Deleted")
   57              except Exception as exc:
   58                  print(f"  ✗ Failed: {exc}")
   59  
   60      print(f"\nDone. Deleted {deleted} tweet(s) containing AI refusal text.")
   61  
   62  
   63  if __name__ == "__main__":
   64      main()


================================================================================
  FILE: exchange.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  exchange.py – Crypto.com Exchange V1 API wrapper.
    3  
    4  Public endpoints (no auth):
    5    - get_candlesticks(instrument_name, timeframe, depth)
    6  
    7  Private endpoints (HMAC-SHA256 auth with CDX_API_KEY / CDX_API_SECRET):
    8    - get_balance()
    9    - place_order(instrument_name, side, quantity, order_type, price)
   10    - get_open_orders(instrument_name)
   11    - cancel_order(order_id)
   12  """
   13  from __future__ import annotations
   14  
   15  import hashlib
   16  import hmac
   17  import json
   18  import logging
   19  import time
   20  
   21  import requests
   22  
   23  import config
   24  
   25  logger = logging.getLogger("exchange")
   26  
   27  _SESSION = requests.Session()
   28  _SESSION.headers.update({"Content-Type": "application/json"})
   29  
   30  # ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   31  
   32  def _nonce() -> int:
   33      return int(time.time() * 1000)
   34  
   35  
   36  def _sign(method: str, request_id: int, params: dict, nonce: int) -> str:
   37      """
   38      Crypto.com Exchange HMAC-SHA256 signature.
   39  
   40      sig = HMAC_SHA256(api_secret,
   41                        method + str(request_id) + api_key + param_str + str(nonce))
   42      where param_str = sorted key+value pairs concatenated as a single string.
   43      """
   44      param_str = "".join(
   45          f"{k}{v}"
   46          for k, v in sorted(params.items())
   47      )
   48      payload = method + str(request_id) + config.CDX_API_KEY + param_str + str(nonce)
   49      return hmac.new(
   50          config.CDX_API_SECRET.encode(),
   51          payload.encode(),
   52          hashlib.sha256,
   53      ).hexdigest()
   54  
   55  
   56  def _public_get(endpoint: str, params: dict | None = None) -> dict | None:
   57      """GET a public (unauthenticated) endpoint."""
   58      url = f"{config.CDX_BASE}/{endpoint}"
   59      for attempt in range(3):
   60          try:
   61              resp = _SESSION.get(url, params=params or {}, timeout=15)
   62              if resp.status_code == 429:
   63                  wait = 2 ** (attempt + 1)
   64                  logger.warning("Rate limited (429) on %s — retrying in %ds", endpoint, wait)
   65                  time.sleep(wait)
   66                  continue
   67              resp.raise_for_status()
   68              return resp.json()
   69          except requests.RequestException as exc:
   70              logger.error("Request failed for %s: %s", endpoint, exc)
   71              if attempt < 2:
   72                  time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))
   73      return None
   74  
   75  
   76  def _private_post(method: str, params: dict | None = None) -> dict | None:
   77      """POST a private (authenticated) endpoint."""
   78      if not config.CDX_API_KEY or not config.CDX_API_SECRET:
   79          logger.error("CDX_API_KEY / CDX_API_SECRET not set — cannot call private endpoint %s", method)
   80          return None
   81  
   82      url = f"{config.CDX_BASE}/{method}"
   83      request_id = int(time.time() * 1000)
   84      nonce = _nonce()
   85      params = params or {}
   86  
   87      sig = _sign(method.split("/")[-1], request_id, params, nonce)
   88  
   89      body = {
   90          "id": request_id,
   91          "method": method,
   92          "api_key": config.CDX_API_KEY,
   93          "params": params,
   94          "nonce": nonce,
   95          "sig": sig,
   96      }
   97  
   98      for attempt in range(3):
   99          try:
  100              resp = _SESSION.post(url, data=json.dumps(body), timeout=15)
  101              if resp.status_code == 429:
  102                  wait = 2 ** (attempt + 1)
  103                  logger.warning("Rate limited (429) on %s — retrying in %ds", method, wait)
  104                  time.sleep(wait)
  105                  continue
  106              resp.raise_for_status()
  107              data = resp.json()
  108              if data.get("code") != 0:
  109                  logger.error("API error on %s: code=%s msg=%s",
  110                               method, data.get("code"), data.get("message"))
  111                  return None
  112              return data
  113          except requests.RequestException as exc:
  114              logger.error("Request failed for %s: %s", method, exc)
  115              if attempt < 2:
  116                  time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))
  117      return None
  118  
  119  
  120  # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  121  
  122  def get_candlesticks(
  123      instrument_name: str,
  124      timeframe: str = "15m",
  125      depth: int = 200,
  126  ) -> list[dict]:
  127      """
  128      Fetch OHLCV candles from the public endpoint.
  129  
  130      Returns list of dicts with keys: t (timestamp ms), o, h, l, c, v
  131      Ordered oldest → newest.
  132      """
  133      data = _public_get(
  134          "public/get-candlestick",
  135          params={
  136              "instrument_name": instrument_name,
  137              "timeframe": timeframe,
  138              "depth": depth,
  139          },
  140      )
  141      if not data:
  142          return []
  143      result = data.get("result", {})
  144      candles = result.get("data", [])
  145      if not isinstance(candles, list):
  146          return []
  147      # Sort oldest first
  148      candles.sort(key=lambda c: c.get("t", 0))
  149      return candles
  150  
  151  
  152  # ── Private API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  153  
  154  def get_balance() -> dict[str, float]:
  155      """
  156      Fetch spot account balances.
  157      Returns dict of currency → available amount.
  158      """
  159      data = _private_post("private/get-account-summary")
  160      if not data:
  161          return {}
  162      accounts = data.get("result", {}).get("accounts", [])
  163      return {
  164          acct["currency"]: float(acct.get("available", 0))
  165          for acct in accounts
  166          if float(acct.get("available", 0)) > 0
  167      }
  168  
  169  
  170  def place_order(
  171      instrument_name: str,
  172      side: str,           # "BUY" or "SELL"
  173      quantity: float,
  174      order_type: str = "MARKET",
  175      price: float | None = None,
  176  ) -> dict | None:
  177      """
  178      Place an order.  Returns the API response or None on failure.
  179      side: "BUY" | "SELL"
  180      order_type: "MARKET" | "LIMIT"
  181      """
  182      params: dict = {
  183          "instrument_name": instrument_name,
  184          "side": side.upper(),
  185          "type": order_type.upper(),
  186          "quantity": str(quantity),
  187      }
  188      if order_type.upper() == "LIMIT" and price is not None:
  189          params["price"] = str(price)
  190  
  191      return _private_post("private/create-order", params)
  192  
  193  
  194  def get_open_orders(instrument_name: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
  195      """Return list of open orders, optionally filtered by instrument."""
  196      params: dict = {}
  197      if instrument_name:
  198          params["instrument_name"] = instrument_name
  199      data = _private_post("private/get-open-orders", params)
  200      if not data:
  201          return []
  202      return data.get("result", {}).get("order_list", [])
  203  
  204  
  205  def cancel_order(order_id: str, instrument_name: str) -> bool:
  206      """Cancel an order by ID.  Returns True if successful."""
  207      data = _private_post("private/cancel-order", {
  208          "order_id": order_id,
  209          "instrument_name": instrument_name,
  210      })
  211      return data is not None


================================================================================
  FILE: fear_greed.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Fear & Greed Index monitor – polls the alternative.me API (free, no key needed).
    3  
    4  Posts the current index value with historical comparison.
    5  API docs: https://alternative.me/crypto/fear-and-greed-index/
    6  """
    7  from __future__ import annotations
    8  
    9  import logging
   10  import time
   11  
   12  import requests
   13  
   14  import ai_writer
   15  import chart_generator
   16  import state
   17  
   18  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   19  
   20  _API_URL = "https://api.alternative.me/fng/"
   21  
   22  # Minimum gap between Fear & Greed posts (6 hours)
   23  _MIN_POST_GAP = 6 * 3600
   24  
   25  
   26  def fetch_fear_greed() -> dict | None:
   27      """
   28      Fetch the current Fear & Greed Index.
   29      Returns dict with keys: value, value_classification, timestamp
   30      Also fetches yesterday's value for comparison.
   31      """
   32      for attempt in range(3):
   33          try:
   34              resp = requests.get(
   35                  _API_URL,
   36                  params={"limit": 2, "format": "json"},
   37                  timeout=15,
   38              )
   39              if resp.status_code == 429:
   40                  time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))
   41                  continue
   42              resp.raise_for_status()
   43              data = resp.json().get("data", [])
   44              if not data:
   45                  return None
   46              result = {
   47                  "value": int(data[0]["value"]),
   48                  "classification": data[0]["value_classification"],
   49                  "timestamp": int(data[0]["timestamp"]),
   50              }
   51              if len(data) > 1:
   52                  result["yesterday_value"] = int(data[1]["value"])
   53                  result["yesterday_classification"] = data[1]["value_classification"]
   54              return result
   55          except (requests.RequestException, KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
   56              logger.warning("Fear & Greed fetch failed (attempt %d/3): %s", attempt + 1, exc)
   57              if attempt < 2:
   58                  time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))
   59      return None
   60  
   61  
   62  def _classification_emoji(classification: str) -> str:
   63      """Map classification to emoji."""
   64      mapping = {
   65          "Extreme Fear": "😱",
   66          "Fear": "😨",
   67          "Neutral": "😐",
   68          "Greed": "🤑",
   69          "Extreme Greed": "🤩",
   70      }
   71      return mapping.get(classification, "📊")
   72  
   73  
   74  def _value_bar(value: int) -> str:
   75      """Create a simple visual bar for the index value."""
   76      filled = value // 10
   77      empty = 10 - filled
   78      return "█" * filled + "░" * empty
   79  
   80  
   81  def should_post(value: int) -> bool:
   82      """Check if we should post (avoid duplicate/too-frequent posts).
   83  
   84      Guards:
   85        1. Time-based: at least 6 hours since last Fear & Greed post (persisted to disk).
   86        2. Value-based: skip if exact same value was posted last time.
   87      """
   88  
   89      # Check persistent cooldown (survives restarts)
   90      last_ts = state.get_fear_greed_last_posted_ts()
   91      if last_ts and (time.time() - last_ts) < _MIN_POST_GAP:
   92          logger.info("Fear & Greed cooldown active (%.0f min remaining), skipping.",
   93                       (_MIN_POST_GAP - (time.time() - last_ts)) / 60)
   94          return False
   95  
   96      last_val = state.get_fear_greed_last_value()
   97      if last_val is not None and last_val == value:
   98          return False
   99  
  100      return True
  101  
  102  
  103  def record_posted(data: dict) -> None:
  104      """Record that a Fear & Greed tweet was posted (persisted to disk)."""
  105      state.record_fear_greed_posted(data["value"])
  106  
  107  
  108  def format_fear_greed_tweet(data: dict) -> tuple[str, str | None] | None:
  109      """Generate a Fear & Greed Index tweet and gauge chart image.
  110  
  111      Returns (tweet_text, img_path) on success, or None on failure.
  112      img_path may be None if chart generation fails.
  113      """
  114      value = data["value"]
  115      classification = data["classification"]
  116      yesterday = data.get("yesterday_value")
  117  
  118      # Try AI first
  119      if ai_writer.is_available():
  120          change_str = ""
  121          if yesterday is not None:
  122              diff = value - yesterday
  123              change_str = f"Yesterday: {yesterday} ({data.get('yesterday_classification', '')}). Change: {diff:+d} points."
  124  
  125          prompt = f"""Write a punchy 1–2 line tweet reacting to the Crypto Fear & Greed Index.
  126  
  127  Current reading: {value}/100 — {classification}
  128  {change_str}
  129  
  130  Rules:
  131  - The gauge image already shows the number and label — do NOT repeat them in the text
  132  - Go straight to what this reading MEANS, not what it IS
  133  - Data without interpretation is noise. Don't say "Fear is at 14" — say what that implies
  134  - Extreme fear doesn't mark bottoms. It marks opportunity windows. Frame it analytically, not emotionally.
  135  - If extreme (below 25 or above 75), make the contrarian call directly
  136  - Structure: observation → what it means → implication
  137  - Declarative, no hedging ('could', 'might', 'may')
  138  - Maximum 2 lines, max 180 characters total
  139  - No questions, no hashtags, no disclaimers
  140  - Emojis only from: 📉 🚀 ⚡ 👀
  141  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  142  
  143          system = """You are @CoinWatchAlert. You read the Fear & Greed Index as a contrarian signal — analytical, not emotional. Extreme readings mark opportunity windows, not certainties. Calm, confident, slightly cryptic. Prioritise: clear takeaway, strong hook, information advantage. Avoid: obvious statements, neutral summaries, raw data without interpretation."""
  144  
  145          tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  146          if tweet and len(tweet) <= 180:
  147              img_path = chart_generator.generate_fear_greed_gauge(value, classification)
  148              return tweet, img_path
  149  
  150      # Template fallback — gauge image shows the number, text is commentary only
  151      if value <= 20:
  152          tweet = "Extreme fear. Historically this is where BTC cycle lows form. 🚀"
  153      elif value <= 30:
  154          tweet = "Fear this deep has preceded every major BTC recovery. 🚀"
  155      elif value >= 80:
  156          tweet = "Extreme greed. Corrections follow readings like this. 📉"
  157      elif value >= 70:
  158          tweet = "Greed building. Overextension risk is real. 👀"
  159      elif value >= 50:
  160          tweet = "Market leaning greedy. Stay sharp. 👀"
  161      else:
  162          tweet = "Sentiment cooling off. Smart money watches for opportunity. ⚡"
  163  
  164      if len(tweet) > 180:
  165          tweet = tweet[:177].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
  166      img_path = chart_generator.generate_fear_greed_gauge(value, classification)
  167      return tweet, img_path


================================================================================
  FILE: gematria_scanner.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Gematria Daily Intelligence Dashboard
    3  Multi-source news scanning, signal phase classification, pattern detection,
    4  cross-source matching, and trend analysis.
    5  """
    6  
    7  import csv
    8  import os
    9  import random
   10  import string
   11  import urllib.request
   12  import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
   13  from collections import Counter
   14  from datetime import date, timedelta
   15  
   16  import streamlit as st
   17  import pandas as pd
   18  
   19  # --- Config ---
   20  
   21  LOG_FILE = "gematria_log.csv"
   22  
   23  RSS_FEEDS = {
   24      "BBC":        "http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/rss.xml",
   25      "Reuters":    "https://feeds.reuters.com/reuters/topNews",
   26      "Sky News":   "https://feeds.skynews.com/feeds/rss/home.xml",
   27      "Al Jazeera": "https://www.aljazeera.com/xml/rss/all.xml",
   28      "RT":         "https://www.rt.com/rss/",
   29      "Daily Mail": "https://www.dailymail.co.uk/articles.rss",
   30  }
   31  
   32  _COUNTRY_KW = {"us", "iran", "israel", "uk", "russia", "china", "ukraine", "gaza", "india", "syria"}
   33  _TOPIC_KW = {"war", "deal", "strike", "ceasefire", "attack", "collapse", "summit",
   34               "bomb", "missile", "sanctions", "trade", "crisis", "election", "killed"}
   35  _TRACKED_KW = _COUNTRY_KW | _TOPIC_KW
   36  
   37  # --- Gematria Functions ---
   38  
   39  def ordinal_gematria(text):
   40      total = 0
   41      for char in text.upper():
   42          if 'A' <= char <= 'Z':
   43              total += ord(char) - ord('A') + 1
   44      return total
   45  
   46  
   47  def reduce_number(n):
   48      while n >= 10:
   49          n = sum(int(d) for d in str(n))
   50      return n
   51  
   52  
   53  def date_sum(d):
   54      return sum(int(ch) for ch in str(d) if ch.isdigit())
   55  
   56  
   57  def signal_score(ordinal, reduced, scan_date, log_df):
   58      score = 0
   59      reasons = []
   60      matches = 0
   61  
   62      if not log_df.empty and len(log_df) >= 5:
   63          total = len(log_df)
   64          count = (log_df["ordinal"] == ordinal).sum()
   65          freq = count / total
   66          pct = f"{freq:.0%}"
   67          if freq < 0.05:
   68              score += 30; matches += 1
   69              reasons.append(f"Ordinal {ordinal} very rare ({pct}) +30")
   70          elif freq < 0.10:
   71              score += 20; matches += 1
   72              reasons.append(f"Ordinal {ordinal} rare ({pct}) +20")
   73          elif freq < 0.20:
   74              score += 10
   75              reasons.append(f"Ordinal {ordinal} uncommon ({pct}) +10")
   76          elif freq < 0.30:
   77              score += 5
   78              reasons.append(f"Ordinal {ordinal} moderate ({pct}) +5")
   79          else:
   80              reasons.append(f"Ordinal {ordinal} common ({pct}) +0")
   81      else:
   82          score += 15
   83          reasons.append("Insufficient data — neutral +15")
   84  
   85      ds = date_sum(scan_date)
   86      if ordinal == ds:
   87          score += 25; matches += 1
   88          reasons.append(f"Ordinal matches date sum {ds} +25")
   89      else:
   90          reasons.append(f"Ordinal != date sum {ds} +0")
   91  
   92      ds_r = reduce_number(ds)
   93      if reduced == ds_r:
   94          score += 30; matches += 1
   95          reasons.append(f"Reduced {reduced} matches date reduced {ds_r} +30")
   96      else:
   97          reasons.append(f"Reduced {reduced} != date reduced {ds_r} +0")
   98  
   99      if matches >= 2:
  100          score += 15
  101          reasons.append(f"Multi-signal bonus ({matches} matched) +15")
  102      if matches == 0:
  103          score -= 10
  104          reasons.append("No matches -10")
  105  
  106      score = max(0, min(score, 100))
  107      if score >= 70:
  108          label = "HIGH SIGNAL"
  109      elif score >= 40:
  110          label = "MEDIUM SIGNAL"
  111      else:
  112          label = "LOW SIGNAL"
  113      return score, label, reasons
  114  
  115  
  116  # --- CSV ---
  117  
  118  def save_entry(text, scan_date, ordinal, reduced, score, source="manual"):
  119      clean = str(text).strip().replace("\n", " ").replace("\r", "")
  120      if len(clean) > 300 or ordinal > 5000 or not clean:
  121          return
  122      file_exists = os.path.exists(LOG_FILE)
  123      with open(LOG_FILE, "a", newline="") as f:
  124          writer = csv.writer(f)
  125          if not file_exists:
  126              writer.writerow(["text", "date", "ordinal", "reduced", "score", "source"])
  127          writer.writerow([clean, scan_date, ordinal, reduced, score, source])
  128  
  129  
  130  def load_log():
  131      if os.path.exists(LOG_FILE):
  132          df = pd.read_csv(LOG_FILE)
  133          if "source" not in df.columns:
  134              df["source"] = "unknown"
  135          df = df[df["text"].astype(str).str.len() <= 500]
  136          return df
  137      return pd.DataFrame(columns=["text", "date", "ordinal", "reduced", "score", "source"])
  138  
  139  
  140  # --- RSS ---
  141  
  142  def fetch_rss_headlines(url, n=15):
  143      try:
  144          req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "GematriaScanner/2.0"})
  145          with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
  146              tree = ET.parse(resp)
  147          headlines = []
  148          for item in tree.getroot().iter("item"):
  149              title = item.find("title")
  150              if title is not None and title.text:
  151                  t = title.text.strip()
  152                  if len(t) > 10 and len(t) <= 300:
  153                      headlines.append(t)
  154                      if len(headlines) >= n:
  155                          break
  156          return headlines
  157      except Exception:
  158          return []
  159  
  160  
  161  def fetch_all_feeds():
  162      all_headlines = {}
  163      for name, url in RSS_FEEDS.items():
  164          hlines = fetch_rss_headlines(url, n=15)
  165          if hlines:
  166              all_headlines[name] = hlines
  167      return all_headlines
  168  
  169  
  170  def auto_scan_today():
  171      """Fetch all feeds and scan if today's data is not already in CSV."""
  172      today_str = str(date.today())
  173      log = load_log()
  174      today_entries = log[log["date"].astype(str) == today_str]
  175      if len(today_entries) >= 50:
  176          return log, False  # already scanned today
  177  
  178      all_headlines = fetch_all_feeds()
  179      if not all_headlines:
  180          return log, False
  181  
  182      existing_log = load_log()
  183      # Collect today's existing headlines for dedup
  184      today_texts = set()
  185      if not existing_log.empty:
  186          today_rows = existing_log[existing_log["date"].astype(str) == today_str]
  187          today_texts = set(today_rows["text"].astype(str).str.strip().tolist())
  188  
  189      for source, headlines in all_headlines.items():
  190          for headline in headlines:
  191              clean = headline.strip()
  192              if clean in today_texts:
  193                  continue  # skip duplicate
  194              ov = ordinal_gematria(clean)
  195              rv = reduce_number(ov)
  196              sc, _, _ = signal_score(ov, rv, date.today(), existing_log)
  197              save_entry(clean, date.today(), ov, rv, sc, source)
  198              today_texts.add(clean)
  199              existing_log = load_log()
  200  
  201      return load_log(), True
  202  
  203  
  204  # --- Analysis Functions ---
  205  
  206  def _headline_words(text):
  207      words = str(text).lower().replace("-", " ").split()
  208      return {w.strip(".,;:!?\"'()[]") for w in words}
  209  
  210  
  211  def extract_entities(headlines, top_n=5):
  212      counts = Counter()
  213      for h in headlines:
  214          words = _headline_words(h)
  215          for kw in _TRACKED_KW:
  216              if kw in words:
  217                  counts[kw] += 1
  218      return counts.most_common(top_n)
  219  
  220  
  221  def detect_clusters(headlines):
  222      groups = Counter()
  223      for h in headlines:
  224          words = _headline_words(h)
  225          found = sorted(w for w in words if w in _TRACKED_KW)
  226          if found:
  227              groups["-".join(found[:2])] += 1
  228      return [{"topic": t, "count": c} for t, c in groups.most_common(5)]
  229  
  230  
  231  def compute_phase(df):
  232      if df.empty:
  233          return "BASELINE", "No data available"
  234      ds = date_sum(date.today())
  235      ds_r = reduce_number(ds)
  236      high_count = int((df["score"] >= 70).sum())
  237      date_matches = int(((df["ordinal"] == ds) | (df["reduced"] == ds_r)).sum())
  238      red_counts = df["reduced"].value_counts()
  239      dom_red_pct = red_counts.iloc[0] / len(df) if not red_counts.empty else 0
  240  
  241      if date_matches >= 2 and dom_red_pct >= 0.15:
  242          return "IMMINENT", "Multiple date matches and structural alignment detected"
  243      if date_matches >= 1 and high_count >= 1:
  244          return "TRIGGER", "Date match detected with HIGH signal — monitor closely"
  245      if dom_red_pct >= 0.15 and high_count >= 2 and date_matches == 0:
  246          return "FORMATION", "Alignment building — no trigger yet"
  247      return "BASELINE", "No significant phase pattern detected"
  248  
  249  
  250  def find_cross_source_matches(df):
  251      """Find headlines from different sources sharing ordinal or reduced on same day."""
  252      matches = []
  253      if df.empty or "source" not in df.columns:
  254          return matches
  255      for d in df["date"].unique():
  256          day_df = df[df["date"] == d]
  257          if day_df["source"].nunique() < 2:
  258              continue
  259          for val_col in ["ordinal", "reduced"]:
  260              val_counts = day_df.groupby(val_col)["source"].nunique()
  261              shared_vals = val_counts[val_counts >= 2].index.tolist()
  262              for v in shared_vals:
  263                  rows = day_df[day_df[val_col] == v]
  264                  sources = rows["source"].unique().tolist()
  265                  texts = rows["text"].head(3).tolist()
  266                  matches.append({
  267                      "date": d,
  268                      "type": val_col,
  269                      "value": int(v),
  270                      "sources": sources,
  271                      "headlines": texts,
  272                  })
  273      return matches[:10]
  274  
  275  
  276  # --- Control Group ---
  277  
  278  def run_control_group(n=100, word_length=6):
  279      results = []
  280      for _ in range(n):
  281          word = ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_lowercase, k=word_length))
  282          ov = ordinal_gematria(word)
  283          rv = reduce_number(ov)
  284          results.append({"word": word, "ordinal": ov, "reduced": rv})
  285      return pd.DataFrame(results)
  286  
  287  
  288  # ===================== STREAMLIT APP =====================
  289  
  290  st.set_page_config(page_title="Gematria Intelligence Dashboard", layout="wide")
  291  st.title("Gematria Intelligence Dashboard")
  292  
  293  # --- Auto-scan on load ---
  294  with st.spinner("Scanning headlines..."):
  295      log_df, just_scanned = auto_scan_today()
  296  today_str = str(date.today())
  297  today_df = log_df[log_df["date"].astype(str) == today_str] if not log_df.empty else log_df
  298  
  299  # --- Signal Intelligence Panel (always visible) ---
  300  if not log_df.empty:
  301      src = today_df if not today_df.empty else log_df
  302      phase, alert_msg = compute_phase(src)
  303      headlines = src["text"].dropna().tolist()
  304      entities = extract_entities(headlines, top_n=5)
  305      clusters = detect_clusters(headlines)
  306      red_counts = src["reduced"].value_counts()
  307      dom_red = int(red_counts.index[0]) if not red_counts.empty else 0
  308      dom_red_pct = red_counts.iloc[0] / len(src) if not red_counts.empty else 0
  309  
  310      # Phase alert
  311      if phase == "IMMINENT":
  312          st.error(f"SIGNAL PHASE: {phase} — {alert_msg}")
  313      elif phase == "TRIGGER":
  314          st.warning(f"SIGNAL PHASE: {phase} — {alert_msg}")
  315      elif phase == "FORMATION":
  316          st.info(f"SIGNAL PHASE: {phase} — {alert_msg}")
  317      else:
  318          st.success(f"SIGNAL PHASE: {phase} — {alert_msg}")
  319  
  320      # Metrics row
  321      m1, m2, m3, m4 = st.columns(4)
  322      m1.metric("Dominant Reduced", f"{dom_red} ({dom_red_pct:.0%})")
  323      m2.metric("Headlines Scanned", len(src))
  324      m3.metric("HIGH Signals", int((src["score"] >= 70).sum()))
  325      m4.metric("Sources Active", src["source"].nunique() if "source" in src.columns else 1)
  326  
  327      # Entities + Clusters + Source Breakdown
  328      ec1, ec2, ec3 = st.columns(3)
  329      with ec1:
  330          st.write("**Top Entities**")
  331          if entities:
  332              for kw, cnt in entities:
  333                  st.write(f"- {kw}: {cnt}")
  334          else:
  335              st.write("- None detected")
  336      with ec2:
  337          st.write("**Clusters**")
  338          if clusters:
  339              for cl in clusters:
  340                  st.write(f"- {cl['topic']}: {cl['count']}")
  341          else:
  342              st.write("- None detected")
  343      with ec3:
  344          st.write("**Source Breakdown**")
  345          if "source" in src.columns:
  346              for s, c in src["source"].value_counts().items():
  347                  st.write(f"- {s}: {c}")
  348  
  349      st.divider()
  350  
  351  if just_scanned:
  352      st.caption("Today's headlines were auto-scanned on load.")
  353  
  354  # --- Tabs ---
  355  tab_today, tab_history, tab_patterns, tab_control = st.tabs(
  356      ["Today's Scan", "History", "Patterns", "Control Group"]
  357  )
  358  
  359  # ===================== TAB 1: TODAY'S SCAN =====================
  360  with tab_today:
  361      st.header("Today's Scan")
  362  
  363      # Manual scan
  364      col1, col2 = st.columns([3, 1])
  365      with col1:
  366          text_input = st.text_input("Manual scan (headline, name, phrase):", key="scan_text")
  367      with col2:
  368          date_input = st.date_input("Date:", value=date.today(), key="scan_date")
  369  
  370      if st.button("Scan", key="scan_btn"):
  371          if text_input.strip():
  372              ov = ordinal_gematria(text_input)
  373              rv = reduce_number(ov)
  374              existing = load_log()
  375              sc, label, reasons = signal_score(ov, rv, date_input, existing)
  376              save_entry(text_input, date_input, ov, rv, sc, "manual")
  377              c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
  378              c1.metric("Ordinal", ov)
  379              c2.metric("Reduced", rv)
  380              c3.metric("Score", f"{sc}/100")
  381              if sc >= 70:
  382                  st.error(f"**{label}**")
  383              elif sc >= 40:
  384                  st.warning(f"**{label}**")
  385              else:
  386                  st.info(f"**{label}**")
  387              st.write("**Why this score?**")
  388              for r in reasons:
  389                  st.write(f"- {r}")
  390  
  391      # Refresh button
  392      st.divider()
  393      if st.button("Refresh Today's Data", key="refresh_btn"):
  394          # Clear today's entries and re-fetch
  395          full_log = load_log()
  396          keep = full_log[full_log["date"].astype(str) != today_str]
  397          keep.to_csv(LOG_FILE, index=False)
  398          auto_scan_today()
  399          st.success("Re-fetched today's headlines. Reload the page to see updated data.")
  400  
  401      # Today's results table
  402      st.divider()
  403      st.subheader("Today's Headlines")
  404      today_refresh = load_log()
  405      today_show = today_refresh[today_refresh["date"].astype(str) == today_str]
  406      if not today_show.empty:
  407          # HIGH signal alerts
  408          high_df = today_show[today_show["score"] >= 70]
  409          for _, row in high_df.iterrows():
  410              st.error(f"**HIGH SIGNAL** — {row['text']} (Score: {row['score']}, Source: {row.get('source', '?')})")
  411  
  412          st.dataframe(
  413              today_show[["text", "ordinal", "reduced", "score", "source"]].sort_values("score", ascending=False),
  414              use_container_width=True,
  415          )
  416      else:
  417          st.caption("No headlines for today yet.")
  418  
  419      # Cross-source matches
  420      st.divider()
  421      st.subheader("Cross-Source Matches")
  422      st.caption("Headlines from DIFFERENT sources sharing the same ordinal or reduced value today.")
  423      xmatches = find_cross_source_matches(today_show if not today_show.empty else pd.DataFrame())
  424      if xmatches:
  425          for xm in xmatches:
  426              st.write(
  427                  f"**{xm['type'].title()} {xm['value']}** — shared by: {', '.join(xm['sources'])}"
  428              )
  429              for h in xm["headlines"]:
  430                  st.write(f"  - {h}")
  431      else:
  432          st.caption("No cross-source matches detected today.")
  433
