  108              user_id = user_resp.data.id
  109  
  110              tweets_resp = client.get_users_tweets(
  111                  id=user_id,
  112                  max_results=TWEETS_PER_ACCT,
  113                  tweet_fields=["public_metrics", "created_at"],
  114                  exclude=["retweets", "replies"],
  115              )
  116              if not tweets_resp.data:
  117                  continue
  118  
  119              for tw in tweets_resp.data:
  120                  pm = tw.public_metrics or {}
  121                  results.append({
  122                      "id":         str(tw.id),
  123                      "account":    username,
  124                      "text":       tw.text,
  125                      "like_count": pm.get("like_count", 0),
  126                      "url":        f"https://twitter.com/{username}/status/{tw.id}",
  127                  })
  128          except tweepy.TweepyException as exc:
  129              print(f"  @{username}: Twitter error — {exc}")
  130  
  131      # Sort by likes descending, cap at 10
  132      results.sort(key=lambda t: t["like_count"], reverse=True)
  133      return results[:10]
  134  
  135  
  136  # ── Claude reply generation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  137  
  138  def generate_reply(tweet: dict) -> str:
  139      """Call Claude to generate an analyst-voice reply to tweet['text']."""
  140      if not cfg.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
  141          print("\n[ERROR] ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set in .env")
  142          sys.exit(1)
  143  
  144      anthropic = _require("anthropic")
  145      client    = anthropic.Anthropic(api_key=cfg.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)
  146  
  147      prompt = (
  148          f"Original tweet by @{tweet['account']}:\n"
  149          f"\"{tweet['text']}\"\n\n"
  150          f"Write your reply:"
  151      )
  152  
  153      message = client.messages.create(
  154          model="claude-sonnet-4-6",
  155          max_tokens=300,
  156          system=CLAUDE_SYSTEM,
  157          messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
  158      )
  159      reply = message.content[0].text.strip()
  160  
  161      # Hard-trim if Claude exceeded the limit
  162      if len(reply) > REPLY_MAX_CHARS:
  163          reply = reply[: REPLY_MAX_CHARS - 1].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
  164  
  165      return reply
  166  
  167  
  168  # ── Tweet posting ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  169  
  170  def post_reply(tweet_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
  171      """Post a reply to tweet_id. Returns True on success."""
  172      tweepy = _require("tweepy")
  173      client = _twitter_client()
  174      try:
  175          resp = client.create_tweet(text=text, in_reply_to_tweet_id=tweet_id)
  176          new_id = resp.data["id"]
  177          # Persist to tweet log
  178          import datetime
  179          with open(TWEET_LOG, "a") as f:
  180              ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
  181              f.write(f"[{ts}] REPLY to {tweet_id} → {new_id}: {text}\n")
  182          return True
  183      except tweepy.TweepyException as exc:
  184          print(f"\n[ERROR] Failed to post reply: {exc}")
  185          return False
  186  
  187  
  188  # ── Display helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  189  
  190  def _print_tweet(n: int, tw: dict) -> None:
  191      wrapped = textwrap.fill(tw["text"], width=72, subsequent_indent="              ")
  192      print(f"\n  [{n:2d}]  @{tw['account']:20s}  ❤ {tw['like_count']}")
  193      print(f"        {wrapped}")
  194  
  195  
  196  # ── Main interactive loop ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  197  
  198  def main() -> None:
  199      print("\n─── Crypto Reply Tool ───────────────────────────────────────────────")
  200      print("Fetching recent tweets from:", ", ".join(f"@{a}" for a in TARGET_ACCOUNTS))
  201  
  202      tweets = fetch_recent_tweets()
  203      if not tweets:
  204          print("\nNo tweets fetched. Check your Twitter credentials and network.")
  205          sys.exit(1)
  206  
  207      print(f"\nFound {len(tweets)} tweet(s):\n")
  208      for i, tw in enumerate(tweets, 1):
  209          _print_tweet(i, tw)
  210  
  211      # Pick a tweet
  212      print()
  213      while True:
  214          raw = input(f"Pick a tweet to reply to [1–{len(tweets)}], or q to quit: ").strip()
  215          if raw.lower() == "q":
  216              print("Aborted.")
  217              sys.exit(0)
  218          if raw.isdigit() and 1 <= int(raw) <= len(tweets):
  219              chosen = tweets[int(raw) - 1]
  220              break
  221          print(f"  Enter a number between 1 and {len(tweets)}.")
  222  
  223      print(f"\nGenerating reply for @{chosen['account']}…")
  224      reply = generate_reply(chosen)
  225  
  226      print(f"\n─── Generated Reply ({len(reply)} chars) ────────────────────────────────")
  227      print(f"\n  {reply}\n")
  228      print(f"─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
  229  
  230      confirm = input("Post this reply? [Y/n]: ").strip().lower()
  231      if confirm in ("", "y", "yes"):
  232          ok = post_reply(chosen["id"], reply)
  233          if ok:
  234              print(f"\nPosted successfully.")
  235          else:
  236              print(f"\nPost failed — see error above.")
  237      else:
  238          print("Cancelled — reply not posted.")
  239  
  240  
  241  if __name__ == "__main__":
  242      main()


================================================================================
  FILE: state.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  state.py – Persistent bot state (monthly tweet counter + daily type counts).
    3  
    4  Tracks monthly tweet count against the 1,500/month Twitter free-tier cap,
    5  and per-type daily counts that reset at UK midnight.
    6  State is persisted to .bot_state.json so restarts don't reset the counter.
    7  """
    8  from __future__ import annotations
    9  
   10  import datetime
   11  import json
   12  import logging
   13  import os
   14  from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
   15  
   16  _LONDON_TZ = ZoneInfo("Europe/London")
   17  
   18  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   19  
   20  _STATE_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".bot_state.json")
   21  MONTHLY_TWEET_CAP = 1500
   22  
   23  _state: dict = {}
   24  
   25  
   26  def _load() -> None:
   27      global _state
   28      try:
   29          with open(_STATE_FILE) as f:
   30              _state = json.load(f)
   31      except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   32          _state = {}
   33  
   34  
   35  def _save() -> None:
   36      try:
   37          with open(_STATE_FILE, "w") as f:
   38              json.dump(_state, f)
   39      except OSError as exc:
   40          logger.warning("Could not save bot state: %s", exc)
   41  
   42  
   43  def _month_key() -> str:
   44      return datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y-%m")
   45  
   46  
   47  def _day_key_uk() -> str:
   48      """Return today's date string in UK/London timezone (YYYY-MM-DD)."""
   49      return datetime.datetime.now(_LONDON_TZ).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
   50  
   51  
   52  def can_tweet() -> bool:
   53      """Return True if we're still under the 1,500 tweet/month cap."""
   54      _load()
   55      count = _state.get(_month_key(), 0)
   56      return count < MONTHLY_TWEET_CAP
   57  
   58  
   59  def record_tweet(count: int = 1) -> None:
   60      """Increment the monthly tweet counter and persist."""
   61      _load()
   62      key = _month_key()
   63      _state[key] = _state.get(key, 0) + max(1, count)
   64      _save()
   65      logger.debug("Tweet recorded. Monthly total: %d", _state[key])
   66  
   67  
   68  def record_content_category(category: str) -> None:
   69      """Track content category for variety enforcement."""
   70      _load()
   71      cats = _state.get("recent_categories", [])
   72      cats.append(category)
   73      _state["recent_categories"] = cats[-20:]  # keep last 20
   74      _save()
   75  
   76  
   77  def get_recent_content_categories(n: int = 6) -> list[str]:
   78      """Return the last n content categories posted."""
   79      _load()
   80      return _state.get("recent_categories", [])[-n:]
   81  
   82  
   83  def record_quote_style(style: str) -> None:
   84      """Track last quote tweet template style."""
   85      _load()
   86      _state["last_quote_style"] = style
   87      _save()
   88  
   89  
   90  def get_last_quote_style() -> str:
   91      """Return last quote tweet template style, or empty string."""
   92      _load()
   93      return _state.get("last_quote_style", "")
   94  
   95  
   96  # ── Daily counts (resets at UK midnight) ─────────────────────────────────────
   97  
   98  def get_daily_count(tweet_type: str) -> int:
   99      """Return today's post count for a given tweet type (UK date)."""
  100      _load()
  101      day = _day_key_uk()
  102      return _state.get("daily_counts", {}).get(day, {}).get(tweet_type, 0)
  103  
  104  
  105  def get_total_daily_tweets() -> int:
  106      """Return total tweets posted today (UK date)."""
  107      _load()
  108      day = _day_key_uk()
  109      return _state.get("daily_counts", {}).get(day, {}).get("_total", 0)
  110  
  111  
  112  def get_thread_topic_index() -> int:
  113      """Return the persisted evening thread topic index."""
  114      _load()
  115      return _state.get("thread_topic_index", 0)
  116  
  117  
  118  def set_thread_topic_index(index: int) -> None:
  119      """Persist the evening thread topic index."""
  120      _load()
  121      _state["thread_topic_index"] = index
  122      _save()
  123  
  124  
  125  def get_replied_ids() -> set[str]:
  126      """Return the set of tweet IDs we have already replied to."""
  127      _load()
  128      return set(_state.get("replied_ids", []))
  129  
  130  
  131  def add_replied_id(tweet_id: str) -> None:
  132      """Record a tweet ID as replied-to and persist. Keeps last 500."""
  133      _load()
  134      ids = _state.get("replied_ids", [])
  135      if tweet_id not in ids:
  136          ids.append(tweet_id)
  137      _state["replied_ids"] = ids[-500:]
  138      _save()
  139  
  140  
  141  def get_fear_greed_last_posted_ts() -> float | None:
  142      """Return the Unix timestamp of the last Fear & Greed post, or None."""
  143      _load()
  144      return _state.get("fear_greed_last_ts")
  145  
  146  
  147  def get_fear_greed_last_value() -> int | None:
  148      """Return the value posted in the last Fear & Greed tweet, or None."""
  149      _load()
  150      return _state.get("fear_greed_last_value")
  151  
  152  
  153  def record_fear_greed_posted(value: int) -> None:
  154      """Persist timestamp and value of the most recent Fear & Greed post."""
  155      _load()
  156      import time as _time
  157      _state["fear_greed_last_ts"] = _time.time()
  158      _state["fear_greed_last_value"] = value
  159      _save()
  160  
  161  
  162  def increment_daily_count(tweet_type: str, amount: int = 1) -> None:
  163      """Increment today's count for tweet_type and the daily total. Persists to disk."""
  164      _load()
  165      day = _day_key_uk()
  166      # Prune any stale day entries (keep only today)
  167      _state["daily_counts"] = {day: _state.get("daily_counts", {}).get(day, {})}
  168      counts = _state["daily_counts"][day]
  169      counts[tweet_type] = counts.get(tweet_type, 0) + amount
  170      counts["_total"] = counts.get("_total", 0) + amount
  171      _save()
  172      logger.debug("Daily count: %s=%d, total=%d (day=%s, persisted)",
  173                   tweet_type, counts[tweet_type], counts["_total"], day)


================================================================================
  FILE: telegram_client.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Telegram Bot API client — sends messages and photos to the configured channel.
    3  
    4  Uses only the `requests` library (no python-telegram-bot dependency).
    5  Every call is wrapped so a Telegram failure never crashes the main bot.
    6  """
    7  from __future__ import annotations
    8  
    9  import logging
   10  import os
   11  
   12  import requests
   13  
   14  import config
   15  
   16  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   17  
   18  _API_BASE = "https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}"
   19  
   20  
   21  def _api_url(method: str) -> str:
   22      token = config.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN
   23      if not token:
   24          raise RuntimeError("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN is not set")
   25      return f"{_API_BASE.format(token=token)}/{method}"
   26  
   27  
   28  def send_telegram(text: str, image_path: str | None = None) -> bool:
   29      """Send a message (and optional photo) to the Telegram channel.
   30  
   31      Returns True on success, False on any failure.
   32      """
   33      if not config.TELEGRAM_ENABLED:
   34          return False
   35  
   36      token = config.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN
   37      chat_id = config.TELEGRAM_CHANNEL_ID
   38      if not token or not chat_id:
   39          logger.debug("Telegram credentials missing — skipping")
   40          return False
   41  
   42      try:
   43          if image_path and os.path.isfile(image_path):
   44              # Send photo with caption
   45              url = _api_url("sendPhoto")
   46              with open(image_path, "rb") as img:
   47                  resp = requests.post(
   48                      url,
   49                      data={"chat_id": chat_id, "caption": text, "parse_mode": "HTML"},
   50                      files={"photo": img},
   51                      timeout=30,
   52                  )
   53          else:
   54              # Text-only message
   55              url = _api_url("sendMessage")
   56              resp = requests.post(
   57                  url,
   58                  json={
   59                      "chat_id": chat_id,
   60                      "text": text,
   61                      "parse_mode": "HTML",
   62                      "disable_web_page_preview": True,
   63                  },
   64                  timeout=15,
   65              )
   66  
   67          if resp.status_code == 200 and resp.json().get("ok"):
   68              logger.info("Telegram message sent: %.60s", text)
   69              return True
   70  
   71          logger.warning(
   72              "Telegram API error %d: %s", resp.status_code, resp.text[:200]
   73          )
   74          return False
   75  
   76      except Exception as exc:
   77          logger.warning("Telegram send failed: %s", exc)
   78          return False


================================================================================
  FILE: trading_bot.py
================================================================================

    1  #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2  """
    3  trading_bot.py – Crypto.com multi-pair spot trading bot.
    4  
    5  Strategy: weighted composite of 5 technical signals.
    6    RSI(w=0.30)  EMA(w=0.25)  MOM(w=0.20)  BB(w=0.15)  VOL(w=0.10)
    7  
    8  Buy  when composite score >= +0.10 AND at least 3/5 signals are positive.
    9  Sell when composite score <= -0.30 OR stop-loss / take-profit triggered.
   10  
   11  Usage:
   12      python trading_bot.py            # live trading (requires CDX keys)
   13      python trading_bot.py --dry-run  # simulate without placing orders
   14  """
   15  from __future__ import annotations
   16  
   17  import argparse
   18  import logging
   19  import math
   20  import os
   21  import signal
   22  import sys
   23  import time
   24  from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   25  
   26  import config
   27  import exchange
   28  
   29  # ── Logging ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   30  _fmt = "%(asctime)s  %(levelname)-8s  %(name)-20s %(message)s"
   31  _handlers: list[logging.Handler] = [logging.FileHandler(config.TRADING_LOG_FILE)]
   32  logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=_fmt, handlers=_handlers)
   33  logger = logging.getLogger("bot")
   34  
   35  # ── Signal weights ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   36  _W_RSI = 0.30
   37  _W_EMA = 0.25
   38  _W_MOM = 0.20
   39  _W_BB  = 0.15
   40  _W_VOL = 0.10
   41  
   42  # ── Parameters ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   43  _RSI_PERIOD       = 14
   44  _RSI_OVERSOLD     = 30.0
   45  _RSI_OVERBOUGHT   = 70.0
   46  _EMA_SHORT        = 9
   47  _EMA_LONG         = 21
   48  _MOM_PERIOD       = 10
   49  _BB_PERIOD        = 20
   50  _BB_STD           = 2.0
   51  _VOL_PERIOD       = 20
   52  _VOL_SURGE_MULT   = 1.5   # vol must be ≥ this × avg to boost signal
   53  
   54  BUY_SCORE_THRESHOLD  = 0.10
   55  BUY_MIN_SIGNALS      = 3
   56  SELL_SCORE_THRESHOLD = -0.30
   57  
   58  # ── Position ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   59  @dataclass
   60  class Position:
   61      pair: str
   62      side: str            # "long"
   63      entry_price: float
   64      quantity: float      # base asset
   65      cost: float          # USDT spent
   66      stop_loss: float
   67      take_profit: float
   68      opened_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
   69  
   70      def pnl_pct(self, current_price: float) -> float:
   71          return (current_price - self.entry_price) / self.entry_price
   72  
   73      def should_stop(self, current_price: float) -> bool:
   74          return current_price <= self.stop_loss
   75  
   76      def should_take_profit(self, current_price: float) -> bool:
   77          return current_price >= self.take_profit
   78  
   79  
   80  # ── Indicator helpers (pure Python, no numpy) ─────────────────────────────────
   81  
   82  def _ema(values: list[float], period: int) -> list[float]:
   83      """Compute EMA for a series.  Returns same-length list (NaN at start)."""
   84      if len(values) < period:
   85          return [float("nan")] * len(values)
   86      k = 2.0 / (period + 1)
   87      result: list[float] = [float("nan")] * len(values)
   88      # Seed with SMA of first `period` values
   89      result[period - 1] = sum(values[:period]) / period
   90      for i in range(period, len(values)):
   91          result[i] = values[i] * k + result[i - 1] * (1 - k)
   92      return result
   93  
   94  
   95  def _rsi(closes: list[float], period: int = 14) -> float | None:
   96      """Return latest RSI value or None if insufficient data."""
   97      if len(closes) < period + 1:
   98          return None
   99      gains, losses = [], []
  100      for i in range(1, len(closes)):
  101          diff = closes[i] - closes[i - 1]
  102          gains.append(max(diff, 0.0))
  103          losses.append(max(-diff, 0.0))
  104      # Use Wilder smoothing (equivalent to EMA with period)
  105      avg_gain = sum(gains[:period]) / period
  106      avg_loss = sum(losses[:period]) / period
  107      for i in range(period, len(gains)):
  108          avg_gain = (avg_gain * (period - 1) + gains[i]) / period
  109          avg_loss = (avg_loss * (period - 1) + losses[i]) / period
  110      if avg_loss == 0:
  111          return 100.0
  112      rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
  113      return 100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
  114  
  115  
  116  def _bollinger(closes: list[float], period: int = 20, num_std: float = 2.0) -> tuple[float, float, float] | None:
  117      """Return (lower, mid, upper) for the latest bar."""
  118      if len(closes) < period:
  119          return None
  120      window = closes[-period:]
  121      mid = sum(window) / period
  122      variance = sum((x - mid) ** 2 for x in window) / period
  123      std = math.sqrt(variance)
  124      return mid - num_std * std, mid, mid + num_std * std
  125  
  126  
  127  def _momentum(closes: list[float], period: int = 10) -> float | None:
  128      """Return close[-1] - close[-period-1]."""
  129      if len(closes) < period + 1:
  130          return None
  131      return closes[-1] - closes[-(period + 1)]
  132  
  133  
  134  # ── Signal scoring ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  135  
  136  def score_candles(candles: list[dict]) -> tuple[float, int]:
  137      """
  138      Compute the composite signal score and the count of positive/negative signals.
  139  
  140      Returns (score, n_active_signals) where score ∈ [-1, +1].
  141      Each indicator contributes its weight positively (bullish) or negatively (bearish).
  142      """
  143      if len(candles) < _BB_PERIOD + 1:
  144          return 0.0, 0
  145  
  146      closes  = [float(c["c"]) for c in candles]
  147      volumes = [float(c["v"]) for c in candles]
  148  
  149      score = 0.0
  150      n_signals = 0
  151  
  152      # RSI
  153      rsi_val = _rsi(closes, _RSI_PERIOD)
  154      if rsi_val is not None:
  155          if rsi_val < _RSI_OVERSOLD:
  156              score += _W_RSI
  157              n_signals += 1
  158          elif rsi_val > _RSI_OVERBOUGHT:
  159              score -= _W_RSI
  160              n_signals += 1
  161  
  162      # EMA crossover
  163      ema_short_series = _ema(closes, _EMA_SHORT)
  164      ema_long_series  = _ema(closes, _EMA_LONG)
  165      ema_short = ema_short_series[-1]
  166      ema_long  = ema_long_series[-1]
  167      if not math.isnan(ema_short) and not math.isnan(ema_long):
  168          if ema_short > ema_long:
  169              score += _W_EMA
  170              n_signals += 1
  171          elif ema_short < ema_long:
  172              score -= _W_EMA
  173              n_signals += 1
  174  
  175      # Momentum
  176      mom = _momentum(closes, _MOM_PERIOD)
  177      if mom is not None:
  178          if mom > 0:
  179              score += _W_MOM
  180              n_signals += 1
  181          elif mom < 0:
  182              score -= _W_MOM
  183              n_signals += 1
  184  
  185      # Bollinger Bands
  186      bb = _bollinger(closes, _BB_PERIOD, _BB_STD)
  187      if bb is not None:
  188          lower, mid, upper = bb
  189          price = closes[-1]
  190          if price < lower:
  191              score += _W_BB
  192              n_signals += 1
  193          elif price > upper:
  194              score -= _W_BB
  195              n_signals += 1
  196  
  197      # Volume surge (amplifies the directional bias)
  198      if len(volumes) >= _VOL_PERIOD + 1:
  199          avg_vol = sum(volumes[-_VOL_PERIOD - 1:-1]) / _VOL_PERIOD
  200          cur_vol = volumes[-1]
  201          if avg_vol > 0 and cur_vol >= avg_vol * _VOL_SURGE_MULT:
  202              # Volume surge — add weight in the direction the score is leaning
  203              direction = 1.0 if score >= 0 else -1.0
  204              score += _W_VOL * direction
  205              n_signals += 1
  206  
  207      return score, n_signals
  208  
  209  
  210  # ── Capital / position management ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  211  
  212  class Portfolio:
  213      def __init__(self, total_capital: float, dry_run: bool):
  214          self.total_capital = total_capital
  215          self.used_capital  = 0.0
  216          self.positions: dict[str, Position] = {}
  217          self.dry_run = dry_run
  218  
  219      @property
  220      def available(self) -> float:
  221          return self.total_capital - self.used_capital
  222  
  223      def max_trade_size(self) -> float:
  224          limit_by_cap  = min(config.TRADING_MAX_PER_TRADE, self.available)
  225          limit_by_pct  = self.total_capital * config.TRADING_MAX_POS_PCT
  226          return min(limit_by_cap, limit_by_pct)
  227  
  228      def open_position(self, pair: str, price: float, cost: float) -> bool:
  229          if pair in self.positions:
  230              return False
  231          if cost > self.available + 0.01:
  232              logger.warning("%s: insufficient capital (need %.2f, have %.2f)", pair, cost, self.available)
  233              return False
  234  
  235          quantity = cost / price
  236          stop_loss   = price * (1 - config.TRADING_STOP_LOSS_PCT)
  237          take_profit = price * (1 + config.TRADING_TAKE_PROFIT_PCT)
  238  
  239          if not self.dry_run:
  240              result = exchange.place_order(pair, "BUY", quantity, "MARKET")
  241              if not result:
  242                  logger.error("%s: BUY order failed", pair)
  243                  return False
  244              logger.info("%s: BUY %.6f @ %.4f (cost $%.2f)", pair, quantity, price, cost)
  245          else:
  246              logger.info("[DRY RUN] %s: BUY %.6f @ %.4f (cost $%.2f)", pair, quantity, price, cost)
  247  
  248          self.positions[pair] = Position(
  249              pair=pair,
  250              side="long",
  251              entry_price=price,
  252              quantity=quantity,
  253              cost=cost,
  254              stop_loss=stop_loss,
  255              take_profit=take_profit,
  256          )
  257          self.used_capital += cost
  258          return True
  259  
  260      def close_position(self, pair: str, price: float, reason: str) -> bool:
  261          pos = self.positions.get(pair)
  262          if not pos:
  263              return False
  264  
  265          pnl_pct = pos.pnl_pct(price)
  266          pnl_usd = pos.cost * pnl_pct
  267  
  268          if not self.dry_run:
  269              result = exchange.place_order(pair, "SELL", pos.quantity, "MARKET")
  270              if not result:
  271                  logger.error("%s: SELL order failed", pair)
  272                  return False
  273              logger.info("%s: SELL %.6f @ %.4f | PnL: %+.2f%% ($%+.2f) [%s]",
  274                          pair, pos.quantity, price, pnl_pct * 100, pnl_usd, reason)
  275          else:
  276              logger.info("[DRY RUN] %s: SELL %.6f @ %.4f | PnL: %+.2f%% ($%+.2f) [%s]",
  277                          pair, pos.quantity, price, pnl_pct * 100, pnl_usd, reason)
  278  
  279          self.used_capital -= pos.cost
  280          del self.positions[pair]
  281          return True
  282  
  283      def summary(self) -> str:
  284          lines = [
  285              f"  Capital     : ${self.used_capital:.2f} used / ${self.total_capital:.2f} total"
  286              f"  (${self.available:.2f} available)",
  287              f"  Open positions ({len(self.positions)}):",
  288          ]
  289          for pair, pos in self.positions.items():
  290              lines.append(
  291                  f"    {pair:12s}  entry={pos.entry_price:.4f}  qty={pos.quantity:.6f}"
  292                  f"  sl={pos.stop_loss:.4f}  tp={pos.take_profit:.4f}"
  293              )
  294          return "\n".join(lines)
  295  
  296  
  297  # ── Candle cache ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  298  
  299  # pair → list of candles (oldest first), kept trimmed to last 300
  300  _candle_cache: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
  301  _MIN_CANDLES = _BB_PERIOD + _MOM_PERIOD + 5  # need at least ~35 bars
  302  
  303  
  304  def _update_candles(pair: str, timeframe: str) -> list[dict]:
  305      """Fetch fresh candles and merge into cache.  Returns updated candle list."""
  306      fresh = exchange.get_candlesticks(pair, timeframe, depth=200)
  307      if not fresh:
  308          return _candle_cache.get(pair, [])
  309  
  310      existing = _candle_cache.get(pair, [])
  311      if not existing:
  312          _candle_cache[pair] = fresh
  313      else:
  314          last_t = existing[-1]["t"]
  315          new = [c for c in fresh if c["t"] > last_t]
  316          if new:
  317              existing.extend(new)
  318              # Trim to last 300
  319              _candle_cache[pair] = existing[-300:]
  320  
  321      return _candle_cache[pair]
  322  
  323  
  324  # ── Shutdown ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  325  
  326  def _shutdown(signum, frame):
  327      logger.info("Received signal %d — shutting down.", signum)
  328      sys.exit(0)
  329  
  330  signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _shutdown)
  331  signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,  _shutdown)
  332  
  333  
  334  # ── Main ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  335  
  336  def main() -> None:
  337      parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Crypto.com Trading Bot")
  338      parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
  339                          help="Simulate trades without placing real orders")
  340      args = parser.parse_args()
  341      dry_run: bool = args.dry_run
  342  
  343      # Also attach stdout handler so the launch-agent log file captures output
  344      if sys.stdout.isatty() or dry_run:
  345          logging.getLogger().addHandler(logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout))
  346  
  347      pairs     = config.TRADING_PAIRS
  348      timeframe = config.TRADING_TIMEFRAME
  349  
  350      signal_desc = (
  351          f"RSI(w={_W_RSI:.2f}) "
  352          f"EMA(w={_W_EMA:.2f}) "
  353          f"MOM(w={_W_MOM:.2f}) "
  354          f"BB(w={_W_BB:.2f}) "
  355          f"VOL(w={_W_VOL:.2f})"
  356      )
  357  
  358      logger.info("=" * 65)
  359      logger.info("  Crypto Trading Bot")
  360      logger.info("=" * 65)
  361      logger.info("  Mode          :   %s", "DRY RUN (no real orders)" if dry_run else "LIVE")
  362      logger.info("  Pairs         : %s", ", ".join(pairs))
  363      logger.info("  Timeframe     : %s", timeframe)
  364      logger.info("  Total capital : $%.2f", config.TRADING_CAPITAL)
  365      logger.info("  Max per trade : $%.2f", config.TRADING_MAX_PER_TRADE)
  366      logger.info("  Max pos. size : %.0f%% of capital", config.TRADING_MAX_POS_PCT * 100)
  367      logger.info("  Stop loss     : %.1f%%", config.TRADING_STOP_LOSS_PCT * 100)
  368      logger.info("  Take profit   : %.1f%%", config.TRADING_TAKE_PROFIT_PCT * 100)
  369      logger.info("  Poll interval : %ds", config.TRADING_POLL_INTERVAL)
  370      logger.info("  Signals       : %s", signal_desc)
  371      logger.info("  Buy threshold : score>=+%.2f  min signals: %d/5",
  372                  BUY_SCORE_THRESHOLD, BUY_MIN_SIGNALS)
  373      logger.info("  Sell threshold: score<=%.2f", SELL_SCORE_THRESHOLD)
  374      logger.info("=" * 65)
  375  
  376      portfolio = Portfolio(config.TRADING_CAPITAL, dry_run)
  377  
  378      # ── Pre-fetch candle history ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  379      logger.info("Pre-fetching candle history (%s) …", timeframe)
  380      for pair in pairs:
  381          candles = _update_candles(pair, timeframe)
  382          vols = [float(c["v"]) for c in candles[-3:]] if candles else []
  383          vol_avg = (
  384              sum(float(c["v"]) for c in candles[-_VOL_PERIOD - 1:-1]) / _VOL_PERIOD
  385              if len(candles) > _VOL_PERIOD
  386              else 0.0
  387          )
  388          logger.info("  %-12s %3d candles  vol(last3)=%-20s vol_avg20=%.2f",
  389                      pair, len(candles), str([round(v, 2) for v in vols]), vol_avg)
  390      logger.info("=" * 65)
  391  
  392      cycle = 0
  393      while True:
  394          cycle += 1
  395          logger.info("─── Cycle %d ───────────────────────────────────────────────", cycle)
  396  
  397          for pair in pairs:
  398              # ── Update candles ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  399              candles = _update_candles(pair, timeframe)
  400              if not candles:
  401                  logger.warning("%s: no candle data returned, skipping", pair)
  402                  continue
  403              if len(candles) < _MIN_CANDLES:
  404                  logger.debug("%s: only %d candles (need %d), skipping",
  405                               pair, len(candles), _MIN_CANDLES)
  406                  continue
  407  
  408              current_price = float(candles[-1]["c"])
  409  
  410              # ── Manage open positions ─────────────────────────────────────────
  411              if pair in portfolio.positions:
  412                  pos = portfolio.positions[pair]
  413                  if pos.should_stop(current_price):
  414                      portfolio.close_position(pair, current_price, "stop-loss")
  415                      continue
  416                  if pos.should_take_profit(current_price):
  417                      portfolio.close_position(pair, current_price, "take-profit")
  418                      continue
  419                  score, n_sig = score_candles(candles)
  420                  if score <= SELL_SCORE_THRESHOLD:
  421                      portfolio.close_position(pair, current_price, f"signal-sell score={score:.3f}")
  422                  continue
  423  
  424              # ── Evaluate buy signal ───────────────────────────────────────────
  425              score, n_sig = score_candles(candles)
  426              logger.debug("%s: score=%.3f  n_signals=%d  price=%.4f",
  427                           pair, score, n_sig, current_price)
  428  
  429              if score >= BUY_SCORE_THRESHOLD and n_sig >= BUY_MIN_SIGNALS:
  430                  trade_size = portfolio.max_trade_size()
  431                  if trade_size < 1.0:
  432                      logger.info("%s: BUY signal but insufficient capital ($%.2f)", pair, trade_size)
  433                      continue
  434                  logger.info("%s: BUY signal — score=%.3f n_sig=%d price=%.4f size=$%.2f",
  435                              pair, score, n_sig, current_price, trade_size)
  436                  portfolio.open_position(pair, current_price, trade_size)
  437  
  438          # ── Cycle summary ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  439          logger.info("Cycle %d complete.\n%s", cycle, portfolio.summary())
  440          logger.info("Sleeping %ds …", config.TRADING_POLL_INTERVAL)
  441          time.sleep(config.TRADING_POLL_INTERVAL)
  442  
  443  
  444  if __name__ == "__main__":
  445      main()


================================================================================
  FILE: trending_monitor.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Trending coin monitor – detects coins surging outside the configured
    3  watchlist by scanning CoinGecko's trending and top movers endpoints.
    4  
    5  Posts tweets when a coin outside the tracked list is pumping hard.
    6  """
    7  from __future__ import annotations
    8  
    9  import logging
   10  import re
   11  import time
   12  
   13  import requests
   14  
   15  import config
   16  import state
   17  import ai_writer
   18  
   19  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   20  
   21  # Coins we already track — skip these
   22  _TRACKED_IDS = set(config.COINS.keys())
   23  
   24  # Only tweet about coins ranked top-100 by market cap
   25  _MCAP_RANK_LIMIT = 100
   26  
   27  # In-memory cooldown to avoid spamming the same trending coin
   28  _recently_tweeted: dict[str, float] = {}
   29  _TRENDING_COOLDOWN = 21600  # 6 hours before tweeting same trending coin
   30  
   31  
   32  def _cooldown_ok(coin_id: str) -> bool:
   33      now = time.time()
   34      last = _recently_tweeted.get(coin_id, 0)
   35      return (now - last) >= _TRENDING_COOLDOWN
   36  
   37  
   38  def _symbol_cooldown_ok(symbol: str) -> bool:
   39      """Check cooldown by symbol (uppercase) to catch same coin via different IDs."""
   40      key = f"sym:{symbol.upper()}"
   41      now = time.time()
   42      last = _recently_tweeted.get(key, 0)
   43      return (now - last) >= _TRENDING_COOLDOWN
   44  
   45  
   46  def _record(coin_id: str, symbol: str = "") -> None:
   47      _recently_tweeted[coin_id] = time.time()
   48      if symbol:
   49          _recently_tweeted[f"sym:{symbol.upper()}"] = time.time()
   50      # Prune old entries
   51      cutoff = time.time() - _TRENDING_COOLDOWN * 2
   52      expired = [k for k, v in _recently_tweeted.items() if v < cutoff]
   53      for k in expired:
   54          del _recently_tweeted[k]
   55  
   56  
   57  def _fetch_coin_details(coin_id: str) -> dict | None:
   58      """
   59      Fetch price, 24h change, volume, and logo URL for a single coin.
   60      Returns None if the request fails or price/volume are missing — callers
   61      must skip the coin entirely in that case.
   62      """
   63      try:
   64          resp = requests.get(
   65              f"{config.COINGECKO_BASE}/coins/markets",
   66              params={
   67                  "vs_currency": "usd",
   68                  "ids": coin_id,
   69                  "price_change_percentage": "24h",
   70              },
   71              timeout=15,
   72          )
   73          resp.raise_for_status()
   74          data = resp.json()
   75          if not data:
   76              return None
   77          coin = data[0]
   78          price = coin.get("current_price")
   79          volume = coin.get("total_volume")
   80          if not price or not volume:
   81              logger.debug("Skipping %s — no price or volume data", coin_id)
   82              return None
   83          return {
   84              "current_price": price,
   85              "pct_24h":       coin.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0,
   86              "market_cap":    coin.get("market_cap", 0),
   87              "volume_24h":    volume,
   88              "image":         coin.get("image"),
   89          }
   90      except requests.RequestException as exc:
   91          logger.warning("Coin details fetch failed for %s: %s", coin_id, exc)
   92          return None
   93  
   94  
   95  def fetch_trending() -> list[dict]:
   96      """
   97      Fetch CoinGecko trending coins (free endpoint, no key).
   98  
   99      Returns list of dicts with: id, symbol, name, market_cap_rank, price_btc
  100      """
  101      try:
  102          resp = requests.get(
  103              f"{config.COINGECKO_BASE}/search/trending",
  104              timeout=15,
  105          )
  106          resp.raise_for_status()
  107          data = resp.json()
  108          coins = data.get("coins", [])
  109          results = []
  110          for entry in coins:
  111              coin = entry.get("item", {})
  112              # score is the 0-indexed position in CoinGecko's trending list,
  113              # so trending_rank 1 = most-searched coin right now.
  114              score = coin.get("score", 0)
  115              try:
  116                  trending_rank = int(score) + 1
  117              except (TypeError, ValueError):
  118                  trending_rank = None
  119              # market_cap_rank can be null for very new / unranked tokens.
  120              mcap_rank = coin.get("market_cap_rank")
  121              if mcap_rank is not None:
  122                  try:
  123                      mcap_rank = int(mcap_rank)
  124                  except (TypeError, ValueError):
  125                      mcap_rank = None
  126              results.append({
  127                  "id":              coin.get("id", ""),
  128                  "symbol":          coin.get("symbol", "").upper(),
  129                  "name":            coin.get("name", ""),
  130                  "market_cap_rank": mcap_rank,
  131                  "trending_rank":   trending_rank,
  132                  "price_btc":       coin.get("price_btc", 0),
  133                  "score":           score,
  134                  "source":          "trending",
  135              })
  136          return results
  137      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  138          logger.warning("CoinGecko trending fetch failed: %s", exc)
  139          return []
  140  
  141  
  142  def fetch_top_movers() -> list[dict]:
  143      """
  144      Fetch top 250 coins and find those with big 24h moves that we don't
  145      already track. Returns coins moving 10%+ in 24h.
  146      """
  147      try:
  148          resp = requests.get(
  149              f"{config.COINGECKO_BASE}/coins/markets",
  150              params={
  151                  "vs_currency": "usd",
  152                  "order": "market_cap_desc",
  153                  "per_page": 250,
  154                  "page": 1,
  155                  "price_change_percentage": "24h",
  156              },
  157              timeout=15,
  158          )
  159          resp.raise_for_status()
  160          data = resp.json()
  161  
  162          movers = []
  163          for coin in data:
  164              coin_id = coin.get("id", "")
  165              if coin_id in _TRACKED_IDS:
  166                  continue  # already covered by price_monitor
  167              pct = coin.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  168              if abs(pct) >= config.TRENDING_SURGE_PCT:
  169                  movers.append({
  170                      "id":            coin_id,
  171                      "symbol":        coin.get("symbol", "").upper(),
  172                      "name":          coin.get("name", ""),
  173                      "current_price": coin.get("current_price", 0),
  174                      "pct_24h":       pct,
  175                      "market_cap_rank": coin.get("market_cap_rank"),
  176                      "market_cap":    coin.get("market_cap", 0),
  177                      "volume_24h":    coin.get("total_volume", 0),
  178                      "image":         coin.get("image"),
  179                  })
  180          # Sort by absolute move size
  181          movers.sort(key=lambda c: abs(c["pct_24h"]), reverse=True)
  182          return movers[:5]
  183  
  184      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  185          logger.warning("CoinGecko top movers fetch failed: %s", exc)
  186          return []
  187  
  188  
  189  def check_trending() -> list[dict]:
  190      """
  191      Main entry point — returns tweetable trending coin alerts.
  192  
  193      Guards (all must pass):
  194        1. Not already in our main watchlist
  195        2. Market cap rank ≤ 100
  196        3. Per-coin cooldown clear (6 h, by coin_id and symbol)
  197        4. Price AND volume data available from CoinGecko
  198      """
  199      alerts = []
  200  
  201      # 1. CoinGecko trending (search popularity)
  202      trending = fetch_trending()
  203      for coin in trending[:5]:
  204          coin_id = coin["id"]
  205          if coin_id in _TRACKED_IDS:
  206              continue
  207          mcap_rank = coin.get("market_cap_rank")
  208          if mcap_rank is None or mcap_rank > _MCAP_RANK_LIMIT:
  209              logger.debug("Trending skip %s — mcap rank %s > %d",
  210                           coin_id, mcap_rank, _MCAP_RANK_LIMIT)
  211              continue
  212          if not _cooldown_ok(coin_id) or not _symbol_cooldown_ok(coin["symbol"]):
  213              continue
  214          details = _fetch_coin_details(coin_id)
  215          if not details:
  216              logger.debug("Trending skip %s — no price/volume data", coin_id)
  217              continue
  218          alerts.append({
  219              "source":          "trending",
  220              "id":              coin_id,
  221              "symbol":          coin["symbol"],
  222              "name":            coin["name"],
  223              "trending_rank":   coin.get("trending_rank"),
  224              "market_cap_rank": mcap_rank,
  225              **details,
  226          })
  227  
  228      # 2. Big movers (price action)
  229      movers = fetch_top_movers()
  230      seen_ids = {a["id"] for a in alerts}
  231      for coin in movers:
  232          if coin["id"] in seen_ids:
  233              continue
  234          mcap_rank = coin.get("market_cap_rank")
  235          if mcap_rank is None or mcap_rank > _MCAP_RANK_LIMIT:
  236              logger.debug("Mover skip %s — mcap rank %s > %d",
  237                           coin["id"], mcap_rank, _MCAP_RANK_LIMIT)
  238              continue
  239          if not _cooldown_ok(coin["id"]) or not _symbol_cooldown_ok(coin["symbol"]):
  240              continue
  241          if not coin.get("current_price") or not coin.get("volume_24h"):
  242              logger.debug("Mover skip %s — missing price or volume", coin["id"])
  243              continue
  244          alerts.append({
  245              "source":          "mover",
  246              "id":              coin["id"],
  247              "symbol":          coin["symbol"],
  248              "name":            coin["name"],
  249              "current_price":   coin["current_price"],
  250              "pct_24h":         coin.get("pct_24h", 0),
  251              "market_cap_rank": mcap_rank,
  252              "market_cap":      coin.get("market_cap", 0),
  253              "volume_24h":      coin.get("volume_24h", 0),
  254              "image":           coin.get("image"),
  255          })
  256  
  257      return alerts[:3]  # max 3 candidates per check; daily cap enforced in bot.py
  258  
  259  
  260  def format_trending_tweet(alert: dict) -> str | None:
  261      """Format a trending coin alert into a tweet."""
  262      symbol = alert["symbol"]
  263      name   = alert["name"]
  264  
  265      # For "trending" alerts: show the CoinGecko trending position (1 = most searched).
  266      # For "mover" alerts: show market cap rank if available.
  267      if alert["source"] == "trending":
  268          rank        = alert.get("trending_rank")        # e.g. 1, 2, 3 …
  269          mcap_rank   = alert.get("market_cap_rank")
  270          rank_label  = f"#{rank} trending on CoinGecko" if rank is not None else "trending on CoinGecko"
  271          mcap_label  = f"market cap rank #{mcap_rank}" if mcap_rank is not None else None
  272      else:
  273          rank        = alert.get("market_cap_rank")
  274          rank_label  = f"rank #{rank}" if rank is not None else None
  275          mcap_label  = None
  276  
  277      if alert["source"] == "mover":
  278          price = alert.get("current_price", 0)
  279          pct   = alert.get("pct_24h", 0)
  280          emoji = "🟢" if pct > 0 else "🔴"
  281          sign  = "+" if pct > 0 else ""
  282          if price < 0.01:
  283              price_str = f"${price:.6f}"
  284          elif price < 1000:
  285              price_str = f"${price:,.2f}"
  286          else:
  287              price_str = f"${price:,.0f}"
  288  
  289          rank_context = f" ({rank_label})" if rank_label else ""
  290  
  291          tweet = None
  292          if ai_writer.is_available():
  293              direction = "pumping" if pct > 0 else "dumping"
  294              prompt = f"""Write a tweet about {name} ({symbol}) {direction} hard using this EXACT 4-part structure with a blank line between each part:
  295  
  296  [Punchy opener with key data — price, %, metric.]
  297  
  298  [One line context or analysis.]
  299  
  300  [Market call or directional observation — WHERE it goes next: "breaks $X and this runs to $Y" or "dead cat bounce, avoid."]
  301  
  302  [emoji from 🚀📉⚡👀]
  303  
  304  Data: {symbol}: {price_str} ({sign}{pct:.1f}% 24h){rank_context}
  305  No questions. No first person. NO hashtags. No disclaimers. Max 280 chars total.
  306  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  307              system = "You are @CoinWatchAlert. When you spot a move outside the usual names, you make a quick call — not a wishy-washy observation. Direction + level + conviction."
  308              ai_tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  309              if ai_tweet:
  310                  ai_tweet = ai_writer._strip_nfa(ai_tweet)
  311                  if len(ai_tweet) <= 280:
  312                      tweet = ai_tweet
  313  
  314          if not tweet:
  315              direction_word = "ripping" if pct > 0 else "dumping"
  316              next_move = "break higher and this runs" if pct > 0 else "no real support visible — more downside likely"
  317              tweet = (
  318                  f"{emoji} {symbol} {direction_word} {sign}{pct:.1f}% — now {price_str}"
  319                  f"{f' ({rank_label})' if rank_label else ''}.\n\n"
  320                  f"{next_move}.\n\n"
  321                  f"👀"
  322              )
  323  
  324      else:  # trending search
  325          rank_context = rank_label  # already formatted, e.g. "#1 trending on CoinGecko"
  326          mcap_context = f", {mcap_label}" if mcap_label else ""
  327  
  328          tweet = None
  329          if ai_writer.is_available():
  330              prompt = f"""Write a tweet about {name} ({symbol}) {rank_context}{mcap_context} using this EXACT 4-part structure with a blank line between each part:
  331  
  332  [Punchy opener with key data — trending rank, price if available, or search spike metric.]
  333  
  334  [One line context or analysis — is this legit momentum or hype?]
  335  
  336  [Market call — "This has legs because X" or "Hype with no substance — avoid."]
  337  
  338  [emoji from 🚀📉⚡👀]
  339  
  340  No questions. No first person. NO hashtags. No disclaimers. Max 280 chars total.
  341  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  342              system = "You are @CoinWatchAlert. When a coin starts trending, you tell people whether to pay attention or ignore it — with a reason. Never sit on the fence."
  343              ai_tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  344              if ai_tweet:
  345                  ai_tweet = ai_writer._strip_nfa(ai_tweet)
  346                  if len(ai_tweet) <= 280:
  347                      tweet = ai_tweet
  348  
  349          if not tweet:
  350              tweet = (
  351                  f"{symbol} {rank_context}{mcap_context} — search interest spiking.\n\n"
  352                  f"No price catalyst yet — pure speculation or early accumulation.\n\n"
  353                  f"Avoid chasing without a level. 👀"
  354              )
  355  
  356      _record(alert["id"], symbol)
  357      tweet = tweet[:280]
  358      tweet = re.sub(r'\s*⚠️\s*NFA\.?\s*', '', tweet).strip()
  359      tweet = re.sub(r'[^\w\s\$\%\.\,\!\?\-\:\;—\@🚀📉⚡👀\n]', '', tweet).strip()
  360      # Final nuclear NFA safety net — catch any remaining NFA in any form
  361      tweet = re.sub(r'[^\w\s]*NFA[^\w\s]*', '', tweet, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()
  362      tweet = re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', tweet).strip()
  363      return tweet

    1  """
    2  Tweet generators for scheduled content.

    5  recap tweets using live price data and technical indicators.

   24  # ── Daily caps ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   25  _quote_count = 0
   26  _quote_day = 0
   27  _reply_count = 0
   28  _reply_day = 0
   29  _narrative_count = 0
   30  _narrative_day = 0
   31  
   32  
   33  def _reset_daily_caps() -> None:
   34      """Reset daily counters if day has changed."""
   35      global _quote_count, _quote_day, _reply_count, _reply_day
   36      global _narrative_count, _narrative_day
   37      today = datetime.date.today().toordinal()
   38      if _quote_day != today:
   39          _quote_count = 0
   40          _quote_day = today
   41      if _reply_day != today:
   42          _reply_count = 0
   43          _reply_day = today
   44      if _narrative_day != today:
   45          _narrative_count = 0
   46          _narrative_day = today
   47  
   48  
   49  def can_quote_tweet() -> bool:
   50      _reset_daily_caps()
   51      return _quote_count < config.QUOTE_TWEET_DAILY_CAP
   52  
   53  
   54  def record_quote_tweet() -> None:
   55      global _quote_count
   56      _reset_daily_caps()
   57      _quote_count += 1
   58  
   59  
   60  def can_auto_reply() -> bool:
   61      _reset_daily_caps()
   62      return _reply_count < config.AUTO_REPLY_DAILY_CAP
   63  
   64  
   65  def record_auto_reply() -> None:
   66      global _reply_count
   67      _reset_daily_caps()
   68      _reply_count += 1
   69  
   70  
   71  def can_ct_narrative() -> bool:
   72      _reset_daily_caps()
   73      return _narrative_count < config.CT_NARRATIVE_DAILY_CAP
   74  
   75  
   76  def record_ct_narrative() -> None:
   77      global _narrative_count
   78      _reset_daily_caps()
   79      _narrative_count += 1

   81  
   82  # ── Price data helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   83  
   84  _BINANCE_TICKER_URL = "https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ticker/24hr"
   85  _BINANCE_KLINES_URL = "https://api.binance.com/api/v3/klines"
   86  
   87  # Maps CoinGecko coin_id → (Binance pair, uppercase symbol)
   88  _COIN_BINANCE_MAP: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
   89      "bitcoin":     ("BTCUSDT",  "BTC"),
   90      "ethereum":    ("ETHUSDT",  "ETH"),
   91      "binancecoin": ("BNBUSDT",  "BNB"),
   92      "solana":      ("SOLUSDT",  "SOL"),
   93      "ripple":      ("XRPUSDT",  "XRP"),
   94      "cardano":     ("ADAUSDT",  "ADA"),
   95      "dogecoin":    ("DOGEUSDT", "DOGE"),
   96      "avalanche-2": ("AVAXUSDT", "AVAX"),
   97      "polkadot":    ("DOTUSDT",  "DOT"),
   98      "chainlink":   ("LINKUSDT", "LINK"),
   99  }
  100  
  101  
  102  def _fetch_binance_coin(coin_id: str) -> dict | None:
  103      """Fetch price + 24h change for a single coin from Binance.
  104  
  105      Returns a normalised dict with the same keys the rest of the module
  106      expects (id, symbol, current_price, price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency,
  107      price_change_percentage_24h, market_cap).
  108      """
  109      mapping = _COIN_BINANCE_MAP.get(coin_id)
  110      if not mapping:
  111          logger.warning("No Binance pair for coin_id '%s'", coin_id)
  112          return None
  113      pair, symbol = mapping
  114      try:
  115          resp = requests.get(_BINANCE_TICKER_URL, params={"symbol": pair}, timeout=15)
  116          resp.raise_for_status()
  117          t = resp.json()
  118          price = float(t["lastPrice"])
  119          pct_24h = float(t["priceChangePercent"])
  120          return {
  121              "id": coin_id,
  122              "symbol": symbol.lower(),
  123              "current_price": price,
  124              "price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency": pct_24h,
  125              "price_change_percentage_24h": pct_24h,
  126              "market_cap": 0,
  127          }
  128      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  129          logger.warning("Binance ticker fetch failed for %s: %s", coin_id, exc)
  130          return None
  131  
  132  
  133  def _get_btc_data() -> dict | None:
  134      """Fetch BTC price, 24h change, and 7d change from Binance."""
  135      try:
  136          resp = requests.get(_BINANCE_TICKER_URL, params={"symbol": "BTCUSDT"}, timeout=15)
  137          resp.raise_for_status()
  138          t = resp.json()
  139          price = float(t["lastPrice"])
  140          pct_24h = float(t["priceChangePercent"])
  141      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  142          logger.warning("Binance fetch failed for BTC data: %s", exc)
  143          return None
  144  
  145      # Derive 7d change from daily klines (8 candles = open 7 days ago → now)
  146      pct_7d = 0.0
  147      try:
  148          kr = requests.get(
  149              _BINANCE_KLINES_URL,
  150              params={"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "interval": "1d", "limit": 8},
  151              timeout=15,
  152          )
  153          kr.raise_for_status()
  154          klines = kr.json()
  155          if len(klines) >= 8:
  156              open_7d_ago = float(klines[0][1])
  157              if open_7d_ago > 0:
  158                  pct_7d = (price - open_7d_ago) / open_7d_ago * 100
  159      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  160          logger.warning("Binance klines fetch failed for BTC 7d: %s", exc)
  161  
  162      return {
  163          "id": "bitcoin",
  164          "symbol": "btc",
  165          "current_price": price,
  166          "price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency": pct_24h,
  167          "price_change_percentage_24h": pct_24h,
  168          "price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency": pct_7d,
  169          "market_cap": 0,
  170      }
  171  
  172  
  173  def _get_top_coins_data() -> list[dict]:
  174      """Fetch 24h market data for top coins from Binance batch ticker."""
  175      pairs = [pair for pair, _ in _COIN_BINANCE_MAP.values()]
  176      symbols_param = json.dumps(pairs, separators=(',', ':'))
  177      try:
  178          resp = requests.get(
  179              _BINANCE_TICKER_URL,
  180              params={"symbols": symbols_param},
  181              timeout=15,
  182          )
  183          resp.raise_for_status()
  184          tickers = resp.json()
  185      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  186          logger.warning("Binance fetch failed for top coins: %s", exc)
  187          return []
  188  
  189      ticker_map = {t["symbol"]: t for t in tickers}
  190      result = []
  191      for coin_id, (pair, symbol) in _COIN_BINANCE_MAP.items():
  192          t = ticker_map.get(pair)
  193          if not t:
  194              continue
  195          price = float(t["lastPrice"])
  196          pct_24h = float(t["priceChangePercent"])
  197          result.append({
  198              "id": coin_id,
  199              "symbol": symbol.lower(),
  200              "current_price": price,
  201              "price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency": pct_24h,
  202              "price_change_percentage_24h": pct_24h,
  203              "market_cap": 0,
  204          })
  205      return result
  206  
  207  
  208  def _fmt_pct(val: float) -> str:
  209      """Format a percentage with sign."""
  210      return f"{'+' if val > 0 else ''}{val:.1f}%"
  211  
  212  
  213  def _fmt_price(val: float) -> str:
  214      """Format a USD price."""
  215      if val >= 1000:
  216          return f"${val:,.0f}"
  217      if val >= 1:
  218          return f"${val:.0f}"
  219      return f"${val:.2f}"
  220  
  221  
  222  def _pick_hashtags(symbols: list[str], extra: list[str] | None = None) -> str:
  223      """Deprecated — returns empty string. Hashtags hurt reach on X/Twitter."""
  224      return ""
