  226  
  227  # ── Quote tweet (market analysis) ────────────────────────────────────────────
  228  
  229  # Each generator function produces a different style of tweet.
  230  # The main generate_quote_tweet picks one at random.
  231  
  232  def _quote_price_action(btc: dict, coins: list[dict]) -> str:
  233      """Price-focused analysis tweet with BTC + top mover."""
  234      price = btc["current_price"]
  235      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  236      pct_7d = btc.get("price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency") or 0
  237  
  238      if pct_24h > 2:
  239          emoji, mood = "🟢", "pushing higher"
  240      elif pct_24h < -2:
  241          emoji, mood = "🔴", "under pressure"
  242      elif abs(pct_24h) <= 0.5:
  243          emoji, mood = "➡️", "moving sideways"
  244      elif pct_24h > 0:
  245          emoji, mood = "🟢", "ticking up"
  246      else:
  247          emoji, mood = "🔴", "drifting lower"
  248  
  249      lines = [
  250          f"📊 BTC at {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} 24h)",
  251          "",
  252      ]
  253  
  254      # Add context line about the 7d trend
  255      if abs(pct_7d) > 5:
  256          lines.append(f"📈 7-day: {_fmt_pct(pct_7d)} — {'strong momentum' if pct_7d > 0 else 'correction deepening'}")
  257      else:
  258          lines.append(f"📈 7-day: {_fmt_pct(pct_7d)} — {mood}")
  259  
  260      # Add a top mover if available
  261      if coins:
  262          non_btc = [c for c in coins if c["id"] != "bitcoin"]
  263          if non_btc:
  264              mover = max(non_btc, key=lambda c: abs(c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0))
  265              m_sym = config.COINS.get(mover["id"], mover["symbol"].upper())
  266              m_pct = mover.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  267              m_price = mover.get("current_price", 0)
  268              if abs(m_pct) > 1:
  269                  m_emoji = "🟢" if m_pct > 0 else "🔴"
  270                  lines.extend(["", f"{m_emoji} {m_sym} at {_fmt_price(m_price)} ({_fmt_pct(m_pct)})"])
  271  
  272      return "\n".join(lines)
  273  
  274  
  275  def _quote_on_chain(btc: dict, _coins: list[dict]) -> str:
  276      """On-chain metric focused tweet."""
  277      price = btc["current_price"]
  278      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  279  
  280      metrics = [
  281          (
  282              "Exchange reserves continue dropping",
  283              "coins leaving exchanges = reduced sell pressure. Holders aren't selling here.",
  284              ["Bitcoin", "BTC", "OnChain"],
  285          ),
  286          (
  287              "Long-term holder supply just hit a new high",
  288              "conviction remains strong despite the chop. Weak hands have been shaken out.",
  289              ["Bitcoin", "BTC", "OnChain"],
  290          ),
  291          (
  292              "Hash rate at all-time highs",
  293              "miners continue to invest in network security. Fundamentals remain solid.",
  294              ["Bitcoin", "BTC", "Mining"],
  295          ),
  296          (
  297              "Stablecoin supply on exchanges climbing",
  298              "dry powder building up. When buyers decide to deploy, supply is thin.",
  299              ["Bitcoin", "Crypto", "Stablecoins"],
  300          ),
  301          (
  302              "Active addresses showing steady growth",
  303              "network usage expanding quietly. Adoption doesn't wait for price.",
  304              ["Bitcoin", "BTC", "Adoption"],
  305          ),
  306          (
  307              "MVRV ratio in the neutral zone",
  308              "market not overheated, not capitulating. Classic accumulation territory.",
  309              ["Bitcoin", "BTC", "OnChain"],
  310          ),
  311      ]
  312  
  313      headline, body, tags = random.choice(metrics)
  314  
  315      lines = [
  316          f"BTC at {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} 24h)",
  317          "",
  318          f"{headline} —",
  319          "",
  320          body,
  321      ]
  322      return "\n".join(lines)
  323  
  324  
  325  def _quote_market_structure(btc: dict, coins: list[dict]) -> str:
  326      """Market structure / sentiment tweet."""
  327      price = btc["current_price"]
  328      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  329      mcap = btc.get("market_cap", 0)
  330  
  331      if pct_24h > 1:
  332          sentiment_takes = [
  333              "Funding rates normalizing after the flush — healthier setup for continuation.",
  334              "Spot buying leading this move. Leverage isn't driving it — that's bullish.",
  335              "Shorts getting squeezed while spot bids stack up. Classic accumulation.",
  336              "Market structure shifted bullish on the daily. Key support reclaimed.",
  337          ]
  338      elif pct_24h < -1:
  339          sentiment_takes = [
  340              "Leverage getting flushed — but that's how bottoms form. Watch for a reclaim.",
  341              "Cascading liquidations clearing out late longs. The reset was needed.",
  342              "Spot premium still positive despite the dip — real demand underneath.",
  343              "Fear rising but long-term holder behavior unchanged. They've seen this before.",
  344          ]
  345      else:
  346          sentiment_takes = [
  347              "Low volatility compression like this usually precedes a big move. Stay ready.",
  348              "Range-bound but volume building. Someone is quietly accumulating here.",
  349              "Boring markets make money for patient traders. Breakout loading.",
  350              "Tight range, declining volume — coiled spring. Direction TBD.",
  351          ]
  352  
  353      take = random.choice(sentiment_takes)
  354  
  355      mcap_str = ""
  356      if mcap > 0:
  357          mcap_str = f"Market cap: ${mcap / 1e12:.2f}T" if mcap >= 1e12 else f"Market cap: ${mcap / 1e9:.0f}B"
  358  
  359      lines = [
  360          f"BTC at {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} 24h)",
  361      ]
  362      if mcap_str:
  363          lines.append(mcap_str)
  364      lines.extend([
  365          "",
  366          take,
  367      ])
  368      return "\n".join(lines)
  369  
  370  
  371  def _quote_multi_coin(btc: dict, coins: list[dict]) -> str:
  372      """Multi-coin market snapshot."""
  373      price = btc["current_price"]
  374      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  375  
  376      lines = [
  377          "Market check:",
  378          "",
  379          f"→ BTC: {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct_24h)})",
  380      ]
  381  
  382      if coins:
  383          eth = next((c for c in coins if c["id"] == "ethereum"), None)
  384          if eth:
  385              e_price = eth.get("current_price", 0)
  386              e_pct = eth.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  387              lines.append(f"→ ETH: {_fmt_price(e_price)} ({_fmt_pct(e_pct)})")
  388  
  389          non_btc_eth = [c for c in coins if c["id"] not in ("bitcoin", "ethereum")]
  390          movers = sorted(
  391              non_btc_eth,
  392              key=lambda c: abs(c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0),
  393              reverse=True,
  394          )[:2]
  395  
  396          for coin in movers:
  397              sym = config.COINS.get(coin["id"], coin["symbol"].upper())
  398              c_pct = coin.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  399              c_price = coin.get("current_price", 0)
  400              emoji = "🟢" if c_pct > 0 else "🔴"
  401              lines.append(f"→ {emoji} {sym}: {_fmt_price(c_price)} ({_fmt_pct(c_pct)})")
  402  
  403      if coins:
  404          lines.append("")
  405          green = sum(1 for c in coins if (c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0) > 0)
  406          total = len(coins)
  407          lines.append(f"{green}/{total} coins green on the day")
  408  
  409      return "\n".join(lines)
  410  
  411  
  412  def _quote_alt_focus(_btc: dict, coins: list[dict]) -> str:
  413      """Alt-focused tweet that leads with altcoin movers, not BTC."""
  414      if not coins:
  415          return _quote_multi_coin(_btc, coins)  # fallback
  416  
  417      non_btc = [c for c in coins if c["id"] != "bitcoin"]
  418      if not non_btc:
  419          return _quote_multi_coin(_btc, coins)
  420  
  421      # Find the biggest mover
  422      top = max(non_btc, key=lambda c: abs(c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0))
  423      sym = config.COINS.get(top["id"], top["symbol"].upper())
  424      pct = top.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  425      price = top.get("current_price", 0)
  426      emoji = "🟢" if pct > 0 else "🔴"
  427  
  428      lines = [
  429          f"{emoji} {sym} {_fmt_pct(pct)} today — {'leading' if pct > 0 else 'lagging'} the market at {_fmt_price(price)}",
  430          "",
  431      ]
  432  
  433      # Add 1-2 more alts
  434      others = [c for c in non_btc if c["id"] != top["id"]]
  435      others_sorted = sorted(others, key=lambda c: abs(c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0), reverse=True)
  436      for coin in others_sorted[:2]:
  437          s = config.COINS.get(coin["id"], coin["symbol"].upper())
  438          p = coin.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  439          pr = coin.get("current_price", 0)
  440          e = "🟢" if p > 0 else "🔴"
  441          lines.append(f"→ {e} {s}: {_fmt_price(pr)} ({_fmt_pct(p)})")
  442  
  443      green = sum(1 for c in non_btc if (c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0) > 0)
  444      lines.extend(["", f"Alts: {green}/{len(non_btc)} green"])
  445  
  446      return "\n".join(lines)
  447  
  448  
  449  def _quote_narrative(_btc: dict, _coins: list[dict]) -> str:
  450      """Narrative / macro tweet that doesn't lead with a price."""
  451      price = _btc["current_price"]
  452      pct_7d = _btc.get("price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency") or 0
  453  
  454      narratives = [
  455          f"BTC at {_fmt_price(price)} with {_fmt_pct(pct_7d)} on the week.\n\nETF flows still the main driver — institutional demand hasn't slowed.",
  456          f"Market cap holding steady while volume drops.\n\nConsolidation at {_fmt_price(price)} BTC. The next macro catalyst decides direction.",
  457          f"Halving cycle comparison: we're tracking ahead of 2020 at this stage.\n\nBTC at {_fmt_price(price)}. History doesn't repeat but it rhymes.",
  458          f"DXY weakness + BTC at {_fmt_price(price)}.\n\nIf the dollar keeps fading, risk assets benefit. Watching the correlation closely.",
  459          f"Stablecoin market cap hitting new highs while BTC sits at {_fmt_price(price)}.\n\nDry powder waiting to deploy.",
  460      ]
  461  
  462      return random.choice(narratives)
  463  
  464  
  465  # Weighted generators: alt-focused and multi-coin appear twice to reduce BTC dominance
  466  _QUOTE_GENERATORS = [
  467      _quote_price_action,
  468      _quote_on_chain,
  469      _quote_market_structure,
  470      _quote_multi_coin,
  471      _quote_multi_coin,
  472      _quote_alt_focus,
  473      _quote_alt_focus,
  474      _quote_narrative,
  475  ]
  476  
  477  
  478  def generate_quote_tweet() -> str | None:
  479      """
  480      Generate a market tweet with enforced content variety.
  481      Tries AI (Claude) first with category rotation, falls back to templates.
  482      """
  483      btc = _get_btc_data()
  484      if not btc:
  485          return None
  486  
  487      price = btc.get("current_price", 0)
  488      if not price or price <= 0:
  489          logger.warning("BTC price is zero/missing — skipping quote tweet")
  490          return None
  491  
  492      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  493      pct_7d = btc.get("price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency") or 0
  494      mcap = btc.get("market_cap", 0)
  495      coins = _get_top_coins_data()
  496  
  497      # Try AI-generated tweet first (with category rotation)
  498      if ai_writer.is_available():
  499          result = ai_writer.generate_quote_tweet(price, pct_24h, pct_7d, mcap, coins)
  500          ai_tweet, category = result
  501          if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  502              logger.info("Using AI-generated quote tweet (category: %s)", category)
  503              state.record_content_category(category)
  504              return ai_tweet
  505          elif ai_tweet:
  506              logger.info("AI tweet too long (%d chars), falling back to template", len(ai_tweet))
  507  
  508      # Template fallback — pick a different style than last time
  509      last_style = state.get_last_quote_style()
  510      candidates = [g for g in _QUOTE_GENERATORS if g.__name__ != last_style]
  511      if not candidates:
  512          candidates = _QUOTE_GENERATORS
  513      generator = random.choice(candidates)
  514      state.record_quote_style(generator.__name__)
  515      state.record_content_category("template_" + generator.__name__)
  516  
  517      try:
  518          tweet = generator(btc, coins)
  519      except Exception as exc:
  520          logger.warning("Quote generator %s failed: %s", generator.__name__, exc)
  521          tweet = f"BTC at {_fmt_price(price)} ({_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} 24h)"
  522  
  523      if len(tweet) > 275:
  524          tweet = tweet[:272].rsplit("\n", 1)[0] + "…"
  525      return tweet
  526  
  527  
  528  # ── Morning recap ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  529  
  530  # Stores data from the most recent generate_morning_recap() call
  531  # so that bot.py can generate a chart without a second API fetch.
  532  _last_morning_top_gainer: dict | None = None
  533  _last_morning_coins: list[dict] = []
  534  
  535  
  536  def generate_morning_recap() -> str | None:
  537      """
  538      Generate a morning market recap tweet with top movers.
  539      Tries AI first, falls back to formatted template.
  540      """
  541      global _last_morning_top_gainer, _last_morning_coins
  542      _last_morning_top_gainer = None
  543      _last_morning_coins = []
  544  
  545      coins = _get_top_coins_data()
  546      if not coins:
  547          return None
  548  
  549      btc = next((c for c in coins if c["id"] == "bitcoin"), None)
  550      eth = next((c for c in coins if c["id"] == "ethereum"), None)
  551      if not btc:
  552          return None
  553  
  554      btc_price = btc.get("current_price", 0)
  555      if not btc_price or btc_price <= 0:
  556          logger.warning("BTC price is zero/missing — skipping morning recap")
  557          return None
  558  
  559      btc_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  560      btc_emoji = "🚀" if btc_24h >= 0 else "📉"
  561  
  562      eth_price = eth.get("current_price", 0) if eth else 0
  563      eth_24h = (eth.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0) if eth else 0
  564      eth_emoji = "🚀" if eth_24h >= 0 else "📉"
  565  
  566      # Top gainer by 24h % (excluding BTC/ETH, only if up more than BTC)
  567      alts = [c for c in coins if c["id"] not in ("bitcoin", "ethereum")]
  568      gainers = sorted(
  569          [c for c in alts if (c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0) > 0],
  570          key=lambda c: c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0,
  571          reverse=True,
  572      )
  573      top_gainer = gainers[0] if gainers else None
  574      _last_morning_top_gainer = top_gainer
  575      _last_morning_coins = coins[:5]
  576  
  577      # Green coin count
  578      green = sum(1 for c in coins if (c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0) > 0)
  579      total = len(coins)
  580      green_ratio = green / total if total else 0
  581  
  582      # Market read based on BTC direction and breadth
  583      if btc_24h >= 0:
  584          if green_ratio >= 0.7:
  585              market_read = "Bulls in control"
  586          elif green_ratio >= 0.5:
  587              market_read = "Momentum building"
  588          else:
  589              market_read = "Consolidating"
  590      else:
  591          if green_ratio >= 0.5:
  592              market_read = "Caution ahead"
  593          else:
  594              market_read = "Bears pressing"
  595  
  596      # Build line parts
  597      btc_line = f"BTC {_fmt_price(btc_price)} ({_fmt_pct(btc_24h)}) {btc_emoji}"
  598      eth_line = f"ETH {_fmt_price(eth_price)} ({_fmt_pct(eth_24h)}) {eth_emoji}" if eth else ""
  599  
  600      top_gainer_line = ""
  601      if top_gainer:
  602          sym = config.COINS.get(top_gainer["id"], top_gainer["symbol"].upper())
  603          tg_pct = top_gainer.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  604          top_gainer_line = f"{sym} top gainer +{tg_pct:.1f}% ⚡"
  605  
  606      green_line = f"{green}/{total} coins green"
  607      market_line = f"{market_read}."
  608  
  609      # Context string passed to Claude — all 5 coins + green count + market read
  610      coin_context_parts = []
  611      for c in (_last_morning_coins or [])[:5]:
  612          sym = config.COINS.get(c["id"], c["symbol"].upper())
  613          cp = c.get("current_price") or 0
  614          cpct = c.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  615          cemoji = "🚀" if cpct > 0 else "📉"
  616          coin_context_parts.append(f"{sym} {_fmt_price(cp)} ({_fmt_pct(cpct)}) {cemoji}")
  617      context = "  ".join(filter(None, coin_context_parts + [top_gainer_line, green_line, market_line]))
  618  
  619      # Try AI first
  620      if ai_writer.is_available():
  621          ai_tweet = ai_writer.generate_morning_recap_from_market(btc, coins, context)
  622          if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 220:
  623              logger.info("Using AI-generated morning recap")
  624              return ai_tweet
  625  
  626      # Fallback: multi-line template with top 5 coins
  627      lines = []
  628      for c in (_last_morning_coins or [])[:5]:
  629          p = _fmt_price(c.get("current_price", 0))
  630          pct = c.get("price_change_percentage_24h", 0) or 0
  631          emoji = "🚀" if pct >= 0 else "📉"
  632          sign = "+" if pct >= 0 else ""
  633          lines.append(f"{c['symbol'].upper()} {p} ({sign}{pct:.1f}%) {emoji}")
  634      coin_lines = "\n".join(lines)
  635      tweet = f"{coin_lines}\n\n{green_line}\n\n{market_read}."
  636      if len(tweet) > 220:
  637          tweet = tweet[:217] + "…"
  638      return tweet
  639  
  640  
  641  # ── Opinion tweet ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  642  
  643  _BULLISH_TAKES = [
  644      "Exchange outflows at multi-month highs. Someone's loading and they're not selling anytime soon.",
  645      "Long-term holders refuse to sell here. Last time conviction was this high, we rallied 40% in 8 weeks.",
  646      "Funding rates clean, leverage flushed, spot buying increasing. This is how bottoms form. I'm adding.",
  647      "Supply on exchanges at multi-year lows while ETF flows are positive. Do the math — this goes higher.",
  648      "Every dip is getting bought faster than the last one. Smart money isn't waiting for lower.",
  649      "Network hash rate at ATH, addresses growing, exchange supply shrinking. The chart catches up to fundamentals.",
  650      "This is the most hated rally I've seen. Which means it has legs. Disbelief is fuel.",
  651      "Spot premium over perps, funding neutral, and OI rising slowly. This is organic demand, not leverage. Bullish.",
  652  ]
  653  
  654  _BEARISH_TAKES = [
  655      "Distribution happening in plain sight. Whales are selling into your bids. I'm reducing risk here.",
  656      "Exchange inflows spiking — this is profit-taking. Lower prices before higher. I'd wait.",
  657      "Leverage at levels that historically precede a 10%+ flush. It's not if, it's when.",
  658      "DXY strengthening, yields rising, Fed hawkish. Every macro signal says risk-off. Don't fight the Fed.",
  659      "Price making higher highs while RSI makes lower highs. This divergence resolves down. Classic setup.",
  660      "Realized profit-taking at levels that preceded every major correction this cycle. I'm moving to stables.",
  661      "Euphoria metrics in the danger zone. When everyone's bullish, be the one taking profit.",
  662      "Short-term holders underwater and panic selling. This doesn't bottom until they capitulate completely.",
  663  ]
  664  
  665  _NEUTRAL_TAKES = [
  666      "Volatility compression this tight usually precedes a 10%+ move. I'm positioned for both directions.",
  667      "Range-bound for weeks now. The longer the compression, the more violent the breakout. Pick your side.",
  668      "Volume dying in this range. Something big is loading. When it breaks, it won't give you time to react.",
  669      "Bulls and bears both trapped. Next macro catalyst picks the winner. I'm not guessing — I'm waiting for confirmation.",
  670      "Everyone has an opinion here. But the chart says WAIT. Breakout or breakdown — both are in play.",
  671      "Sitting on hands until we get a clear break. No shame in patience when the edge isn't there.",
  672  ]
  673  
  674  
  675  def generate_opinion_tweet() -> str | None:
  676      """
  677      Generate an opinion/analysis tweet.
  678      Tries AI first, falls back to template.
  679      """
  680      btc = _get_btc_data()
  681      if not btc:
  682          return None
  683  
  684      price = btc.get("current_price", 0)
  685      if not price or price <= 0:
  686          logger.warning("BTC price is zero/missing — skipping opinion tweet")
  687          return None
  688  
  689      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  690      pct_7d = btc.get("price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency") or 0
  691  
  692      coins = _get_top_coins_data()
  693  
  694      # Try AI first — enrich with DeFi TVL data when available
  695      if ai_writer.is_available():
  696          # Fetch DeFi TVL context for richer analysis
  697          defi_context = None
  698          try:
  699              tvl_data = defi_monitor.fetch_total_tvl()
  700              if tvl_data:
  701                  defi_context = f"DeFi TVL: ${tvl_data.get('tvl', 0) / 1e9:.1f}B ({tvl_data.get('pct_24h', 0):+.1f}% 24h)"
  702          except Exception:
  703              pass
  704          ai_tweet = ai_writer.generate_opinion_tweet(price, pct_24h, pct_7d, coins,
  705                                                       defi_context=defi_context)
  706          if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  707              logger.info("Using AI-generated opinion tweet")
  708              return ai_tweet
  709  
  710      if pct_24h > 1.5:
  711          take = random.choice(_BULLISH_TAKES)
  712      elif pct_24h < -1.5:
  713          take = random.choice(_BEARISH_TAKES)
  714      else:
  715          take = random.choice(_NEUTRAL_TAKES)
  716  
  717      lines = [
  718          f"BTC at {_fmt_price(price)} — {_fmt_pct(pct_24h)} today, {_fmt_pct(pct_7d)} this week.",
  719          take,
  720      ]
  721  
  722      tweet = "\n".join(lines)
  723      if len(tweet) > 275:
  724          tweet = tweet[:272].rsplit("\n", 1)[0] + "…"
  725      return tweet
  726  
  727  
  728  # ── Engagement tweet (question / discussion) ────────────────────────────────
  729  
  730  _FALLBACK_QUESTIONS = [
  731      "BTC at {price} and the end-of-month target is higher — receipts on the line.",
  732      "The highest conviction alt for the next 90 days isn't what CT is talking about.",
  733      "{price} BTC and the next move is higher by Friday — not a guess, a read.",
  734      "Alts bleeding while BTC holds {price} — only one or two survive this rotation.",
  735      "BTC {pct_24h} today and the buyers stepping in here are the ones who get paid.",
  736      "80% of the top 100 coins won't exist in 5 years — the survivors are already obvious.",
  737      "The one coin CT is sleeping on right now is the one quietly posting the cleanest chart.",
  738      "BTC holding above {price} this cycle is the floor — that level doesn't break again.",
  739  ]
  740  
  741  
  742  def generate_engagement_tweet() -> str | None:
  743      """
  744      Generate a question/discussion tweet designed to get replies.
  745      Tries AI first, falls back to templates.
  746      """
  747      btc = _get_btc_data()
  748      if not btc:
  749          return None
  750  
  751      price = btc.get("current_price", 0)
  752      if not price or price <= 0:
  753          return None
  754  
  755      pct_24h = btc.get("price_change_percentage_24h_in_currency") or 0
  756      pct_7d = btc.get("price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency") or 0
  757      coins = _get_top_coins_data()
  758  
  759      # Try AI first
  760      if ai_writer.is_available():
  761          ai_tweet = ai_writer.generate_engagement_tweet(price, pct_24h, pct_7d, coins)
  762          if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  763              logger.info("Using AI-generated engagement tweet")
  764              return ai_tweet
  765  
  766      # Template fallback
  767      template = random.choice(_FALLBACK_QUESTIONS)
  768      return template.format(
  769          price=_fmt_price(price),
  770          pct_24h=_fmt_pct(pct_24h),
  771          pct_7d=_fmt_pct(pct_7d),
  772      )


================================================================================
  FILE: twitter_client.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Twitter/X client wrapper around Tweepy v4 (API v2).
    3  
    4  Twitter API v2 free tier ("Essential" access) allows:
    5    - 1,500 tweets/month (write)
    6    - OAuth 1.0a User Context required for posting
    7  
    8  To get credentials:
    9    1. Go to https://developer.twitter.com/en/portal/dashboard
   10    2. Create a new project + app
   11    3. Enable OAuth 1.0a with Read & Write permissions
   12    4. Generate Access Token & Secret
   13  """
   14  from __future__ import annotations
   15  
   16  import datetime
   17  import logging
   18  import os
   19  import re
   20  
   21  import tweepy
   22  
   23  import config
   24  import state
   25  
   26  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   27  
   28  _client: tweepy.Client | None = None
   29  _api_v1: tweepy.API | None = None
   30  
   31  
   32  # ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   33  
   34  def _ensure_line_breaks(text: str) -> str:
   35      """Add a newline after sentence-ending periods before a capital letter."""
   36      return re.sub(r'\.(?= [A-Z])', '.\n', text)
   37  
   38  
   39  # ── Client factories ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   40  
   41  def get_client() -> tweepy.Client:
   42      """Return a cached Tweepy v2 client (creates it on first call).
   43      Raises RuntimeError if credentials are missing."""
   44      global _client
   45      if _client is None:
   46          missing = [
   47              name
   48              for name, val in [
   49                  ("TWITTER_API_KEY",             config.TWITTER_API_KEY),
   50                  ("TWITTER_API_SECRET",          config.TWITTER_API_SECRET),
   51                  ("TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN",        config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN),
   52                  ("TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN_SECRET", config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN_SECRET),
   53              ]
   54              if not val
   55          ]
   56          if missing:
   57              raise RuntimeError(
   58                  f"Missing Twitter credentials in .env: {', '.join(missing)}"
   59              )
   60          _client = tweepy.Client(
   61              bearer_token=config.TWITTER_BEARER_TOKEN or None,
   62              consumer_key=config.TWITTER_API_KEY,
   63              consumer_secret=config.TWITTER_API_SECRET,
   64              access_token=config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN,
   65              access_token_secret=config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN_SECRET,
   66              wait_on_rate_limit=True,
   67          )
   68      return _client
   69  
   70  
   71  def _get_api_v1() -> tweepy.API | None:
   72      """Return a cached Tweepy v1.1 API client used for media uploads.
   73      Returns None on any initialisation failure."""
   74      global _api_v1
   75      if _api_v1 is None:
   76          try:
   77              auth = tweepy.OAuth1UserHandler(
   78                  config.TWITTER_API_KEY,
   79                  config.TWITTER_API_SECRET,
   80                  config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN,
   81                  config.TWITTER_ACCESS_TOKEN_SECRET,
   82              )
   83              _api_v1 = tweepy.API(auth)
   84          except Exception as exc:
   85              logger.warning("Could not initialise v1.1 API: %s", exc)
   86              return None
   87      return _api_v1
   88  
   89  
   90  # Public alias so callers using either name work.
   91  get_api_v1 = _get_api_v1
   92  
   93  
   94  # ── Media ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   95  
   96  def upload_media(image_path: str) -> str | None:
   97      """Upload an image via Twitter API v1.1. Returns media_id string, or None on failure."""
   98      api = _get_api_v1()
   99      if api is None:
  100          logger.warning("v1.1 API unavailable — skipping media upload")
  101          return None
  102      try:
  103          media = api.media_upload(filename=image_path)
  104          logger.info("Media uploaded (id=%s)", media.media_id_string)
  105          return media.media_id_string
  106      except tweepy.TweepyException as exc:
  107          logger.warning("Media upload failed: %s", exc)
  108          return None
  109  
  110  
  111  # ── Posting ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  112  
  113  def post_tweet(
  114      text: str,
  115      image_path: str | None = None,
  116      in_reply_to_tweet_id: str | None = None,
  117  ) -> bool:
  118      """Post a single tweet with an optional image attachment and/or reply target.
  119  
  120      - Strips hashtags (they hurt reach on X/Twitter)
  121      - Cleans up newlines
  122      - Truncates at 275 chars at a word boundary
  123      - Checks monthly cap before posting
  124      - Records the tweet in state on success
  125  
  126      Returns True on success, False on failure.
  127      """
  128      logger.info("[POST] ENTRY: text=%r, reply_to=%s, image=%s",
  129                  text[:50], in_reply_to_tweet_id, bool(image_path))
  130  
  131      stripped = text.strip()
  132      if not stripped or len(stripped) < 20:
  133          logger.warning("[POST] BLOCKED — too short or empty (%d chars): %.60s",
  134                         len(stripped) if stripped else 0, text)
  135          return False
  136      if "$0 " in text or "$0," in text or "at $0." in text:
  137          logger.warning("[POST] BLOCKED — contains $0 price (API likely down): %.60s", text)
  138          return False
  139  
  140      if not state.can_tweet():
  141          logger.warning("[POST] BLOCKED by state.can_tweet() — monthly cap reached")
  142          return False
  143  
  144      # Strip hashtags
  145      text = re.sub(r'\s*#\w+', '', text).strip()
  146      # Remove trailing blank lines left by stripped hashtags
  147      text = re.sub(r'\n\s*\n\s*$', '', text).strip()
  148      # Improve readability with sentence-level line breaks
  149      text = _ensure_line_breaks(text)
  150  
  151      if len(text) > 275:
  152          text = text[:272].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
  153  
  154      media_ids = None
  155      if image_path:
  156          media_id = upload_media(image_path)
  157          if media_id:
  158              media_ids = [media_id]
  159  
  160      try:
  161          client = get_client()
  162          kwargs: dict = {"text": text}
  163          if media_ids:
  164              kwargs["media_ids"] = media_ids
  165          if in_reply_to_tweet_id:
  166              kwargs["in_reply_to_tweet_id"] = in_reply_to_tweet_id
  167          logger.info("[POST] create_tweet kwargs: %s",
  168                      {k: (v[:50] if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in kwargs.items()})
  169          response = client.create_tweet(**kwargs)
  170          tweet_id = response.data["id"]
  171          state.record_tweet()
  172          logger.info("[POST] Success (id=%s, reply_to=%s, media=%s): %.80s",
  173                      tweet_id, in_reply_to_tweet_id, bool(media_ids), text)
  174          return True
  175      except tweepy.errors.Forbidden as exc:
  176          logger.error("[POST] Twitter 403 Forbidden (reply_to=%s): %s",
  177                       in_reply_to_tweet_id, exc)
  178      except tweepy.errors.TooManyRequests:
  179          logger.warning("[POST] Twitter rate limit hit (reply_to=%s)", in_reply_to_tweet_id)
  180      except tweepy.TweepyException as exc:
  181          logger.error("[POST] Twitter error (reply_to=%s): %s", in_reply_to_tweet_id, exc)
  182      return False
  183  
  184  
  185  def post_thread(tweets: list[str], first_tweet_image_path: str | None = None) -> bool:
  186      """Post a list of tweets as a thread (each replying to the previous).
  187  
  188      first_tweet_image_path: optional image attached to the first tweet only.
  189      Returns True if all tweets posted successfully, False on first failure.
  190      """
  191      if not tweets:
  192          return False
  193  
  194      client = get_client()
  195      previous_id: str | None = None
  196  
  197      for i, text in enumerate(tweets):
  198          if len(text) > 280:
  199              text = text[:277].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
  200          try:
  201              kwargs: dict = {"text": text}
  202              if previous_id:
  203                  kwargs["in_reply_to_tweet_id"] = previous_id
  204              if i == 0 and first_tweet_image_path:
  205                  media_id = upload_media(first_tweet_image_path)
  206                  if media_id:
  207                      kwargs["media_ids"] = [media_id]
  208              response = client.create_tweet(**kwargs)
  209              previous_id = response.data["id"]
  210              logger.info(
  211                  "Thread tweet %d/%d posted (id=%s): %.60s",
  212                  i + 1, len(tweets), previous_id, text,
  213              )
  214          except tweepy.errors.Forbidden as exc:
  215              logger.error("Twitter 403 Forbidden on thread tweet %d: %s", i + 1, exc)
  216              return False
  217          except tweepy.errors.TooManyRequests:
  218              logger.warning("Twitter rate limit hit on thread tweet %d", i + 1)
  219              return False
  220          except tweepy.TweepyException as exc:
  221              logger.error("Twitter error on thread tweet %d: %s", i + 1, exc)
  222              return False
  223  
  224      return True
  225  
  226  
  227  # ── Search ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  228  
  229  def search_recent_tweets(query: str, max_results: int = 10) -> list[dict]:
  230      """Search recent tweets using Twitter API v2.
  231  
  232      Returns a list of dicts with keys:
  233          id, text, author_id, like_count, retweet_count, followers_count, created_at
  234  
  235      Returns an empty list on any failure.
  236      """
  237      try:
  238          client = get_client()
  239          response = client.search_recent_tweets(
  240              query=query,
  241              max_results=max(10, min(max_results, 100)),
  242              tweet_fields=["public_metrics", "created_at", "author_id"],
  243              expansions=["author_id"],
  244              user_fields=["public_metrics"],
  245          )
  246      except tweepy.TweepyException as exc:
  247          logger.warning("Twitter search failed: %s", exc)
  248          return []
  249  
  250      if not response.data:
  251          return []
  252  
  253      followers_by_id: dict[str, int] = {}
  254      if response.includes and response.includes.get("users"):
  255          for user in response.includes["users"]:
  256              followers_by_id[str(user.id)] = user.public_metrics["followers_count"]
  257  
  258      results = []
  259      for tweet in response.data:
  260          author_id = str(tweet.author_id)
  261          pm = tweet.public_metrics or {}
  262          results.append({
  263              "id":              str(tweet.id),
  264              "text":            tweet.text,
  265              "author_id":       author_id,
  266              "like_count":      pm.get("like_count", 0),
  267              "retweet_count":   pm.get("retweet_count", 0),
  268              "followers_count": followers_by_id.get(author_id, 0),
  269              "created_at":      tweet.created_at,
  270          })
  271  
  272      return results


================================================================================
  FILE: volume_anomaly_scanner.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Volume anomaly scanner — detects unusual volume without matching price moves.
    3  
    4  Uses Binance 1d klines to find coins where current volume is 2x+ the 7-day
    5  average but price has barely moved (< 3%). This signals accumulation,
    6  distribution, or early positioning before a larger move.
    7  """
    8  from __future__ import annotations
    9  
   10  import logging
   11  import requests
   12  
   13  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   14  
   15  _BINANCE_KLINES_URL = "https://api.binance.com/api/v3/klines"
   16  
   17  # Coins to monitor — Binance USDT pairs
   18  _TRACKED_COINS = [
   19      ("BTC", "BTCUSDT"),
   20      ("ETH", "ETHUSDT"),
   21      ("SOL", "SOLUSDT"),
   22      ("XRP", "XRPUSDT"),
   23      ("BNB", "BNBUSDT"),
   24      ("ADA", "ADAUSDT"),
   25      ("AVAX", "AVAXUSDT"),
   26      ("DOGE", "DOGEUSDT"),
   27      ("LINK", "LINKUSDT"),
   28      ("DOT", "DOTUSDT"),
   29  ]
   30  
   31  
   32  def scan() -> dict | None:
   33      """Scan tracked coins for volume anomalies.
   34  
   35      Returns the top anomaly as a dict with keys:
   36          symbol, price, vol_ratio, price_change
   37      or None if no coins meet the threshold.
   38      """
   39      anomalies: list[dict] = []
   40  
   41      for symbol, pair in _TRACKED_COINS:
   42          try:
   43              resp = requests.get(
   44                  _BINANCE_KLINES_URL,
   45                  params={"symbol": pair, "interval": "1d", "limit": 8},
   46                  timeout=15,
   47              )
   48              resp.raise_for_status()
   49              klines = resp.json()
   50  
   51              if not isinstance(klines, list) or len(klines) < 8:
   52                  logger.debug("[ANOMALY] %s: insufficient klines (%d)",
   53                               symbol, len(klines) if isinstance(klines, list) else 0)
   54                  continue
   55  
   56              # kline format: [open_time, open, high, low, close, volume, ...]
   57              current = klines[-1]
   58              previous_7 = klines[:-1]  # 7 prior candles
   59  
   60              current_volume = float(current[5])
   61              current_close = float(current[4])
   62              previous_close = float(klines[-2][4])
   63  
   64              avg_volume = sum(float(k[5]) for k in previous_7) / len(previous_7)
   65  
   66              if avg_volume == 0:
   67                  logger.debug("[ANOMALY] %s: avg_volume is 0 — skipping", symbol)
   68                  continue
   69  
   70              vol_ratio = current_volume / avg_volume
   71              price_change = ((current_close - previous_close) / previous_close) * 100
   72  
   73              logger.debug("[ANOMALY] %s: vol_ratio=%.2f, price_change=%.2f%%",
   74                           symbol, vol_ratio, price_change)
   75  
   76              # Detection: 2x+ volume with < 3% price move
   77              if vol_ratio >= 2.0 and abs(price_change) <= 3.0:
   78                  anomalies.append({
   79                      "symbol": symbol,
   80                      "price": current_close,
   81                      "vol_ratio": round(vol_ratio, 2),
   82                      "price_change": round(price_change, 2),
   83                  })
   84                  logger.info("[ANOMALY] %s: %.2fx volume, %.2f%% move — DETECTED",
   85                              symbol, vol_ratio, price_change)
   86  
   87          except requests.RequestException as exc:
   88              logger.warning("[ANOMALY] Failed to fetch klines for %s: %s", symbol, exc)
   89          except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError) as exc:
   90              logger.warning("[ANOMALY] Data parsing error for %s: %s", symbol, exc)
   91  
   92      if not anomalies:
   93          logger.debug("[ANOMALY] No anomalies detected this cycle")
   94          return None
   95  
   96      # Sort by vol_ratio descending, return top 1
   97      anomalies.sort(key=lambda a: a["vol_ratio"], reverse=True)
   98      top = anomalies[0]
   99      logger.info("[ANOMALY] Top anomaly: %s — %.2fx volume, %.2f%% move, price $%.4f",
  100                  top["symbol"], top["vol_ratio"], top["price_change"], top["price"])
  101      return top


================================================================================
  FILE: whale_monitor.py
================================================================================

    1  """
    2  Whale monitor – tracks large crypto transactions using free public APIs.
    3  
    4  Uses Blockchain.com and Etherscan-like APIs to detect large transfers
    5  that could signal institutional moves.
    6  
    7  Also uses Whale Alert-style data from free endpoints.
    8  """
    9  from __future__ import annotations
   10  
   11  import logging
   12  import time
   13  
   14  import requests
   15  
   16  import ai_writer
   17  
   18  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   19  
   20  # Cooldown to avoid tweeting about same whale activity
   21  _recent_alerts: dict[str, float] = {}
   22  _WHALE_COOLDOWN = 7200  # 2 hours
   23  
   24  
   25  def _cooldown_ok(alert_key: str) -> bool:
   26      last = _recent_alerts.get(alert_key, 0)
   27      return (time.time() - last) >= _WHALE_COOLDOWN
   28  
   29  
   30  def _record_alert(alert_key: str) -> None:
   31      _recent_alerts[alert_key] = time.time()
   32      # Prune
   33      cutoff = time.time() - _WHALE_COOLDOWN * 2
   34      expired = [k for k, v in _recent_alerts.items() if v < cutoff]
   35      for k in expired:
   36          del _recent_alerts[k]
   37  
   38  
   39  def fetch_btc_whale_txns() -> list[dict]:
   40      """
   41      Fetch large BTC transactions from Blockchain.com API (free, no key).
   42      Looks at recent unconfirmed transactions over $10M.
   43      """
   44      try:
   45          # Use Blockchair API (free tier, no key for basic queries)
   46          resp = requests.get(
   47              "https://api.blockchair.com/bitcoin/transactions",
   48              params={
   49                  "s": "output_total(desc)",
   50                  "limit": 10,
   51              },
   52              timeout=15,
   53          )
   54          if resp.status_code != 200:
   55              return []
   56          data = resp.json()
   57          txns = data.get("data", [])
   58  
   59          whales = []
   60          for tx in txns:
   61              value_usd = tx.get("output_total_usd", 0) or 0
   62              if value_usd >= 10_000_000:  # $10M+
   63                  value_btc = tx.get("output_total", 0) / 1e8  # satoshis to BTC
   64                  alert_key = f"btc_whale_{tx.get('hash', '')[:16]}"
   65                  if not _cooldown_ok(alert_key):
   66                      continue
   67                  whales.append({
   68                      "chain": "Bitcoin",
   69                      "symbol": "BTC",
   70                      "value_usd": value_usd,
   71                      "value_native": value_btc,
   72                      "tx_hash": tx.get("hash", ""),
   73                      "alert_key": alert_key,
   74                  })
   75          return whales[:3]
   76  
   77      except requests.RequestException as exc:
   78          logger.debug("BTC whale fetch failed: %s", exc)
   79          return []
   80  
   81  
   82  def fetch_eth_whale_txns() -> list[dict]:
   83      """
   84      Fetch large ETH transfers from Etherscan-compatible free API.
   85      """
   86      try:
   87          # Use Blockchair for ETH too
   88          resp = requests.get(
   89              "https://api.blockchair.com/ethereum/transactions",
   90              params={
   91                  "s": "value(desc)",
   92                  "limit": 10,
   93              },
   94              timeout=15,
   95          )
   96          if resp.status_code != 200:
   97              return []
   98          data = resp.json()
   99          txns = data.get("data", [])
  100  
  101          whales = []
  102          for tx in txns:
  103              value_usd = tx.get("value_usd", 0) or 0
  104              if value_usd >= 5_000_000:  # $5M+
  105                  value_eth = tx.get("value", 0) / 1e18
  106                  alert_key = f"eth_whale_{tx.get('hash', '')[:16]}"
  107                  if not _cooldown_ok(alert_key):
  108                      continue
  109                  whales.append({
  110                      "chain": "Ethereum",
  111                      "symbol": "ETH",
  112                      "value_usd": value_usd,
  113                      "value_native": value_eth,
  114                      "tx_hash": tx.get("hash", ""),
  115                      "alert_key": alert_key,
  116                  })
  117          return whales[:3]
  118  
  119      except requests.RequestException as exc:
  120          logger.debug("ETH whale fetch failed: %s", exc)
  121          return []
  122  
  123  
  124  def fetch_exchange_flows() -> dict | None:
  125      """
  126      Fetch exchange netflow data from CryptoQuant-style free endpoints.
  127      Uses CoinGlass as a proxy for exchange flow data.
  128      """
  129      try:
  130          resp = requests.get(
  131              "https://open-api.coinglass.com/public/v2/index/bitcoin-profitable-days",
  132              timeout=15,
  133          )
  134          # This endpoint may not have exactly what we want,
  135          # but we try to get exchange flow signals
  136          if resp.status_code == 200:
  137              data = resp.json()
  138              return data.get("data")
  139      except requests.RequestException:
  140          pass
  141      return None
  142  
  143  
  144  def check_whale_activity() -> list[dict]:
  145      """
  146      Main entry point — check for whale transactions.
  147      Returns list of tweetable whale alerts.
  148      """
  149      alerts = []
  150  
  151      btc_whales = fetch_btc_whale_txns()
  152      alerts.extend(btc_whales)
  153  
  154      eth_whales = fetch_eth_whale_txns()
  155      alerts.extend(eth_whales)
  156  
  157      # Sort by value
  158      alerts.sort(key=lambda a: a.get("value_usd", 0), reverse=True)
  159      return alerts[:2]  # max 2 per check
  160  
  161  
  162  def format_whale_tweet(alert: dict) -> str | None:
  163      """Format a whale alert into a tweet."""
  164      _record_alert(alert["alert_key"])
  165  
  166      symbol = alert["symbol"]
  167      value_usd = alert["value_usd"]
  168      value_native = alert.get("value_native", 0)
  169      chain = alert["chain"]
  170  
  171      usd_str = f"${value_usd / 1e6:.0f}M" if value_usd >= 1e6 else f"${value_usd:,.0f}"
  172      native_str = f"{value_native:,.0f}" if value_native >= 1 else f"{value_native:,.2f}"
  173  
  174      if ai_writer.is_available():
  175          prompt = f"""Write a tweet about a large whale transaction detected.
  176  
  177  Chain: {chain}
  178  Amount: {native_str} {symbol} (~{usd_str})
  179  
  180  Speculate on what this could mean — exchange deposit (sell pressure),
  181  exchange withdrawal (accumulation), or OTC deal.
  182  Keep it under 275 chars. NO hashtags. Sound like a trader who watches on-chain.
  183  
  184  Write the tweet now. Nothing else."""
  185          system = "You are @CoinWatchAlert. You spot whale moves before CT does. Data-driven, no hype."
  186          ai_tweet = ai_writer._call_claude(system, prompt)
  187          if ai_tweet and len(ai_tweet) <= 275:
  188              return ai_tweet
  189  
  190      # Template fallback
  191      return (
  192          f"🐋 {native_str} {symbol} ({usd_str}) just moved on {chain}.\n\n"
  193          f"Exchange deposit = potential sell pressure. Withdrawal = accumulation. Direction matters."
  194      )


================================================================================
  FILE: start_bot.sh
================================================================================

    1  #!/bin/bash
    2  # Start (or restart) the CoinWatchAlert bot via launchd
    3  PLIST="$HOME/Library/LaunchAgents/com.coinwatchalert.bot.plist"
    4  SRC="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)/com.coinwatchalert.bot.plist"
    5  
    6  mkdir -p "$HOME/Library/LaunchAgents"
    7  
    8  # Inject API key into reply_tool.html
    9  "$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)/inject_key.sh"
   10  
   11  # Clean bootout before restarting — no zombie processes
   12  launchctl bootout gui/$(id -u) "$PLIST" 2>/dev/null
   13  sleep 2
   14  
   15  cp "$SRC" "$PLIST"
   16  launchctl bootstrap gui/$(id -u) "$PLIST"
   17  echo "CoinWatchAlert bot started. Logs: ~/crypto-bot-/bot.log"


================================================================================
  FILE: stop_bot.sh
================================================================================

    1  #!/bin/bash
    2  # Stop the CoinWatchAlert bot — full cleanup
    3  PLIST="$HOME/Library/LaunchAgents/com.coinwatchalert.bot.plist"
    4  
    5  launchctl bootout gui/$(id -u) "$PLIST" 2>/dev/null
    6  pkill -9 -f bot.py 2>/dev/null
    7  rm -f ~/crypto-bot-/bot.pid
    8  echo "CoinWatchAlert bot stopped."


================================================================================
  FILE: inject_key.sh
================================================================================

    1  #!/bin/bash
    2  FILE=~/crypto-bot-/reply_tool.html
    3  KEY=$(grep ANTHROPIC_API_KEY ~/crypto-bot-/.env | cut -d'=' -f2 | tr -d '[:space:]')
    4  git checkout -- "$FILE"
    5  sed -i '' "s|REPLY_TOOL_API_KEY_PLACEHOLDER|$KEY|" "$FILE"
    6  echo "Key injected cleanly."
